(d) zvichienderana nekusiya basa kunenge kwaita munhu kuburikidza nekuchembera, kurwara kana chimwewo chikonzero; uye zvinosanganisira kushandurwa kwenzira inoshandiswa pakubhadharwa kwemudyandigere, mari inobhadharwa munhu pagore, mari inopihwa munhu pakusiya basa kana imwe mari inobhadharwa kumunhu, uye mari inodzorerwa kumunhu pamari dzese idzi; “pfuma” zvinoreva pfuma chero ipi zvayo uye kodzero kana chido chekuva nepfuma.

(2) Zvichienderana nezviri muchikamu 72, munhu wese ane kodzero, munzvimbo chero ipi zvayo yeZimbabwe, yekutenga kana kuwana, kuchengeta, kupinda, kushandisa, kushandura uridzi, kushandisa sechibatiso pakuwana chikwereti, kurojesa kana kutengesa mhando dzese dzepfuma, ari ega kana kuti akabatana nevamwe.

(3) Zvichienderana nezviri muchikamu chino uye zviri muchikamu 72, hapana munhu anokwanisa kutorerwa pfuma yake nechisimba kunze kwekunge zvinotevera izvi zvazadzikiswa—

(a)

kutorerwa uku kuchiitwa pasi pemutemo unoshandiswa pane zvakasiyanasiyana;

(b) kutorerwa uku kwakakodzera nekuda kwechikonzero chero chipi zvacho pane zvinotevera—

(i) nechinangwa chekudzivirira vanhu, kuchengeteka kweruzhinji rwevanhu munyika, runyararo munyika, unhu hwakanaka munyika, hutano hweruzhinji rwevanhu kana urongwa hwemashandisirwo enzvimbo mudhorobha kana munyika; kana kuti

(ii) nechinangwa chekuvandudza kana kushandisa nzvimbo iyi kana imwewo, nenzira inobatsira vanhu vari munharaunda;

(c)

mutemo unomanikidza vanoona nezvekutorwa kwepfuma kuti—

(i) vape chiziviso, nenguva yakakodzera, chekuda kutora pfuma iyi kumunhu wese ane chido kana kodzero yekuva nepfuma inozokanganiswa nekutorwa kwepfuma uku;

(ii) vabhadhare muripo wakaenzanirana uye wakakwana pfuma isati yatorwa kana kuti munguva yakakodzera zvichitevera kutorwa kwepfuma; uye

(iii) kana kutorwa kwepfuma kuchipikiswa, vanyorere kudare redzimhosva rine masimba akakodzera, pfuma isati yatorwa, kana kuti mukati memazuva asingapfuuri makumi matatu zvichitevera kutorwa kwepfuma, vachikumbira tsamba inoratidza kuti pfuma yakatorwa;

(d) mutemo unopa kodzero kumunhu chero upi zvake ane pfuma inenge yatorwa yekuti anyorere kudare rine masimba akakodzera achikumbira kuti pfuma yake idzorerwe nekukasika, ndokunge dare redzimhosva risina kupa mvumo yekutorwa kwepfuma iyi; uye

(e)

mutemo unopa kodzero kumunhu chero upi zvake anenge achida kuripwa kuti anyorere kudare rine masimba akakodzera achikumbira kuti riongorore—

(i) kuvapo, mamiriro nekukosha kwechido chake maererano nepfuma inenge ichitaurwa nezvayo;

(ii) kuva pamutemo kwekutorerwa pfuma uku; uye

(iii) uwandu hwemuripo waakakodzera kupihwa;

uye kunyorera kudare achikumbira kuti dare ripe gwaro rinomanikidza kubhadharwa kwemuripo chero upi zvawo.

(3) Kana munhu achinge ane kodzero yekubhadharwa mudyandigere iye zvino kana kuti achigona kuzoda kubhadharwa, mutemo unoita kuti kodzero iyi isavapo kana kuti ideredzwe kuitira kuzadzisa zviri muchikamu chidiki (3) unogashirwa, uye uchaonekwa semutemo unobvumidza kutorwa kwepfuma nechisimba. 72

Kodzero yekuva nemunda wekurima

(1) Muchikamu chino— “munda wekurima” zvinoreva munda unoshandiswa kana kuti wakakodzera kurimwa, kureva kurima maruva, kurima magirepi, kurima miti kana kurima hove nezvimwe zvemumvura kana munda unoshandiswa kupfuya zvipfuyo, kusanganisira—

(a) kuchengeta kana kupfuya mombe, mhuka dzemusango, huku, dzimwewo mhuka kana nyuchi; kana kuti

(b) mafuro emhuka dzepamba kana dzemusango;

asi hazvisanganisiri minda yekumaruwa kana iri pasi pekanzuru yedhorobha kana kuti pasi penzvimbo yemabhizimisi yakavambwa pasi pemutemo une chekuita neurongwa hwekushandiswa kweminda yemadhorobha kana nyika kana kuti sezvinotsanangudzwa mumutemo une chekuita nekuiswa miganhu paminda.

“munda” unosanganisira chero chinhu chakabatira zvachose pavhu kana kuti chiri kumera nekukukura pamunda; “chidimbu chemunda wekurima” zvinoreva chidimbu chemunda wekurima chakanyoreswa sechidimbu chemunda chakazvimirira kubazi rinonyoreswa uridzi hweminda.

(2) Kana munda wekurima uchidikanwa kuti ushandiswe nehurumende, kusanganisira—

(a)

kugariswa patsva kwevanhu kuti varime kana kuita zvimwewo;

(b) kurongwa patsva kweminda, kurima miti, kuchengetedza nharaunda kana kushandiswa kuchengetera mhuka nezvipukanana zvesango kana zvimwe zviwanikwa; kana kuti

(c)

kugariswa patsva kwevanhu vanenge vatorerwa minda yavo kuburikidza nekushandiswa kweminda nechikonzero chadomwa mundima diki (a) kana (b);

munda uyu unogona kutorwa nehurumende kuburikidza nekuisa chiziviso mugwaro reGazette rinoburitsa mitemo nenhau dzehurumende, izvi zvichireva kuti uridzi hwakazara hwemunda uyu hunenge hwava hwehurumende kubva musi unenge waburitswa chiziviso ichi.

(3) Kana munda wekurima (kana kuti kodzero kana chido chero chipi zvacho pamunda uyu) ukatorwa nemutemo kuti ushandiswe kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu chidiki (2)—

(a)

Zvichienderana nezvinotaurwa muchikamu 295 (1) ne (2), hapana muripo uchabhadharwa pakutorwa kwawo, kunze kwemuripo webudiriro yakaitwa pauri usati watorwa;

(b) hapana munhu achanyorera kudare achikumbira kuongororwa kwenyaya chero ipi zvayo ine chekuita nemuripo, kusiya kwemuripo webudiriro

yakaitwa pamunda uyu usati watorwa, uye hapana dare rinogona kutambira kunyorera kwakadai; uye

(c)

kutorwa kwemunda uku hakugoni kupikiswa kuchishandiswa chikonzero chekuti pakaitwa rusarura runopikisana nezviri muchikamu 56.

(4) Minda yese yekurima—

(a)

yakanyorwa muNhamatidzwa 7 yemuBumbiro reMitemo rekare; kana kuti

(b) zuva rekutanga kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, yakatsanangurwa pasi pechikamu 16B(2)(a)(ii) kana kuti (iii) muBumbiro reMitemo rekare; inoramba iri yeHurumende, uye hapana muripo unobhadharwa pakutorwa kwayo kunze kwemuripo webudiriro yakaitwa pairi isati yatorwa.

(5) Nekukurumidza kwese kunogoneka, zvichitevera kutorwa kwemunda wekurima pasi pechikamu chidiki (2), mushandi ane basa rekunyoresa uridzi hwemunda anofanira, pasina kuziviswa zvakare, kupfuurira mberi nekukanzura uridzi hwemunda chero hupi zvahwo kana kunyoreswa mumabhuku chero api zvawo kuitira kukanzura zviri pamutemo uridzi uhwu nekunyoresa uridzi hweHurumende pamunda uyu.

(6) Mutemo weParamende unogona kuita kuti ive mhosva kuti munhu chero upi zvake anenge asina masimba ari pamutemo ave muridzi kana kuti agare pamunda wekurima wataurwa nezvawo muchikamu chino kana mumwewo munda weHurumende.

(7) Takatarisana nenyaya dzeminda yekurima inenge yakatorwa nemutemo nechinangwa chekugarisa vanhu patsva pasi pechirongwa chekugovera ivhu patsva, zvinotevera izvi ndizvo zvinofanira kuva zvakakosha kudarika zvimwe zvese—

(a)

pasi peutongi hwevadzvanyiriri vanhu veZimbabwe vakatorerwa minda nehumwe upfumi hwavo nenzira yakaipa pasina muripo;

(b) vanhu vakazotora zvombo vakarwa kuitira kuti vatore zvakare minda yavo nekuzvitonga; uye izvi ndizvo zvakazounza rusununguko rwenyika yeZimbabwe mugore ra1980;

(c)

vanhu veZimbabwe vanofanira kubvumidzwa kutaridza zvakare kodzero dzavo uye kutora minda yavo zvakare;

uye nokudaro—

(i) nyika yakambotonga Zimbabwe neudzvanyiriri inofanira kubhadhara muripo weminda yekurima yakatorwa nechisimba kuitira kugarisa vanhu patsva, kuburikidza nehomwe yakaringana inovambwa kuitira chinangwa ichi; uye

(ii) kana nyika yakambotonga Zimbabwe neudzvanyiriri ikatadza kubhadhara muripo kuburikidza nehomwe iyi, Hurumende yeZimbabwe haitarisirwi kubhadhara muripo weminda yakatorwa nechisimba kuitira kugarisa vanhu patsva.

(8) Chikamu chino chinoshandiswa pasina kukanganisa mutemo unomanikidza nyika yakambotonga Zimbabwe neudzvanyiriri kuti ibhadhare muripo pamunda wataurwa nezvawo muchikamu chino unenge watorwa nechinangwa chekugarisa vanhu patsva. 73

Kodzero munyaya dzezvenharaunda

(1) Munhu wese ane kodzero—

(a)

yekuva nenharaunda isingakanganisi utano kana kurarama kwake kwakanaka; uye

(b) yekuti nharaunda iyi ichengetedzwe kuitira kubatsira vanhu vari kurarama iye zvino nevachararama mune ramangwana, kuburikidza nemitemo uye mamwe matanho akakodzera—

(i) anodzivirira kusvibiswa kwenharaunda uye kukanganiswa kwezvinorarama munharaunda iyi;

(ii) anokurudzira kuchengetedzwa kwezviwanikwa; uye

(iii) anoita kuti kuve nebudiriro uye kushandiswa kwezviwanikwa kunochengetedza zvinorarama munharaunda pamwe chete nekukurudzira budiriro mune zveupfumi nezvekurarama.

(2) Hurumende inofanira kutora matanho ezvemitemo nemamwewo akakodzera, zvichienderana nemari nezvimwe zvekushandisa zvinenge zviripo, kuzadzikisa kuvapo kwekodzero dziri muchikamu chino. 74

Rusununguko rwekusabviswa panzvimbo zvisina chikonzero

Hapana munhu anogona kudzingwa pamba pake, kana kuparadzirwa musha wake pasina kushandiswa kwemasimba edare anopihwa mushure mekuongorora zvinhu zvese zvakakosha panyaya iyi. 75

Kodzero yekuwana dzidzo

(1) Mugari wese wemuZimbabwe pamwe chete neane mvumo yekugara muZimbabwe zvachose ane kodzero—

(a)

yekuwaniswa dzidzo yakakoshesesa yaanobhadharirwa neHurumende, kusanganisira dzidzo yakakoshesesa yevanhu vakuru; uye

(b) yekuwaniswa dzidzo yepamusoro inowanikwa kuburikidza nematanho akakodzera ezvemitemo nemamwewo anotorwa neHurumende.

(2) Munhu wese ane kodzero yekuvaka nekufambisa, achishandisa mari yake, zvikoro zvakazvimirira zvinowanisa dzidzo yakakodzera, ndokunge asingaiti rusarura chero rupi zvarwo runorambidzwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(3) Mutemo unogona kuvapo uyo unoona kuti zvikoro zvataurwa nezvazvo muchikamu chidiki (2) zvanyoreswa zviri pamutemo uye kuvharwa kwezvikoro chero zvipi zvazvo zvinenge zvisina zvose zvinotarisirwa pakunyoreswa uku.

(4) Nyika inofanira kutora matanho ezvemitemo nemamwewo akakodzera, zvichienderana nemari nezvimwe zvekushandisa zvinenge zviripo, kuzadzikisa kuvapo kwekodzero dziri muchikamu chidiki (1). 76

Kodzero yekuwana utano nekurapwa

(1) Mugari wese wemuZimbabwe pamwe chete neane mvumo yekugara muZimbabwe zvachose ane kodzero yekuwana rubatsiro rwezveutano nekurapwa rwakakoshesesa, kusanganisira rubatsiro mune zvine chekuita nekuzvara vana.

(2) Munhu wese ane urwere husingarapiki ane kodzero yekuwana rubatsiro rwakakoshesesa rwakanangana neurwere uhwu.

(3) Hapana munhu anonyimwa rubatsiro pachipatara chero chipi zvacho kana achinge akuvara kana kurwara zvisina gadziriro.

(4) Hurumende inofanira kutora matanho ezvemutemo nemamwewo akakodzera, zvichienderana nemari nezvimwe zvekushandisa zvinenge zviripo, kuzadzikisa kuvapo kwekodzero dziri muchikamu chino.

2013

Kodzero yekuwana zvekudya nemvura Munhu wese ane kodzero yekuwaniswa—

(a)

mvura yakachena, isina zvirwere uye inotakurika; uye

(b) zvekudya zvakakwana; uye Nyika inofanira kutora matanho ezvemutemo nemamwewo akakodzera, zvichienderana nemari nezvimwe zvekushandisa zvinenge zviripo, kuzadzikisa kuvapo kwekodzero iyi. 78

Kodzero munyaya dzewanano

(1) Munhu wese asvitsa makore gumi nemasere ekuberekwa ane kodzero yekuvamba mhuri.

(2) Hapana munhu anomanikidzwa kupinda muwanano asingadi.

(3) Vanhurume vega kana vanhukadzi vega havatenderwi kuroorana. GANHURO 3 Tsanangudzo Yedzimwe Kodzero 79

Kushandiswa kwezviri muGanhuro 3

(1) Chikamu chino chinotsanangura dzimwe kodzero nemhando dzerusununguko kuitira kuti pave nechokwadi chekuti kodzero nemhando dzerusununguko idzi dzawaniswa kumapoka evanhu adzakanangana nawo.

(2) Chikamu chino hachifaniri kutorwa sechinoderedza kukosha kwekodzero kana mhando yerusununguko chero ipi zvayo yakapihwa muChikamu 2. 80

Kodzero dzevanhukadzi

(1) Munhukadzi wese ane chiremera chakaenzana nechemunhurume uye izvi zvinosanganisira mikana yakaenzana mune zvematongerwo enyika, zveupfumi nezvekurarama.

(2) Vanhukadzi vane kodzero dzakafanana nedzevanhurume maererano nekuchengetwa kwevana, asi Mutemo weParamende unogona kushandiswa pakuzadzikiswa kwekodzero idzi.

(3) Mitemo, tsika, magariro nemararamiro ese anotyora kodzero dzevanhukadzi dzakapihwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino hazvikosheswi pakutyora kwazvinenge zvichiita kodzero idzi. 81

Kodzero dzevana vadiki

(1) Mwana mudiki wese, kureva kuti mwanakomana nemwanasikana wese ane makore ekuberekwa ari pasi pegumi nemasere, ane kodzero—

(a)

yekubatwa zvakaenzana pamutemo, kusanganisira kodzero yekunzikwa;

(b) yekupihwa zita nezita remhuri;

(c)

kana mwana—

(i) akazvarirwa muZimbabwe; kana kuti

(ii) akazvarirwa kunze kweZimbabwe uye ari mugari wemuZimbabwe kuburikidza nemadzitateguru; ane kodzero yekuwaniswa gwaro rekuzvarwa nekukasika;

(d) yekuchengetwa mumhuri kana kuti nevabereki, kana kuti yekuchengeterwa kumwe kwakakodzera kana akabviswa mumhuri;

(e)

yekuchengetedzwa kubva mukushandiswa mune zveupfumi uye zvepabonde, kushandiswa kwevana vadiki uye kubva mukubatwa kwakaipa, kusachengetwa, kana kushungurudzwa kwemhando chero ipi zvayo;

(f)

yekuwaniswa dzidzo, rubatsiro mune zveutano, zvekudya zvinovaka muviri nepekugara;

(g) yekusapinzwa muchikwata chinorwa hondo kana yekusapinzwa muhondo;

(h) yekusamanikidzwa kupinda muzviitiko zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro; uye

(i)

yekusachengetwa muusungwa kunze kwekunge kuchengetwa uku riri danho rekupedzisira uye, kana akachengetwa anofanira—

(i) kuchengetwa kwenguva dikisa yakakodzera;

(ii) kuchengeterwa pakasiyana nepanochengeterwa vanhu vane makore ekuberekwa anosvika kana kupfuura gumi nemasere; uye

(iii) kubatwa uye kuchengetedzwa nenzira inokoshesa zera rake.

(2) Zvakakosha kumwana mudiki ndizvo zvinofanira kutevedzwa panyaya chero ipi zvayo ine chekuita naye.

(3) Vana vadiki vane kodzero yekuchengetedzwa zvakakwana nematare, zvikuru sei neDare repamusoro semuchengeti wavo mukuru. 82

Kodzero dzevakwegura Vanhu vane makore ekuberekwa anopfuura makumi manomwe vane kodzero—

(a)

yekuchengetwa nekupihwa rubatsiro rwakakodzera kubva kumhuri dzavo nekuHurumende;

(b) yekupihwa rubatsiro mune zveutano nekurapwa kubva kuHurumende; uye

(c)

yekupihwa rubatsiro rwemari kuburikidza nechirongwa chekubatsirwa kwevanoshaiwa nehurumende;

uye Hurumende inofanira kutora matanho ezvemutemo nemamwewo, zvichienderana nemari nezvimwe zvekushandisa zvinenge zviripo, kuzadzikisa kuvapo kwekodzero iyi. 83

Kodzero dzevakaremara

Nyika inofanira kutora matanho akakodzera, zvichienderana nemari nezvimwe zvekushandisa zvinenge zviripo, kuona kuti vakaremara vagona kuita zvese zvavanokwanisa kuita nemiviri nepfungwa dzavo, kusanganisira matanho —

(a)

ekuita kuti vakwanise kuzviriritira;

(b) ekuita kuti vakwanise kurarama nemhuri dzavo nekupinda muzviitiko zvekurarama, nezvemitambo yekuzvitandadza;

(c)

ekuvachengetedza kubva mukushandiswa zvisizvo nekushungurudzwa kwemhando dzese;

(d) ekuvawanisa mikana yekurapwa miviri, pfungwa nemitezo inenge yakuvara kana kutyoka;

(e)

ekuvawanisa zvekushandisa zvavanoda pakudzidza kwavo; uye

(f)


2013

ekuvawanisa dzidzo yekuchikoro kana kudzidziswa mabasa kunobhadharirwa neHurumende pavanenge vachida rubatsiro urwu.

Kodzero dzevarwi vehondo yerusununguko

(1) Varwi vehondo yerusununguko, kureva kuti—

(a)

vaya vakarwa muhondo yekusunungura Zimbabwe;

(b) vaya vakabatsira varwi muhondo yekusunungura Zimbabwe; uye

(c)

vaya vakasungwa nekuchengetedzwa muusungwa nezvikonzero zvine chekuita nezvematongerwo enyika muhondo yekusunungura Zimbabwe;

vane kodzero yekuremekedzwa kuburikidza nebasa ravakaita pakusunungura nyika yeZimbabwe, uye yekuwaniswa zvekushandisa mukurarama kwakakodzera zvakaita semudyandigere nerubatsiro mune zveutano.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti kodzero dzinopihwa varwi verusununguko muchikamu chidiki (1) dzizadzikiswe.

GANHURO 4 Mashandiro Ekodzero Dzevanhu Nemhando Dzerusunguko Dzakakoshesesa 85

Mashandiro ekodzero nemhando dzerusununguko dzakakoshesesa

(1) Mumwe nemumwe wevanhu vanotevera, vanoti—

(a)

chero munhu anenge achizviitira chimwe chinhu pachake;

(b) chero munhu anenge achiita chimwe chinhu akamirira mumwe munhu asingagoni kuzviitira pachake;

(c)

chero munhu anenge achiita chimwe chinhu senhengo kana kuti akamiririra zvido zvechikwata chevanhu;

(d) chero munhu anenge achiita chimwe chinhu kuitira kuti zvinakire ruzhinji rwevanhu;

(e)

chero sangano rinenge richiita chimwe chinhu kuti chinakire nhengo dzaro;

ane kodzero yekuenda kudare, achitaura kuti kodzero kana mhando yerusununguko yakakoshesesa iri pasi peChitsauko chino yatyorwa, iri kutyorwa kana kuti inogona kutyorwa, uye dare redzimhosva iri rinogona kupa mutongo wakakodzera, unosanganisira mashoko anosimbisa kodzero neekuti pave nekuripwa.

(2) Zvekuti mumwe munhu atyora mutemo hazvimutadzisi kuenda kudare redzimhosva kuti abatsirwe pasi pechikamu chidiki (1).

(3) Mitemo yedare rimwe nerimwe inofanira kupa zvinofanira kutevedzwa munyaya dzevanenge vachitsvaga rubatsiro pasi pechikamu chidiki (1), uye mitemo iyi inofanira kuona kuti—

(a)

kodzero yekuenda kudare pasi pechikamu chidiki (1) yawaniswa zvakakwana;

(b) zvinofanira kutevedzwa maererano nekutongwa kwemhosva, kusanganisira mavambo ayo, zvishoma;

(c)

dare parinenge richicherechedza mitemo yeruenzaniso yagara ichitarisirwa, harifaniri kumanikidzwa nezvinofanira kutevedzwa pakutongwa kwemhosva nenzira dzisina kukodzera; uye

(d) munhu ane humwe hunyazvi, achibvumidzwa nedare, anogona kumira seshamwari yedare redzimhosva.

(4) Kusavapo kwemitemo yataurwa nezvayo muchikamu chidiki (3) hakuderedzi kodzero yekuvamba kutongwa kwemhosva pasi pechikamu chidiki (1) uye kuti nyaya inzwikwe uye kutongwa nedare.

GANHURO 5 Kushaiswa kweKodzero dzeVanhu neMhando dzeRusununguko Dzakakoshesesa 86

Kushaiswa kwekodzero nerusununguko rwevanhu

(1) Kodzero nemhando dzerusununguko dzakapihwa muChitsauko chino dzinofanira kushandiswa zvakakodzera uye nenzira inoremekedzawo kodzero nerusununguko rwevamwe vanhu.

(2) Kodzero nemhando dzerusununguko dzakapihwa muChitsauko chino dzinogona kurambidzwa chete kana kuchitevedzwa mutemo wezvakasiyana-siyana uye kusvika padanho rekuti kurambidzwa uku kwakafanira, kwakakodzera, kunotarisirwa uye kune zvikonzero munyika inoitira zvinhu pachena, ine ruenzaniso uye inotevedza zvido zveruzhinji rwevanhu, inokoshesa unhu hwevanhu, kuenzana nerusununguko rwavo, kana kuchitariswa zvese zvine chekuita nekodzero nerusununguko izvi, kusanganisira—

(a)

mamiriro ekodzero kana rusununguko zvinenge zvichitaurwa nezvazvo;

(b) chinangwa chekurambidza uku, zvikuru sei kuti zvakakodzera here nechinangwa chekudzivirira vanhu, kuchengeteka kweruzhinji rwevanhu munyika, runyararo munyika, unhu hwakanaka munyika, utano hweruzhinji rwevanhu kana urongwa hwemashandisirwo enzvimbo mumadhorobha kana munyika;

(c)

mamiriro ekurambidzwa uku nepanogumira kurambidzwa uku;

(d) maitiro ekuti kushandiswa kwekodzero nemhando dzerusununguko dzemunhu chero upi zvake hadzikanganisi kodzero nemhando dzerusununguko dzevamwe;

(e)

ukama huri pakati pekurambidzwa kwekodzero nechinangwa chacho, zvikuru sei kuti kurambidza uku kuri kumanikidza kodzero kana rusununguko ruri kutaurwa nezvarwo zvakanyanya kupfuura zvinodikanwa pakuzadzikisa chinangwa chacho here; uye

(f)

kuti pane dzimwe nzira dzisinganyanyomanikidza dzekuzadzikisa chinangwa chekurambidza uku here.

(3) Hapana mutemo unogona kurambidza kodzero dzinotevera idzi dziri muChitsauko chino, uye hapana munhu anofanira kudzityora—

(a)

kodzero yekurarama, kusiya kwepanoenderana nezvakatsanangurwa muchikamu 48;

(b) kodzero yekuva nechiremera semunhu;

(c)

kodzero yekusarwadziswa kana yekusabatwa zvisina unhu uye zvinobvisa chimiro;

(d) kodzero yekusaiswa muutapwa;


(e)

kodzero yekutongwa zvakafanira;

(f)

kodzero yekupihwa gwaro rine masimba ekuti munhu anenge akachengetwa muusungwa asunungurwe kana kuti aendeswe kudare redzimhosva kuti zvionekwe kuti kubatwa kwake kuri pamutemo here, kana kuti kukumbira kuti kunenge kwakachengeterwa munhu muusungwa kuzivikanwe sezviri muchikamu 50(7)(a).

Kushaiswa kwekodzero nerusununguko panguva yedambudziko rakaipisisa munyika

(1) Pamusoro pekushaiswa kwekodzero kwabvumidzwa muchikamu 86, kodzero nemhando dzerusununguko dzakakoshesesa dzakapihwa muChitsauko chino dzinogona kuderedzwa zvakare nemutemo wakanyorwa unopa matanho anoshandiswa pakugadzirisa mamiriro ezvinhu anoitika panguva yedambudziko rakakurisisa munyika, asi kusvika panobvumidzwa nechikamu chino uye neNhamatidzwa 2.

(2) Mutemo wakanyorwa wataurwa nezvawo muchikamu chidiki (1) nematanho ezvemutemo chero api zvawo anotorwa pasi pemutemo uyu anofanira kuziviswa mugwaro rehurumende reGazette.

(3) Kushaiswa kwekodzero chero kupi zvako kunoitwa kuburikidza nekushandisa mutemo wakanyorwa wataurwa nezvawo muchikamu chidiki (1) pakodzero kana mhando yerusununguko yakakoshesesa yapihwa muChitsauko chino hakufaniri kupfurikidza zvinodikanwa panguva yedambudziko renyika rakakurisisa.

(4) Hapana mutemo unodzikwa wakanangana nedambudziko rakakurisisa renyika, uye hapana matanho ezvemitemo kana mamwewo anotorwa kuburikidza nekuziviswa kwedambudziko iri, zvinogona—

(a)

kupa ruchengetedzo kana kubvumira ruchengetedzo rweHurumende kana sangano kana bazi rehurumende padanho chero ripi zvaro, kana kuti munhu chero upi zvake, kana paitwa chinhu chisiri pamutemo chero chipi zvacho; kana kuti

(b) kushaisa kana kutyorwa kwekodzero chero dzipi zvadzo dzataurwa nezvadzo muchikamu 86(3). CHITSAUKO 5 Vatungamiriri GANHURO 1 Masimba Evatungamiriri 88

Masimba evatungamiriri

(1) Masimba evatungamiriri anobva kuvanhu veZimbabwe uye anofanira kushandiswa nenzira inoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(2) Masimba eutungamiriri hwenyika yeZimbabwe ari muMutungamiriri weNyika, uyo anomashandisa zvichienderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino, kuburikidza nedare reMakurukota eHurumende.

GANHURO 2 Mutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika 89

Mutungamiriri weNyika

Mutungamiriri weNyika ndiye mutungamiriri weHurumende ari zvakare mukuru weMauto enyika. 90

Mabasa eMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kucherechedza, kuchengetedza, kuteerera nekuremekedza Bumbiro reMitemo rino semutemo wepamusorosoro wenyika, uye anofanira kuona kuti Bumbiro reMitemo rino nemimwe mitemo yese zvakosheswa chaizvo.

(2) Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira—

(a)

kukurudzira kubatana nerunyararo munyika kuitira kuti zvibatsire vanhu vese vemuZimbabwe;

(b) kucherechedza nekukoshesa vavariro nezvinangwa zvehondo yerusununguko;

(c)

kuona kuti kodzero dzevanhu nemhando dzerusununguko dzakakoshesesa uye kuti mitemo yenyika yachengetedzwa; uye

(d) kuremekedza kusiyana kwevanhu nenharaunda dzenyika yeZimbabwe. 91

Zvinodikanwa kuti munhu asarudzwe kuva Mutungamiriri weNyika kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Munhu anokodzera kusarudzwa seMutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana—

(a)

ari mugari wemuZimbabwe kuburikidza nekuzvarirwamo kana kuti nekuti madzitateguru ake zvaiva zvizvarwa zvemuZimbabwe;

(b) asvitsa makore ekuberekwa makumi mana;

(c)

achigara muZimbabwe; uye

(d) akanyoresa kuvhota.

(2) Munhu haakodzeri kusarudzwa seMutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana akamboita Mutungamiriri weNyika pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino kaviri, kakatevedzana kana kuti kasina kutevedzana, uye pasi pechikamu chidiki chino, makore matatu kana kupfuura ekuva Mutungamiriri weNyika anotorwa senguva yakakwana yekuva Mutungamiriri weNyika. 92

Kusarudzwa kweMutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Kusarudzwa kweMutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vaviri kunofanira kuitwa mukati menguva yakatarwa muchikamu 158.

(2) Wese anenge achikwikwidza kuva Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kusarudza vanhu vaviri vanomira pamwe chete naye musarudzo seVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika, uye anofanira kudoma mumwe wevanhu ava seMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga uye mumwe seMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wechipiri.

(3) Mutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vanosarudzwa panguva imwe chete nevanhu vakanyoresa kuvhota munyika yese yeZimbabwe uye zvinotevedzwa pakusarudzwa kwavo ndezvakatarwa muMutemo weSarudzo.

(4) Zvinodikanwa kuti munhu akwanise kunyoresa semuvhoti, uye kuti akwanise kuzovhota musarudzo yeMutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika zvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 4.

(5) Sarudzo yeMutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika inofanira kuitwa pamwe chete nesarudzo huru dzenhengo dzeParamende, dzemakanzuru ematunhu makuru, dzemakanzuru emaguta nedzemakanzuru emaruwa neemunharaunda. 93

Kupikiswa kwesarudzo yeMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu chino, wese anenge akwikwidza musarudzo anogona kupikisa sarudzo yeMutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti yeMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kuburikidza nekuendesa gwaro rekutsutsumwa kana kunyorera kuDare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo mukati memazuva manomwe zvichitevera kuziviswa kwezvinenge zvabuda musarudzo.

(2) Sarudzo yeMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika inogona chete kupikiswa panyaya yekuti ainge asingakodzeri kusarudzwa.

(3) Dare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rinofanira kunzwa uye kutonga nyaya iyi pasi pemitemo iri muchikamu chidiki (1) mukati memazuva gumi nemana zvichitevera kuturwa kwegwaro rekutsutsumwa kana kwekunyorerwa kunenge kwaitwa kudare iri, uye zvinenge zvatongwa nedare ndizvo zvinofanira kutevedzwa.

(4) Pakutonga nyaya ine chekuita negwaro rekutsutsumwa kana kunyorerwa kunenge kwaitwa kudare redzimhosva iri pasi pechikamu chidiki (1), Dare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rinogona––

(a)

kutaura kuti ndiani anenge akunda musarudzo;

(b) kuti sarudzo haina kuitwa zvakanaka, zvichireva kuti imwe sarudzo itsva inofanira kuitwa mukati memazuva makumi matanhatu kubva musi wapihwa mutongo; kana kuti

(c)

kupa mumwe mutongo warinoona sewakaenzanirana uye wakakodzera.

(5) Kana, mushure megwaro rekutsutsumwa kana gwaro rekunyorera pasi pechikamu chidiki (1)—

(a)

Dare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rikakanzura kusarudzwa kweMutungamiriri weNyika, sarudzo yeVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vaviri inobva yangoraswawo pamwe chete ipapo;

(b) Dare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rikakanzura kusarudzwa kweMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika mumwe chete kana kuti vese vari vaviri, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira, nekukurumidza, kudoma munhu kana vanhu vanokodzera, zvichienderana nezvinenge zviripo, kuti ave kana kuti vave, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika.

Kutanga basa kweMutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Vanhu vanosarudzwa seMutungamiriri weNyika uye seVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vanotanga basa pavanoita mhiko yekuva Mutungamiriri weNyika kana yekuva Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika pamberi peMukuru wevatongi kuchitevedzwa urongwa huri muNhamatidzwa yechitatu. Vanofanira kuita mhiko idzi—

(a)

zuva rechipfumbamwe zvichitevera kuziviswa kwekuti ndivo vanenge vasarudzwa; kana kuti

(b) kana sarudzo yavo yapikiswa, mukati memaawa makumi mana nemasere zvichitevera kuzivisa kweDare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kuti ndivo vanenge vakunda.

(2) Mutungamiriri weNyika anenge aripo anoramba ari pachinzvimbo kusvikira kutanga basa kwemutungamiriri anenge achangosarudzwa, kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (1).

(3) Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika achava Mutungamiriri weNyika zvichitevera kufa, kusiya basa kana kubviswa pachinzvimbo kweMutungamiriri weNyika, uye anotanga basa kana achinge aita mhiko yekuva Mutungamiriri weNyika sezvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 3 pamberi peMukuru wevatongi kana kuti pemutongi mukuru anenge aripo. Anofanira kuita mhiko iyi nekukasika uye chero zvaita sei, mukati memaawa makumi mana nemasere zvichitevera kubva pachigaro kweanga ari Mutungamiriri weNyika. 95

Urefu hwenguva yekuva pachinzvimbo kweMutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Urefu hwenguva yekuti Mutungamiriri weNyika kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika ave pachinzvimbo hunotangira pazuva raagadzwa uye anotanga basa kuchitevedzwa chikamu 94(1)(a) kana kuti 94(3).

(2) Nguva yekuva Mutungamiriri weNyika kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika inopfuurira mberi kusvikira—

(a)

asiya basa kana kuti abviswa pachinzvimbo; kana kuti

(b) mushure mesarudzo, zvaziviswa kuti asarudzwa zvakare kana kuti zvaziviswa kuti pane Mutungamiriri weNyika mutsva asarudzwa; uye, kusiya kwezvinenge zvakanyorwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino, urefu hwenguva yavachava pazvinzvimbo ndehwemakore mashanu uye hunoenderana neurefu hwenguva yeParamende. 96

Kusiya basa kweMutungamiriri weNyika kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Mutungamiriri weNyika anogona kusiya basa kuburikidza neziviso yakanyorwa kuMutauriri weParamende, uyo anofanira kupa chiziviso ichi kuruzhinji rwevanhu nekukasika, uye chero zvaita sei, mukati memaawa makumi maviri nemana.

(2) Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika anogona kusiya basa kuburikidza neziviso yakanyorwa kuMutungamiriri weNyika uyo anofanira kupa chiziviso ichi kuruzhinji rwevanhu nekukasika, uye chero zvaita sei, mukati memaawa makumi maviri nemana.

2013

Kubviswa pachinzvimbo kweMutungamiriri weNyika kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, kuburikidza nekusarudza zvekuita pamwe chete uye kunotsigirwa nevanhu vane uwandu hunosvika kana kupfuura hwepakati nepakati hwenhengo dzemapoka maviri aya, zvinogona kuwirirana panyaya yekuti Mutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika obviswa pachinzvimbo here nekuda kwe—

(a)

kukanganisa basa zvakanyanya;

(b) kukundikana kutevedzera, kukoshesa kana kuchengetedza Bumbiro reMitemo rino;

(c)

kutyora zviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino achiziva uye achida;

(d) kutadza kuita mabasa anotarisirwa pachinzvimbo chake kuburikidza nekurwara kwemuviri kana kwepfungwa; zviongororwe pasi pechikamu chino.

(2) Kuburikidza nekusarudza zvekuita kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (1), Komiti inoona nezveMitemo neZvinofanira kutevedzwa inofanira kusarudza imwe komiti yemubatanidzwa weSeneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika inoumbwa nenhengo pfumbamwe, uye inoenderana nemapato ezvematongerwo enyika ane nhengo dziri muParamende, kuti iongorore kubviswa pachinzvimbo kweMutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti kweMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika.

(3) Kana—

(a)

komiti yemubatanidzwa inenge yasarudzwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu chidiki (2) ikati Mutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika abviswe pachinzvimbo; uye

(b) Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvikabvumirana, kuburikidza nechibvumirano chinoitwa pamwe chete uye chinotsigirwa nevanhu vane uwandu hunosvika kana kupfuura zvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzemapoka maviri aya, kuti Mutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika abviswe pachinzvimbo; Mutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika anobva abva pachinzvimbo. 98

Kuchengetedzwa kweMutungamiriri weNyika kubva mukutongwa mumatare edzimhosva

(1) Paanenge ari pachigaro, Mutungamiriri weNyika haakwanisi kutongwa pachinhu chero chipi zvacho, uye mudare chero ripi zvaro, nekuda kwezvinhu zvaanenge aita kana kuti asina kuita.

(2) Matare anokwanisa kugara pasi achizeya nyaya dzakanangana neaimbova Mutungamiriri weNyika dzakanangana nezvaanenge akaita kana kuti asina kuita asati ava kana kuti ava Mutungamiriri weNyika.

(3) Kubvunzwa chikwereti kana kupomerwa mhosva kunoitwa Mutungamiriri weNyika asati ava, kana kuti ava pachigaro, kunomboiswa padivi panguva yaanenge ari pachigaro.

(4) Pakutongwa kwemhosva dzese dzinenge dzakanangana neaimbova Mutungamiriri weNyika dzakanangana nezvaanenge akaita kana kuti asina kuita, aimbova Mutungamiriri weNyika anokwanisa kukakavara achiudza dare kuti kuita kana kusaita chimwe chinhu kwake kwaive kwakanangana nechikonzero chakanaka.

Mabasa eVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika

Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vanobatsira Mutungamiriri weNyika pakuita basa rake uye, vanoita rimwewo basa ravanonzi vaite neMutungamiriri weNyika, kusanganisira kutungamirira Bazi reHurumende chero ripi zvaro kana Mutemo weParamende. 100 Anofanobata chinzvimbo cheMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Pese apo Mutungamiriri weNyika anenge asiri muZimbabwe kana kuti asingakwanisi kuita basa rake nekuda kwekurwara kana chimwewo chikonzero, basa iri rinofanira kuitwa—

(a)

neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga;

(b) apo Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga anenge asingakwanisi kuita basa iri, neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wechipiri; kana kuti

(c)

kana pasina Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika anokwanisa kuita basa iri, neGurukota reHurumende—

(i) rinogara rakasarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kuti rizoita basa iri kana izvi zvaitika; kana kuti

(ii) rinosarudzwa neDare reMakurukota eHurumende kana pasina Gurukota rakasarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika sezvakatarwa mundima diki (i).

(2) Kunze kwekunge pachitevedzwa zvinenge zvadzikwa neruzhinji rwenhengo dzese dzeDare reMakurukota eHurumende, munhu anenge achimbofanobata chigaro cheMutungamiriri weNyika sezvakatarwa mundima diki (1) haafaniri kushandisa masimba eMutungamiriri weNyika—

(a)

ekutuma mauto enyika kuita rimwewo basa kana kuhondo;

(b) ekupinda mumubatanidzwa nedzimwe nyika dzepasi rese chero upi zvawo kana kuti muchisungo kana chibvumirano chenyika idzi chero chipi zvacho;

(c)

ekusarudza kana kukanzura kusarudzwa kweMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende; kana kuti

(d) kupa kana kushandura basa reMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende kusanganisira, tichitarisa Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana Gurukota reHurumende, kutungamirira mutemo chero upi zvavo weParamende kana kuti Bazi reHurumende kana chikamu cheHurumende chero chipi zvacho, kana kukanzura kupihwa basa iri kwavo. 101 Kutsiviwa kweMutungamiriri weNyika kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekufa, kusiya basa kana kusakwanisa kuita basa

(1) Kana Mutungamiriri weNyika akafa, akasiya basa kana kuti akabviswa pachigaro—

(a)

Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga ndiye anova Mutungamiriri weNyika kusvikira panokwanira nguva yaifanira kushandwa neaimbova Mutungamiriri weNyika;

(b) Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wechipiri ndiye anova Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga, kusvikira panokwanira nguva yaifanira kushandwa neaimbova Mutungamiriri weNyika; uye

(c)

paanotanga kushanda seMutungamiriri weNyika, aimbova Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga anofanira kusarudza munhu akakodzera kuti ave Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wechipiri kusvikira panokwanira nguva yaifanira kushandwa neaimbova Mutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga akafa, akasiya basa kana kuti akabviswa pachigaro—

(a)

Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wechipiri ndiye anova Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga, kusvikira panokwanira nguva yaifanira kushandwa neaimbova Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga; uye

(b) Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kukurumidza kusarudza munhu akakodzera kuti ave Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wechipiri kusvikira panokwanira nguva yaifanira kushandwa neaimbova Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga. 102 Mari dzinotambirwa neMutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika

(1) Mutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vanofanira kuwana muhoro, dzimwe mari, mudyandigere nezvimwewo zvavanowana, izvo zvakatarwa pasi peMutemo weParamende.

(2) Muhoro nedzimwe mari dzinowanikwa neMutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika dzinofanira kubva muMuHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende.

(3) Munhu anenge asiya chinzvimbo cheMutungamiriri weNyika kana cheMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika ane kodzero yekuwana—

(a)

mudyandigere unoenderana nemuhoro unotambirwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kana neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, zvichienderana nekuti ndiani pavaviri ava; uye

(b) Mari dzavanotambira idzodzo nezvimwewo zvavanowana izvo zvinenge zvakatarwa pasi peMutemo weParamende. 103 Mutungamiriri weNyika, Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika nevaimbova pazvinzvimbo izvi havafaniri kushanda mamwe mabasa Mutungamiriri weNyika neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika, nevese vaimbova Vatungamiriri weNyika kana Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika, havafaniri kuva nerimwe basa muHurumende kana kushandira mumwe munhu chero upi zvake, pavanenge vachiri pabasa kana kuti pavanenge votambira mudyandigere kubva kuNyika nekuda kwekuti vaimbova Vatungamiriri veNyika kana kuti Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika.

GANHURO 3 Makurukota eHurumende, Vatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende nedare reMakurukota eHurumende 104 Kusarudzwa kweMakurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende

(1) Mutungamiriri weNyika anosarudza Makurukota eHurumende uye anoapa basa rekuita, kusanganisira kutungamirira Mutemo weParamende chero upi zvawo kana kuti Bazi reHurumende kana chikamu chero chipi zvacho cheHurumende, asi Mutungamiriri weNyika anogona kuzvichengetera basa rekutungamirira mabasa eMutemo, Bazi reHurumende kana chimwe chikamu.

(2) Mutungamiriri weNyika anokwanisa kusarudza Vatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende kuti abatsire Gurukota reHurumende chero ripi zvaro pakuita basa raro.

(3) Makurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende vanosarudzwa kubva pamaSeneta kana kuti paNhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika asi vanosvika vashanu, avo vanosarudzwa nekuda kweunyazvi nekugona basa kwavo, vanokwanisa kunge vasiri nhengo dzeParamende.

(4) Pakusarudza Makurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kutungamirirwa nenyaya yekuti pave nekuenzana kwekumiririrwa kwematunhu uye kwevanhurume nevanhukadzi.

(5) Makurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende vanenge vasiri nhengo dzeParamende, vanokwanisa kupinda nekutaura muSeneti kana muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, asi havabvumirwi kuvhota.

(6) Asati atora chinzvimbo chake, munhu anenge asarudzwa kuva Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende, anofanira kuita mhiko yeMakurukota eHurumende pamberi peMutungamiriri weNyika, kuchitevedzwa zvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 3. 105 Dare reMakurukota eHurumende

(1) Kune Dare reMakurukota eHurumende rinosanganisira Mutungamiriri weNyika, semukuru weDare reMakurukota eHurumende, Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika nemamwewo Makurukota anosarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kuti apinde muDare reMakurukota eHurumende.

(2) Misangano yeDare reMakurukota eHurumende inotungamirirwa neMutungamiriri weNyika, kana kuti paanenge asipo, neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika, kana kuti pavanenge vasipo, neGurukota reHurumende rinotaurwa nezvaro muchikamu 100(1)(c). 106 Mashandiro eVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika, Makurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende

(1) Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wese, Gurukota reHurumende rese uye Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende wese anofanira kutevedza zviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(2) Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika, Makurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende havatarisirwi, pavanenge vari pazvinzvimbo zvavo—

(a)

2013

kuva nerimwe basa chero ripi zvaro muHurumende kana kuita rimwewo basa chero ripi zvaro ravanobhadharwa;

(b) kuita chero zvipi zvazvo zvisingatarisirwi pabasa ravo, kana kuita zvinhu zvinokwanisa kuita kuti pave nemakakatanwa pakati pebasa ravo neupenyu hwavo; kana kuti

(c)

kushandisa chinzvimbo chavo kana ruzivo chero rupi zvarwo rwavanopihwa, pakuzvipfumisa kana kubatsira kupfumisa vamwe vanhu chero vapi zvavo nenzira isina kufanira.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutaura zvinofanira kutevedzwa neVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika, Makurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende. 107 Kuongororwa kwemashandiro eVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika, Makurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende

(1) Kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wese, Gurukota reHurumende rese, neMutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende wese, anofanira kutsanangura basa raanenge achiita, akazvimiririra kana kuti pamwe chete nevamwe, kuMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wese, Gurukota reHurumende rese neMutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende wese, anofanira kupinda mumisangano yeParamende uye yemakomiti eParamende kuitira kuti apindure mibvunzo iri maererano nenyaya dzaane chekuita nadzo, akazvimiririra kana kuti pamwe chete nevamwe. 108 Urefu hwenguva yekuva pachinzvimbo kweMakurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende

(1) Chinzvimbo cheGurukota reHurumende kana cheMutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende chinosara chisina munhu—

(a)

kana abviswa pachinzvimbo ichi neMutungamiriri weNyika;

(b) kana asiya chinzvimbo ichi kuburikidza neziviso yakanyorwa kuMutungamiriri weNyika;

(c)

kana pava nemumwe Mutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (4), Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende aimbenge ari nhengo yeParamende paakasarudzwa kuva Gurukota reHurumende, kana kuti kuva Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende, anobva angosiya chinzvimbo chake kana asisiri nhengo yeParamende.

(3) Kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (4), Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende aimbenge asiri nhengo yeParamende paakasarudzwa kuva Gurukota reHurumende kana kuti kuva Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende, anobva angosiya chinzvimbo chake kana paitika zvaikwanisa kuti, dai aive nhengo yeParamende, chigaro chake chemuParamende chaizosara chisina munhu.

(4) Kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, kana Paramende ichinge yaparadzwa, Makurukota eHurumende kana Vatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende vanoramba vane zvinzvimbo zvavo kusvikira anenge asarudzwa kuva Mutungamiriri weNyika atanga basa zvichitevera sarudzo huru dzemunyika.

109 Kushaya chivimbo neHurumende

(1) Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, kuburikidza nekusarudza zvekuita pamwe chete uye kunotsigirwa nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzezvikamu zviviri izvi, zvinokwanisa kutsigira vhoti yekushaya chivimbo neHurumende.

(2) Nyaya yekuti pave nevhoti yekushaya chivimbo neHurumende inokwanisa kuparurwa chete kana—

(a)

ziviso yenyaya iyi yapihwa Mutauriri pachine nguva inosvika kana kupfuura mazuva manomwe; uye

(b) ziviso yacho yasainwa nechikamu chepakati nepakati kana kupfuura chenhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(3) Nyaya yekuti pave nevhoti yekushaya chivimbo neHurumende—

(a)

inofanira kuzeyiwa pamusangano weZvikamu zviviri zveParamende uye mukati memazuva makumi maviri nerimwe mushure mekunge Mutauriri atambira ziviso yenyaya iyi; uye

(b) inofanira kuvhoterwa mukati memisangano minomwe yakatevedzana kubvira painoparurwa; kana isina kuvhoterwa panguva iyi, haizotariswi zvakare.

(4) Kana Paramende yatsigira vhoti yekushaya chivimbo neHurumende, Mutungamiriri weNyika, mukati memazuva gumi nemana zvichitevera vhoti iyi, anofanira—

(a)

kubvisa Makurukota eHurumende ese neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende vese pazvigaro, kunze kwekuti vachitenge vasiya basa nechekare nekuda kwekutsigirwa kwevhoti, uye anofanira kusarudza vamwe vanhu vanotora zvinzvimbo zvevaanenge abvisa ava; kana

(b) kuparadza Paramende uye, mukati memazuva makumi mapfumbamwe, oita kuti kuve nesarudzo huru dzenyika.

(5) Kana Mutungamiriri weNyika asina kuita sezvakatarwa muchikamu chidiki (4) mukati memazuva gumi nemana zvichitevera vhoti yekushaya chivimbo neHurumende, Paramende inenge yatoparadzwa.

GANHURO 4 Mabasa eUtungamiriri akakosha 110 Mabasa akakosha eUtungamiriri eMutungamiriri weNyika neDare reMakurukota eHurumende

(1) Mutungamiriri weNyika ane masimba aanopihwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino uye neMutemo weParamende kana mumwewo mutemo, kusanganisira masimba ekuti akwanise kuita mabasa eMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, Mutungamiriri weNyika ndiye anoona nezve—

(a)

kutambira nekusaina maBhiri eMutemo;

(b) kuendesa Bhiri reMutemo kuDare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kuti ripe pfungwa dzaro nekurudziro yekuti mutemo uri muBhiri racho unoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo here;

(c)

2013

kudaidza Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, Seneti kana Paramende kumisangano inenge isina kugara yakarongwa nechinangwa chekuita basa rakakosha;

(d) kusarudza vanhu vanotora zvinzvimbo zvinonzi neBumbiro reMitemo kana nemutemo, Mutungamiriri weNyika ndiye anofanira kusarudza vanhu vanozvitora;

(e)

kuita kuti kuitwe sarudzo kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino;

(f)

kuita kuti kuitwe mareferenda pamusoro pechinhu chero chipi zvacho sezvinotarisirwa nemutemo;

(g) kutuma mauto kuhondo;

(h) kupa nyembe nemiripo;

(i)

kusarudza vamiririri veZimbabwe kune dzimwe nyika, vanoshanda nevamiriri ivava uye vamwe vese vane masimba azere ekumiririra Zimbabwe kune dzimwe nyika; uye

(j)

kutambira nekukoshesa vamiririri vedzimwe nyika uye vamwe vese vane masimba azere ekumiririra dzimwe nyika muZimbabwe.

(3) Kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, Dare reMakurukota eHurumende rinoita basa re—

(a)

kutaura kuti basa reHurumende rinofanira kufamba sei;

(b) kufambisa basa reHurumende muParamende;

(c)

kugadzira, kuvamba nekuparura zviri maererano nekushandiswa kwemitemo yenyika;

(d) kugadzira nekushandisa gwara renyika; uye

(e)

kupa mazano kuMutungamiriri weNyika.

(4) Kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, Mutungamiriri weNyika anokwanisa kupinda nekunyorerana zvibvumirano nedzimwe nyika uye nehurumende dzedzimwe nyika uye, anokwanisa kupinza nyika mumibatanidzwa yedzimwe nyika dzepasi rese.

(5) Zvinenge zvafungwa neMutungamiriri weNyika zvinofanira kunyorwa pasi kana zviri zvaitwa pamutemo.

(6) Pakuita basa rake rakakosha, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuita zvinhu apihwa mazano neDare reMakurukota eHurumende kunze kwekunge achiita zvakatarwa muchikamu chidiki (2) chiri pamusoro apa. 111 Hondo nerunyararo

(1) Mutungamiriri weNyika ane simba rekutanga hondo nekuunza runyararo, uye anofanira kuzivisa Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika mukati memazuva manomwe azvinoita misangano yazvo.

(2) Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, zvakabatana uye zvichitsigirwa nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzeParamende, zvinokwanisa kuwirirana kuti hondo yava kuvambwa imiswe.

(3) Kana Paramende yawirirana kuti kuvambwa kwehondo kumiswe, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kutora matanho ese anogoneka ekusiyana nehondo iyi, achikoshesesa nyaya yekuti pasave nenjodzi inowira mauto nezvombo zveZimbabwe.

112 Simba rekuregererera

(1) Mutungamiriri weNyika, mushure mekunzwa zvinofungwa neDare reMakurukota eHurumende, anokwanisa kushandisa simba rekuregererera, zvichireva kuti, anokwanisa—

(a)

kuita kuti munhu chero upi zvake ane chekuita nemhosva kana kuti anenge atongwa akawanikwa ane mhosva yekutyora mutemo chero upi zvawo, aregererwe;

(b) kuita kuti pambomira kupihwa mutongo wemhosva chero ipi zvayo kwenguva inenge yakatarwa kana kuti isina kutarwa;

(c)

kutsiva mutongo unenge watemerwa munhu pamhosva chero ipi zvayo neuri nani; kana

(d) kumboisa padivi, kwenguva yakatarwa kana kubvisa mutongo wese zvawo kana kuti chikamu chemutongo wemhosva chero ipi zvayo, kana kuti muripo unenge wanzi ubhadharwe pamhosva chero ipi zvayo; uye anokwanisa kudzika zvinofanira kutevedzwa pakuregerera, kumisa, kutsiva kana kumboiswa padivi kwemhosva.

(2) Kana munhu anogara muZimbabwe zviri pamutemo atemerwa mutongo ari kune imwe nyika, nemhosva yekutyora mutemo unoshanda munyika iyoyo, Mutungamiriri weNyika anokwanisa kutaura kuti mutongo iwoyo usatorwe semutongo kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino kana mumwewo mutemo unoshanda muZimbabwe.

(3) Kana Mutungamiriri weNyika aregerera munhu, amisa kushanda kwemutemo, atsiva kana kumboisa mutongo padivi, zvipi zvazvo zvaanenge aita zvinofanira kuburitswa muGwaro rehurumende reGazette. 113 Nguva yematambudziko akaipisisa munyika

(1) Mutungamiriri weNyika anokwanisa kudzika muGazette kuti pava nenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa munyika yese kana kuti muchikamu chero chipi zvacho cheZimbabwe.

(2) Kudzikwa kwenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa kunoregera kukosheswa mushure memazuva gumi nemana, kubvira pazuva rekuburitswa muGazette, kunze kwekuti, nguva iyoyo isati yakwana, zvadzikwa zvatsigirwa nevhoti inenge yaitwa nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzeParamende, pamusangano wemubatanidzwa weSeneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(3) Kana Paramende yaparadzwa mukati memazuva gumi nemana mushure mekudzikwa kwenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa, kudzikwa ikoko kunoregedza kukosheswa mushure memazuva makumi maviri nerimwe, kubvira pazuva rekuburitswa muGazette, kunze kwekuti, munguva iyoyo, zvadzikwa zvatsigirwa nevhoti inenge yaitwa neruzhinji rwenhengo dzese dzeParamende itsva, pamusangano wemubatanidzwa weSeneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(4) Kudzikwa kwenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa kunenge kwatsigirwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu chidiki (2) kana (3) kunoramba kuripo kwemwedzi mitatu kubvira pazuva rekuburitswa kweziviso yenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa iyi muGazette kunze kwekunge kwambokanzurwa kana kuti kwarega kukosheswa kuchitevedzwa chikamu chino.

(5) Kana kudzikwa kwenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa kusina kutsigirwa neParamende mushure mekuzeya, kana kuti nekuda kwechikonzero chero chipi zvacho

kusina kutariswa neParamende mukati menguva yakatarwa muchikamu chino, mukati memazuva manomwe, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuburitsa muGazette kuti zvaakambodzika hazvisisina simba.

(6) Kana, zvichitevera chisungo chinenge chatsigirwa neruzhinji rwenhengo dzeSeneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika dzinenge dziri pamusangano wemubatanidzwa wezvikamu zviviri izvi, Paramende yataura kuti kudzikwa kwenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa—

(a)

kunofanira kuenderera mberi kwenguva isingapfuuri mwedzi mitatu, nekukurumidza, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira, kuburikidza nekuburitsa muGazette, kuwedzeredza nguva yezvaakadzika, kwenguva inenge yataurwa;

(b) kunofanira kusakosheswa kana kuti kunofanira kuenderana chete nenzvimbo diki, nekukurumidza, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira, kuburikidza nekuburitsa muGazette, kutaura kuti hakuchakosheswi kana kuti zvaakadzika zvava kuenderana nenzvimbo diki iyoyo.

(7) Zvichitevera kutsutsumwa kwemunhu chero upi zvake, Dare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rinokwanisa kuongorora kukodzera kwe—

(a)

kudzikwa kwenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa;

(b) kuwedzerwa kwenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa.

(8) Dare remitemo chero ripi zvaro rinokwanisa kuongorora kukodzera kwemutemo chero upi zvawo, kana rimwe danho rinenge ratorwa nekuda kwekudzikwa kwenguva yematambudziko akaipisisa. GANHURO 5 Gweta Guru reHurumende 114 Gweta Guru reHurumende

(1) Kune Gweta Guru reHurumende rinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Munhu anenge adomwa kuva Gweta Guru reHurumende anotanga basa mushure mekuita mhiko yekuvimbika neyekuva pachinzvimbo pamberi peMutungamiriri weNyika kana mumwewo munhu anenge apihwa masimba neMutungamiriri weNyika, kuchitevedzwa zvakapihwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(3) Munhu anokodzera kudomwa seGweta Guru reHurumende kana achikodzera kudomwa semutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusoro.

(4) Mabasa eGweta Guru reHurumende ndeanoti—

(a)

kuva mupangi mukuru wemazano ezvemitemo kuHurumende;

(b) kumiririra Hurumende munyaya dzine chekuita nevanhu uye neBumbiro reMitemo;

(c)

kuumba zvinyorwa zvemitemo akamirira Hurumende;

(d) kukurudzira, kuchengetedza nekukoshesa kutevedzwa kwemitemo munyika nekuchengetedza zvido zveruzhinji rwevanhu; uye

(e)

kuita mamwe mabasa anogona kupihwa kuGweta Guru reHurumende neMutemo weParamende;

uye Gweta Guru reHurumende rinogona kuita mabasa aya iro pacharo kana kuburikidza nevamwe vanoshanda vachitevedza zvavanenge vanzi vaite neGweta Guru reHurumende.

(5) Gweta Guru reHurumende rinogona—

(a)

kupinda mumisangano yeDare reMakurukota eHurumende, asi harivhoti;

(b) kugara nekutaura muSeneti nemuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, asi harivhoti; uye

(c)

richibvumidzwa nedare remitemo rinenge richitonga nyaya, kumira seshamwari yedare iri mukutongwa kwemhosva chero ipi zvayo ine chekuita nevanhu, asi isinei neHurumende.

115 Kubviswa pachinzvimbo kweGweta Guru reHurumende Mutungamiriri weNyika anokwanisa kubvisa Gweta Guru reHurumende pachinzvimbo chero nguva ipi zvayo.

CHITSAUKO 6 Dare Revanodzika Mitemo GANHURO 1 Masimba Ekudzika Mitemo 116 Vanodzika Mitemo Vanodzika Mitemo yemuZimbabwe vanosanganisira Paramende neMutungamiriri weNyika uye vachishanda sezvakatarwa muChitsauko chino. 117 Mamiriro nemashandiro emasimba ekudzika mitemo

(1) Masimba ekudzika mitemo muZimbabwe anobva muvanhu uye anoshandiswa neVanodzika Mitemo kuchitevedzwa zviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(2) Masimba ekudzika mitemo anopa Vanodzika Mitemo simba—

(a)

rekushandura Bumbiro reMitemo rino zvichienderana nezviri muchikamu 328;

(b) rekudzika mitemo yekuti Zimbabwe ive nerunyararo, irongeke uye itungamirirwe zvakanaka; uye

(c)

rekupawo mamwe masimba ekudzika mitemo kune rimwewo boka riri pasi paro zvichienderana nezviri muchikamu 134. GANHURO 2 Paramende

118 Paramende Paramende inoumbwa neSeneti uye Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika. 119 Basa reParamende

(1) Paramende inofanira kuchengetedza Bumbiro reMitemo rino nekukurudzira Utungamiriri husina udzvanyiriri muZimbabwe.

(2) Paramende ine simba rekuona kuti mitemo iri muBumbiro reMitemo rino yakosheswa uye kuti mapoka ese nevamiririri vese veNyika neHurumende vanoita basa ravo pachitevedzwa zviri muBumbiro reMitemo uye zvido zvenyika.

(3) Takatarisana nechikamu chidiki (2), mapoka ese uye vamiririri vese padanho chero ripi zvaro, vari pasi peParamende. GANHURO 3 Seneti 120 Nhengo dzeSeneti

(1) Seneti inoumbwa nemaSeneta makumi masere uye pamaSeneta aya—

(a)

vatanhatu vanosarudzwa kubva kudunhu guru rimwe nerimwe reZimbabwe zvichienderana nekuti vanhu vanomiririrwa vakawanda sei uye kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (2);

(b) gumi nevatanhatu madzishe, madzishe maviri acho anosarudzwa neMatare eMadzishe ekumatunhu makuru emuZimbabwe asiri emumaguta;

(c)

Mutungamiriri nemutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weDare reMadzishe ese emunyika;

(d) vaviri vacho vanosarudzwa nenzira yakadzikwa neMutemo weSarudzo kuti vamiririre vakaremara.

(2) Sarudzo dzemaSeneta dzinofanira kuitwa nenzira inotevedza zviri muMutemo weSarudzo, uyo unofanira kuona kuti maSeneta anotaurwa nezvawo muchikamu chidiki

(1)(a) asarudzwa pachishandiswa mazita evanhu vanomiririra mapato akasiyana-siyana nenzira yekuti uwandu hwevamiririri huenderane nevanomiririrwa—

(a)

inoshandisa uwandu hwemavhoti anenge awanikwa nevanhu vanomiririra mapato ezvematongerwo enyika mudunhu guru rimwe nerimwe musarudzo huru yenhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika; uye

(b) apo mazita evanhurume nevanhukadzi vanenge vachikwikwidza musarudzo achipesaniswa uye gwaro rimwe nerimwe remazita richitanga nezita remunhukadzi. 121 Zvinodikanwa kana zvinotadzisa munhu kuti asarudzwe kuva Seneta

(1) Munhu anokodzera kusarudzwa saSeneta atsanangurwa muchikamu 120(1)

(a) kana kuti (d) kana—

(a)

akanyoresa semuvhoti; uye

(b) ane makore ekuberekwa anosvika makumi mana kana kupfuura; kunze kwekunge atadziswa nezviri muchikamu chidiki (4) kana (5).

(2) Munhu anokodzera kusarudzwa saIshe anopinda muSeneti, uyo anotsanangurwa muchikamu 120(1)(b) kana—

(a)

ari Ishe; uye

(b) akanyoresa semuvhoti; kunze kwekunge atadziswa nezviri muchikamu chidiki (4) kana (5).

(3) Munhu anokodzera kusarudzwa saSeneta atsanangurwa muchikamu 120(1)

(d) kana akaremara sezvinotsanangurwa muMutemo weSarudzo, kunze kwekunge atadziswa nezviri muchikamu chidiki (4) kana (5).

(4) Munhu anorambidzwa kukwikwidza musarudzo dzemaSeneta kana—

(a)

achitadziswa nezviri muNhamatidzwa 4 kunyoresa kuva muvhoti; kana kuti

(b) mumakore mashanu sarudzo dzisati dzaitwa, akambosiya chinzvimbo chemuSeneti kana chemuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, kuchitevedzwa chikamu 129(1)(i) mushure mekunge awanikwa ane mhosva.

(5) Munhu anorambidzwa kukwikwidza musarudzo yekutsiva Seneta kana ari nhengo yeParamende. 122 Mutungamiriri weSeneti

(1) Pamusangano wayo wekutanga zvichitevera kuitwa kwesarudzo huru uye isati yamboita chimwe chinhu, Seneti inofanira kusarudza munhu anoona nezvemafambisirwo ebasa muSeneti anozivikanwa seMutungamiriri weSeneti.

(2) Pese zvapo panenge chinzvimbo cheMutungamiriri weSeneti chasara chisina munhu, Seneti inofanira kukurumidza kusarudza mumwe munhu kuti aende pachinzvimbo ichi.

(3) Munhu anokodzera kukwikwidza musarudzo yekuva Mutungamiriri weSeneti kana achitova, kana kuti akambova, Seneta kana kuti, achikodzera kusarudzwa saSeneta.

(4) Sarudzo dzeMutungamiriri weSeneti dzinofanira kuitiswa neMunyori weParamende achitungamirirwa neKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe, pachiitwa sarudzo dzakavanzika uye pachitevedzwa Mitemo Inotevedzwa, uye zvinenge zvabuda musarudzo zvinofanira kuziviswa kuvanhu chiriporipocho.

(5) Asati atanga basa rake, Mutungamiriri weSeneti anofanira kuita mhiko dzekuti achatevedzera mitemo uye achaita basa rake nemazvo pamberi peMutungamiriri wevatongi kana mumwe mutongi mukuru anenge aripo, pachitevedzwa zvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(6) Seneta anosarudzwa kuva Mutungamiriri weSeneti anobva angosiya chinzvimbo chaSeneta uye chinzvimbo ichi chinofanira kuiswa imwe nhengo pachitevedzwa Mutemo weSarudzo.

(7) Mutungamiriri weSeneti anokwanisa kusiya chinzvimbo chake kuburikidza nekuzivisa Seneti nemuromo wake kana kuti, kana Seneti isiri kusangana, kuburikidza nekunyora tsamba yeziviso kuMunyori weParamende.

(8) Mutungamiriri weSeneti anofanira kusiya chinzvimbo chake—

(a)

zuva rinosangana Seneti kekutanga mushure mesarudzo huru;

(b) paanongobvuma kutora rimwe basa muhurumende kana kushandira mumwe munhuwo zvake;

(c)

paanongoita nhengo yeParamende kana Mutauriri;

(d) paanongoita Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende;

(e)

kana pakaitika chiitiko chinoita kuti afanire kusiya chinzvimbo chake, kana akange ambori Seneta; uye

(f)

kana pfungwa yekuti abve pachinzvimbo chake yatambirwa nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzeSeneti.

123 Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti

(1) Nekukurumidza kunogoneka zvichitevera kusarudzwa kweMutungamiriri weSeneti mushure mekuitwa kwesarudzo huru, Seneti inofanira kusarudza mumwe Seneta kuti ave Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti.

(2) Pese zvapo panenge chinzvimbo cheMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti chasara chisina munhu, Seneti inofanira kukurumidza kusarudza munhu kuti aende pachinzvimbo ichi.

(3) Sarudzo dzeMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti dzinofanira kuitiswa neMunyori weParamende achitungamirirwa neKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe, pachiitwa sarudzo dzakavanzika uye pachitevedzwa Mitemo Inotevedzwa, uye zvinenge zvabuda musarudzo zvinofanira kuziviswa kuvanhu chiriporipocho.

(4) Asati atanga basa rake, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti anofanira kuita mhiko dzekuti achatevedzera mitemo uye achaita basa rake nemazvo pamberi peMutungamiriri wevatongi kana mumwe mutongi mukuru anenge aripo, pachitevedzwa zvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(5) Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti anokwanisa kusiya chinzvimbo chake kuburikidza nekuzivisa Seneti nemuromo wake kana kuti, kana Seneti isiri kusangana, kuburikidza nekunyora tsamba yeziviso kuMutungamiriri weSeneti uye kana asipo, kuMunyori weParamende.

(6) Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti anofanira kusiya chinzvimbo chake—

(a)

paanongoregedza kuva Seneta;

(b) paanongoita Mutauriri;

(c)

paanongoita Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri, Gurukota reHurumende, kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende;

(d) kana pfungwa yekuti abve pachinzvimbo chake yatambirwa nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzeSeneti. GANHURO 4 Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika 124 Nhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika

(1) Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika rinoumbwa—

(a)

nenhengo mazana maviri negumi dzinosarudzwa kuburikidza nesarudzo yakavanzika kubva munharaunda dzesarudzo mazana maviri negumi dzemuZimbabwe; uye

(b) PamaParamende maviri ekutanga kubva pazuva rinotaurwa nezvaro, nhengo dzevanhukadzi makumi matanhatu dzinowedzerwa, nhanhatu kubva kudunhu guru rimwe nerimwe muZimbabwe, dzinosarudzwa pachishandiswa uwandu hwevanhu vanomiririrwa zvichitevera uwandu hwemavhoti anenge awanikwa nevanhu vanomiririra mapato ezvematongerwo enyika musarudzo dzenhengo dzenzvimbo dzekuvhotera mumatunhu enyika.

(2) Sarudzo dzenhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika dzinofanira kuitwa pachitevedzwa Mutemo weSarudzo.

(3) Zvinodikanwa pakuti munhu agone kunyoresa kuti akwanise kuvhota uye kuti avhote pasarudzo dzenhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zviri muNhamatidzwa 4. 125 Zvinodikanwa kana zvinotadzisa munhu kuti asarudzwe senhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika

(1) Munhu anokodzera kusarudzwa senhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kana—

(a)

akanyoresa semuvhoti; uye

(b) ane makore ekuberekwa makumi maviri nerimwe kana kupfuura; kunze kwekunge arambidzwa zvinoenderana nezviri muchikamu chidiki (2) kana (3).

(2) Munhu anorambidzwa kukwikwidza musarudzo dzenhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kana—

(a)

achitadziswa neNhamatidzwa 4 kunyoresa kuva muvhoti; kana kuti

(b) mumakore mashanu sarudzo dzisati dzaitwa, akambosiya chinzvimbo chemuSeneti kana chemuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kuchitevedzwa chikamu 129(1)(i) mushure mekunge awanikwa ane mhosva.

(3) Munhu anorambidzwa kukwikwidza musarudzo yekutsiva nhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kana ari nhengo yeParamende. 126 Mutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika

(1) Pamusangano waro wekutanga zvichitevera kuitwa kwesarudzo huru, risati ramboita chimwe chinhu, Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika rinofanira kusarudza munhu anoona nezvemafambisirwo ebasa achazivikanwa seMutauriri.

(2) Pese zvapo panenge chinzvimbo cheMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika chasara chisina munhu, boka iri rinofanira kukurumidza kusarudza munhu kuti aende pachinzvimbo ichi.

(3) Munhu anokodzera kukwikwidza musarudzo yekuva Mutauriri kana achitova kana kuti akambova nhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kana kuti achikodzera kusarudzwa kuti ave nhengo yeDare iri.

(4) Sarudzo dzeMutauriri dzinofanira kuitiswa neMunyori weParamende achitungamirirwa neKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe, pachiitwa sarudzo dzakavanzika uye pachitevedzwa Mitemo Inotevedzwa, uye zvinenge zvabuda musarudzo zvinofanira kuziviswa kuvanhu chiriporipocho.

(5) Asati atanga basa rake, Mutauriri anofanira kuita mhiko dzekuti achatevedzera mitemo uye achaita basa rake nemazvo pamberi peMukuru wevatongi kana kana mumwe mutongi mukuru anenge aripo, pachitevedzwa zvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(6) Nhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika inosarudzwa kuva Mutauriri inobva yangosiya chinzvimbo chayo muDare iri uye chinzvimbo ichi chinofanira kuiswa imwe nhengo pachitevedzwa Mutemo weSarudzo.

(7) Mutauriri anokwanisa kusiya chinzvimbo chake kuburikidza nekuzivisa Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika nemuromo wake kana kuti, kana Dare iri risiri kusangana, anofanira kunyora tsamba yeziviso kuMutauriri weParamende, kana Mutauriri weParamende achinge asipo anoendesa tsamba iyi kuMunyori weParamende.

(8) Mutauriri anofanira kusiya chinzvimbo chake—

(a)

zuva rinosangana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kekutanga mushure mesarudzo huru;

(b) paanongobvuma kutora rimwe basa muhurumende kana kushandira mumwe munhuwo zvake;

(c)

paanongoita nhengo yeParamende kana Mutungamiriri weSeneti;

(d) paanongoita Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri, Gurukota reHurumende, kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende;

(e)

kana pakaitika chiitiko chinoita kuti afanire kusiya chinzvimbo chake, kana akange ambori nhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika; uye

(f)

kana pfungwa yekuti abve pachinzvimbo chake yatambirwa nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

127 Mutevedzeri weMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika

(1) Nekukurumidza kunogoneka zvichitevera kusarudzwa kweMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, mushure mekuitwa kwesarudzo huru, Dare iri rinofanira kusarudza imwe nhengo yaro kuti ive Mutevedzeri weMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(2) Pese zvapo panenge chinzvimbo chemutevedzeri weMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika chasara chisina munhu, Dare iri rinofanira kukurumidza kusarudza munhu kuti aende pachinzvimbo ichi.

(3) Sarudzo dzeMutevedzeri weMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika dzinofanira kuitiswa neMunyori weParamende achitungamirirwa neKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe, pachiitwa sarudzo dzakavanzika uye pachitevedzwa Mitemo Inotevedzwa, uye zvinenge zvabuda musarudzo zvinofanira kuziviswa kuvanhu chiriporipocho.

(4) Asati atanga basa rake, Mutevedzeri weMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika anofanira kuita mhiko dzekuti achatevedzera mitemo uye achaita basa rake nemazvo pamberi peMukuru wevatongi kana mumwe mutongi mukuru anenge aripo, pachitevedzwa zvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(5) Mutevedzeri weMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika anokwanisa kusiya chinzvimbo chake kuburikidza nekuzivisa Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika nemuromo wake kana kuti, kana Dare iri risiri kusangana, anofanira kunyora tsamba yeziviso kuMunyori weParamende.

(6) Mutevedzeri weMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika anofanira kusiya chinzvimbo chake—

(a)

paanongoregedza kuva nhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika iri;

(b) paanongoita Mutungamiriri weSeneti;

(c)

paanongoita Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri, Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende;

(d) kana pfungwa yekuti abve pachinzvimbo chake yatambirwa nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

GANHURO 5 Urefu hwenguva yekuva pachinzvimbo kwenhengo dzeparamende 128 Mhiko yenhengo yeParamende

(1) Isati yaenda pachinzvimbo chemuParamende, nhengo yeParamende inofanira kuita mhiko yenhengo yeParamende pachitevedzerwa zvakanyorwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(2) Mhiko iri kutaurwa nezvayo muchikamu chidiki (1) inofanira kuitirwa pamberi peMunyori weParamende. 129 Urefu hweNguva yekuva pachinzvimbo kwenhengo yeParamende

(1) Chinzvimbo chenhengo yeParamende chinosara chisina munhu—

(a)

kana Paramende yaparadzwa;

(b) kana nhengo yeParamende yasiya basa mushure mekuzivisa Mutungamiriri weSeneti kana Mutauriri kuburikidza netsamba yeziviso;

(c)

kana nhengo yeParamende ikava Mutungamiriri kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri;

(d) kana nhengo yeParamende ikava Mutungamiriri weSeneti kana Mutauriri kana kuti nhengo yechimwewo Chikamu cheParamende;

(e)

kana nhengo yeParamende isingachakwanisi kunyoresa semuvhoti;

(f)

kana nhengo yeParamende isina kuuya kumisangano makumi maviri nemumwe yakatevedzana apo chikamu chayo cheParamende chichisangana asi isina kubvumidzwa kusavapo neMutauriri kana Mutungamiriri weSeneti, uye nhengo dzine uwandu hunopfuura hwepakati nepakati dzavhota dzichiwirirana kuti chinzvimbo chinzi hachisisina munhu;

(g) kana nhengo yeParamende yabvuma kushandira hurumende semumwe weboka rinoona nezvekugadzirwa kwemitemo, yava kushandira bazi chero ripi zvaro riri pasi pehurumende, kanzuru yedunhu guru kana yeguta kana kanzuru yekumaruwa kana kuti yava mushandi wedare rinoona nezvekugadzirwa kwemitemo, wekanzuru yedunhu guru kana yeguta, kana yekumaruwa;

(h) kana nhengo yeParamende yaimboshandira hurumende, kana kuti yaiva nhengo kana mushandi wedare rinoona nezvekugadzirwa kwemitemo, bazi chero ripi zvaro riri pasi pehurumende, kanzuru yedunhu guru kana yeguta kana kanzuru yekumaruwa payakaziviswa senhengo yeParamende, uye yotadza kusiya chinzvimbo chayo kana basa kana unhengo hwayo mukati memazuva makumi matatu ekusarudzwa kuva nhengo yeParamende;

(i)

kana nhengo yeParamende yawanikwa—

(i) muZimbabwe, ine mhosva ine chekuita nekutyora chivimbo, kusavimbika kana mhirizhonga nekurwisana; kana kuti

(ii) kunze kwenyika yeZimbabwe, ine mhosva yekuti ikaparwa muZimbabwe, inenge iri mhosva ine chekuita nekutyora chivimbo, kusavimbika kana mhirizhonga nekurwisana; uye yatemerwa mutongo wekugara mujeri kwemwedzi mitanhatu kana kupfuura pasina mukana wekubhadhara faindi kana wemumwe murango usiri wekugara mujeri, kunze

kwekunge mushure mekupikisa, mhosva yaraswa kana kuti mutongo wekugara mujeri waderedzwa kuva pasi pemwedzi mitanhatu, kana kuti patemwa mumwewo mutongo wekusagara mujeri;

(j)

kana nhengo yeParamende yanzi haisisina mari zvachose nemutemo unoshanda muZimbabwe uye pasina zvaitwa kuti ibatsirwe, kana kuti iyo yashandisa mutemo iwoyo kune vanhu vaine zvikwereti zvavo, zvotadza kupikiswa;

(k) kana nhengo yeParamende isisiri nhengo yebato rezvematongerwo enyika rayaimbova payakasarudzwa kuva nhengo yeParamende uye bato rezvematongerwo enyika racho ranyorera Mutauriri kana Mutungamiriri weSeneti richimuzivisa kuti nhengo iyi haisisiri nhengo yebato iri;

(l)

kana nhengo yeParamende yaisambova nhengo yebato rezvematongerwo enyika payakasarudzwa kuva nhengo yeParamende, asi iye zvino yava nhengo yerimwe bato rezvematongerwo enyika;

(m) kana nhengo yeParamende yaonekwa kuti yakanganisika pfungwa uye haichanyatsofunga zvakanaka nemutemo chero upi zvawo unoshanda muZimbabwe; kana kuti

(n) kana nhengo yeParamende yawanikwa ine mhosva pasi peMutemo weSarudzo uye Dare repamusoro ratonga kuti nhengo iyi haichakwanisi kunyoresa kuti ivhote kana kuvhota musarudzo chero ipi zvayo.

(2) Nhengo yeParamende inotaurwa nezvayo muchikamu chidiki (1)(i) inenge ichipikisa mutongo wayo, inokwanisa kuramba ichiita basa rayo nekuwana mubhadharo senhengo yeParamende, kusvikira zvazonyatsoonekwa kuti ine mhosva here kana kuti kwete, kunze kwekunge dare redzimhosva ratonga kuti inofanira kuvharirwa muchitokisi apo kupikisa kwayo kunenge kuchimboongororwa.

(3) Nhengo yeParamende inosiya chinzvimbo chemuParamende kana ikaitwa sachigaro weKanzuru yedunhu guru. GANHURO 6 Masimba ekudzika Mitemo neezvimwewo 130 Masimba nemabasa eSeneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika

(1) Kunze kwezvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 5, pakushandisa masimba ekudzika mitemo, Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvine simba rekuparura, kugadzira, kuongorora nekuramba mutemo chero upi zvawo.

(2) Pamusoro pemabasa ari muBumbiro reMitemo rino, Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvinokwanisa kuita mamwe mabasa azvinopihwa nemutemo chero upi zvawo. 131 Mitemo yeParamende nekudzikwa kwayo

(1) Masimba eParamende ekudzika mitemo anoshandiswa kuburikidza nekudzika Mitemo yeParamende.

(2) Mutemo weParamende iBhiri reMutemo —

(a)

rakapirwa uye rikapasiswa neZvikamu zviviri zveParamende; uye

(b) rikabvumwa nekusainwa neMutungamiriri weNyika; kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino.

(3) Mashoko anoshandiswa pakudzika Mitemo yeParamende ndeanoti “Wakadzikwa neParamende neMutungamiriri weZimbabwe”, kana mamwewo mashoko anoreva zvimwe chete neizvi.

(4) Zvinofanira kutevedzwa neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika neSeneti maererano neMabhiri eMitemo zvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 5.

(5) Mushure mekunge Bhiri reMutemo ratambirwa neZvikamu zviviri zveParamende sezvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 5, Mutungamiriri weSeneti kana kuti Mutauriri anofanira kukurumidza—

(a)

kuita kuti riendeswe kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuti aritambire nekurisaina, uye rinoenda pamwe chete nechitupa chinonzi neBumbiro reMitemo rino chinofanira kunge chiri pamwe chete neBhiri reMutemo iri; uye

(b) kuzivisa ruzhinji rwevanhu zuva rakaendeswa Bhiri reMutemo iri kuMutungamiriri weNyika.

(6) Kana Bhiri reMutemo raendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuti aritambire nekurisaina, anofanira kuita chimwe chete chezvinotevera mukati memazuva makumi maviri nerimwe—

(a)

kuritambira nekurisaina, oita kuti riburitswe muGazette nekukurumidza; kana kuti

(b) kana achiona sekuti harienderani nezvakatarwa muBumbiro reMitemo kana kuti pane zvimwe zvaasina kugutsikana nazvo, anodzorera Bhiri reMutemo iri kuParamende kuburikidza neMunyori weParamende, pamwe chete nezvikonzero zvizere zvakanyorwa zvekutsanangura kuti sei asingagutsikani neBhiri reMutemo iri pamwe chete nechikumbiro chekuti Bhiri reMutemo iri ritariswe zvakare.

(7) Kana Bhiri reMutemo radzoserwa kuParamende kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (6)(b), Mutauriri anofanira kukurumidza kushevedzera musangano weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, iro rinofanira—

(a)

kutarisa zvakare mutemo uyu, uye kunyatsoongorora zvese zvisina kugutsa Mutungamiriri weNyika; kana

(b) kupasisa Bhiri reMutemo iri rine, kana kuti risina, zvinoda kugadziriswa, izvi zvichiitwa nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika; uye pane chero chipi zvacho chinenge chaitika, Mutauriri anofanira kuita kuti Bhiri reMutemo iri riendeswe kuMutungamiriri weNyika nekukurumidza kuti agoritambira nekurisaina uye Mutauriri anofanira kuzivisa ruzhinji rwevanhu zuva rakaendeswa Bhiri reMutemo iri kuMutungamiriri weNyika.

(8) Kana Bhiri reMutemo raendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (7) uye ragadziriswa, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuritambira nekurisaina mukati memazuva makumi maviri nerimwe, oita kuti riburitswe muGazette nekukurumidza asi kana Mutungamiriri weNyika asati agutsikana naro, anofanira kuita chimwe chete chezvinotevera mukati menguva iyoyo—

(a)

kutambira nekusaina Bhiri reMutemo iri zvisinei nekuti haasi kugutsikana naro; kana

(b) kuendesa Bhiri reMutemo iri kuDare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kuti rione kuti Bhiri reMutemo iri rinoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo here.

(9) Kana Bhiri reMutemo raendeswa kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (8) uye dare iri raona kuti Bhiri reMutemo iri rinoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuritambira nekurisaina chiriporipocho, oita kuti riburitswe muGazette nekukurumidza.

(10) Kana Bhiri reMutemo rikaendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuti aritambire nekurisaina asi risina chitupa chinodikanwa kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, Mutungamiriri weNyika haafaniri kutambira nekusaina Bhiri reMutemo iri kusvikira chitupa ichi chavapo asi anofanira kuita kuti Munyori weParamende aziviswe netsamba chiriporipocho, kuti chitupa hachina kutumirwa pamwe chete neBhiri reMutemo. 132 Kutanga kushanda kweMitemo yeParamende Mutemo weParamende unotanga kushanda panotangira zuva raunoburitswa muGazette kana panotangira zuva rimwewo rinofanira kunyatsotaurwa muMutemo wacho kana zvimwewo zvinotsanangura mashandiro eMitemo yeParamende. 133 Kunyoreswa kweMitemo yeParamende

(1) Kana Mutungamiriri weNyika atambira uye asaina Mutemo weParamende, Munyori weParamende anofanira kuendesa rimwe gwaro rakafanana, remutemo uyu, rine siginecha yeMutungamiriri weNyika uye rine chidhindo chenyika yeZimbabwe, kuti unyoreswe kuhofisi yeMunyori weDare rePamusoro, uye rimwe gwaro rakafanana, remutemo uyu richitova umboo huzere hwezvinotsanangurwa nemutemo iwoyo, kunze kwekunge Mutemo wacho washandurwa neMutemo weParamende sezvinotsanangurwa muchikamu chidiki (2).

(2) Mutemo weParamende unokwanisa kushandiswa pakuita kuti mutemo wakanyorwa, kana kuti chikamu chawo, chitsikiswe patsva kana kunatsurudzwa uye unogonazve kutaura kuti—

(a)

paunotsikiswa, mutemo wakanatsurudzwa uyu ndiwo chete wechokwadi unotsanangura zviri mauri;

(b) rimwe gwaro rakafanana, remutemo uyu unenge wanatsurudzwa, rinofanira kuendeswa kuhofisi yeMunyori weDare reDzimhosva rePamusoro; uye

(c)

rimwe gwaro rakafanana, remutemo uyu rinoendeswa kuhofisi yeMunyori weDare reDzimhosva rePamusoro, rinova umboo huzere hwezvinotsanangurwa nemutemo iwoyo.

(3) Kushanda kweMutemo weParamende kana kunatsurudzwa kwemutemo wakanyorwa hakumiriri kunyoreswa kwemutemo kana kuendeswa kwawo kuhofisi yeMunyori weDare rePamusoro. 134 Mimwe mitemo midiki MuMutemo weParamende, Paramende inokwanisa kupa vamwewo masimba ekuti pagadzirwe tumitemo tunoenderana nezvakadzikwa muMutemo weParamende iwoyo, asi—

(a)

simba reParamende zhinji rekugadzira mitemo harifaniri kupihwa vamwe;

(b) tumitemo tunogadzirwa hatufaniri kukanganisa kana kushomesa kodzero chero dzipi zvadzo kana rusununguko rupi zvarwo rwunotsanangurwa muKuziviswa kweKodzero dzevanhu;

(c)

tumitemo utwu tunofanira kunge tuchienderana nezviri muMutemo weParamende unenge waita kuti tugadzirwe;

(d) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kunyatsotsanangura panogumira masimba ekamutemo kadiki, mhando yekamutemo kanokwanisa kugadzirwa uye zvinotarisirwa kunge zviri mukamutemo ikako;

(e)

tumitemo tunogadzirwa utwu hatuna simba semitemo kunze kwekunge twatsikiswa muGazette; uye

(f)

tumitemo tunogadzirwa tunofanira kuendeswa kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvichienderana neMitemo Inotevedzwa uye kuKomiti yeParamende inoona nezvemitemo kuti twuongororwe. GANHURO 7 Zvinofanira Kutevedzwa MuParamende

135 Mutungamiriri weParamende

(1) Mutauriri weParamende ndiye mutungamiriri weParamende asi anofanira kuita basa rake achitevedza Mitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(2) Zvichienderana neMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa, Mutungamiriri weSeneti ndiye Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weParamende uye ndiye anofanobata chinzvimbo kana Mutauriri asipo nekuda kwechikonzero chero chipi zvacho. 136 Vatungamiriri vemisangano yeParamende

(1) Munhu anotungamirira musangano chero upi zvawo weSeneti anofanira kunge ari—

(a)

Mutungamiriri weSeneti kana kuti paanenge asipo, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti; kana kuti

(b) kana Mutungamiriri weSeneti neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti vasipo, Seneta anenge asarudzwa neSeneti, asi Seneta iyeye haafaniri kunge ari Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende.

(2) Munhu anotungamirira musangano chero upi zvawo weDare rinodzika mitemo yenyika anofanira kunge ari—

(a)

Mutauriri uye, kana asipo, Mutevedzeri weMutauriri; kana kuti

(b) kana Mutauriri neMutevedzeri weMutauriri vasipo, nhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, asi nhengo iyoyo haifaniri kunge iri Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende.

(3) Mutauriri, kana kuti paanenge asipo, Mutungamiriri weSeneti, anofanira kutungamirira musangano chero upi zvavo unobatanidza Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika neSeneti. 137 Uwandu hwenhengo dzeParamende dzinogona kuita musangano Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvinofanira kudzika muMitemo inofanira kutevedzerwa, uwandu hwenhengo dzinofanira kunge dziripo kuti musangano uitwe. 138 Kuvhota uye kodzero yekuteererwa muParamende

(1) Kusiya kweapo Bumbiro reMitemo rino rinotaura zvimwewo—

(a)

2013

mibvunzo yese inonzi iongororwe mudare chero ripi zvaro reParamende inopasiswa kana kusapasiswa nekuda kweuwandu hwemavhoti enhengo dzedare iroro dzinenge dziripo uye dzinenge dzichivhota;

(b) anenge achitungamirira musangano mudare chero ripi zvaro reParamende haana masimba ekutonga pane zvinenge zvabuda pakuvhota kana masimba ekuvhota pachinhu chero chipi zvacho chinenge chichivhoterwa;

(c)

kana mavhoti mudare chero ripi zvaro reParamende akaenzana pakuwirirana nekusawirirana papfungwa inenge ichivhoterwa, pfungwa iyoyo inobva yangoraswa.

(2) Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vanobvumirwa kugara uye kutaura mumisangano yematare ese maviri eParamende asi havabvumirwi kuvhota.

(3) Makurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende vanobvumirwa kugara uye kutaura mumisangano yematare ese maviri eParamende asi havana kodzero yekuvhota mudare ravasiri nhengo.

(4) Nhengo dzeKomiti yeParamende inoona nezvemitemo kana dzemamwewo makomiti emubatanidzwa eParamende dzinobvumirwa kugara uye kutaura mumisangano yematare ese maviri eParamende nechinangwa chekuparura kana kuita hurukuro pamusoro pechinyorwa chekomiti iyoyo chinenge chichitariswa nedare reParamende, asi havana kodzero yekuvhota muChikamu chavasiri nhengo.
