(5) Kana nhengo yeParamende, kunze kweGurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende azivisa nezveBhiri reMutemo mudare reParamende raari nhengo, uye dare iri ratambira Bhiri reMutemo iri, nhengo iyi, kana kuti painenge isipo, imwe nhengo yeDare iroro, inobvumirwa kugara uye kutaura mumisangano yerimwe dare reParamende nechinangwa chekuparura Bhiri reMutemo uyu kune rimwe Dare iri, asi haina kodzero yekuvhota muDare iroro. 139 Mitemo inofanira kutevedzwa

(1) Zvese zvinoitwa neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika neSeneti zvinotevedza mirawo inozivikanwa seMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa, iyo inogadzirwa neMatare eParamende, akabatana kana kuti asina, zvichitevera kurudziro yeKomiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(2) Mitemo inofanira kutevedzwa iyi inokwanisa kushanda pa—

(a)

kupasisa maBhiri eMitemo;

(b) kusarudza makomiti uye kuazivisa kuti anoshanda sei;

(c)

kutaura kuti masimba, zvinobvumirwa uye rusununguko rwematare eParamende zvinoshanda nekumira sei;

(d) kuti nhengo dzeParamende dzibvunzurudze Makurukota eHurumende neVatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende;

(e)

kuzivisa gwara remitemo inofanira kutevedzerwa nenhengo dzeParamende;

(f)

kuzivisa mashandiro ekodzero yeruzhinji rwevanhu yekutura gwaro rekutsutsumwa kuParamende; uye

(g) kungotaura mafambisirwo ebasa ari mugwara anofanira kuitwa nematare maviri eParamende.

(3) Zvese zvinotevedzwa nezvinoitika muParamende nemakomiti ayo, izvo zvakatarwa muMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa, zvinofanira kukurudzira kuitira zvinhu pachena, kupinda muzviitiko zveParamende kweruzhinji rwevanhu nekwenhengo dzemapato ezvematongerwo enyika ese, uye zvinofanira kunge zvine ruenzaniso.

(4) Komiti chero ipi zvayo inoumbwa kana kuti iri pasi peMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa inofanira kunyatsoratidza mamiriro eParamende kana eDare reParamende maererano nemapato ezvematongerwo enyika arimo neuwandu hwevarume nevakadzi varimo. 140 Kutaura pamberi peParamende kweMutungamiriri weNyika nemashoko ake kuParamende

(1) Panguva chero ipi zvayo, Mutungamiriri weNyika anogona kutaura pamberi perimwe reMatare eParamende, kana kuti muMatare aya ari pamwe chete.

(2) Mutungamiriri weNyika anogona kutumira mashoko kuMatare maviri eParamende uye mashoko ake anofanira kuverengwa neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana Gurukota reHurumende kana mumwewo munhu ari kutungamirira musangano muchikamu ichocho.

(3) Mutungamiriri weNyika anogona kupinda muParamende kuti apindure mibvunzo pamusoro pechinhu chero chipi zvacho sezvakatarwa muMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(4) Kamwe chete kana kupfuura pagore, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kutaura pamberi pemubatanidzwa weMatare maviri eParamende pamusoro pemamiriro ezvinhu munyika uye Mutauriri neMutungamiriri weSeneti vanofanira kuronga kuti Paramende izoteerera mashoko eMutungamiriri weNyika aya. 141 Kuwanisa ruzhinji rwevanhu mukana uye kuti vave nechekuita muParamende Paramende inofanira—

(a)

kuita kuti ruzhinji rwevanhu rwuvewo nechekuita painogadzira mitemo uye pane zvimwewo zvainoita kana zvinoitwa nemakomiti ayo;

(b) kuona kuti vanhu vane chekuita nemaBhiri eMitemo vapa pfungwa dzavo pamusoro pemaBhiri aya, kunze kwekunge izvi zvisina kukodzera kana kuti zvisingakwanisiki.

(c)

kuita basa rayo nenzira yakajeka uye kuita misangano yayo, neyemakomiti ayo ruzhinji rwevanhu rwuripo kunyangwe zvazvo matanho achikwanisa kutorwa—

(i) pakuita kuti zviitiko zveParamende zvifambe zvakanaka;

(ii) pakumbomisa ruzhinji rwevanhu, kusanganisira venhau kupinda muParamende kana kutaura nemakomiti ayo;

(iii) pakusadaidza ruzhinji rwevanhu, kusanganisira venhau kumisangano yemakomiti; uye

(iv) pakuita kuti munhu chero upi zvake asechwe uye, pazvakakodzera, kurambidza munhu kupinda muParamende kana kuburitsa munhu muParamende; asi zvese izvi zvichiitwa nenzira yakaenzanirana, yakakodzera uye inotambirika munyika inoitira zvinhu pachena, ine ruenzaniso uye inotevedza zvido zveruzhinji rwevanhu. 142 Kukosha kwezviitiko zveParamende

(1) Chinzvimbo chinenge chisina munhu chemuSeneti kana muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, kana kumbomiswa basa kwenhengo yeParamende, hazvitadzisi Seneti kana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kuita basa.

(2) Kunyange munhu akange asina kodzero yekuve muSeneti kana muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika akapinda uye akavhota, kana kuti akataura nekuteerera, izvi hazvikanganisi basa rinenge richiitwa.

GANHURO 8 Urefu hweNguva, Kuparadzwa uye Misangano yeParamende 143 Urefu hwenguva uye kuparadzwa kweParamende

(1) Paramende inosarudzwa kuti ishande kwemakore mashanu, ayo anobvira pazuva rekugadzwa nekutanga basa kweMutungamiriri weNyika kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki 94(1)(a) uye Paramende inenge yakaparadzwa kubvira pakati peusiku hwezuva riri mumashure mekutanga kwesarudzo huru dzinenge dzanzi dziitwe zvichitevera chikamu chidiki 144.

(2) Mutungamiriri weNyika, kuburikidza nekuzivisa ruzhinji rwevanhu, anofanira kuparadza Paramende kana Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika pazvinenge zvichisangana panguva dzakasiyana, uye kana zvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzeDare rimwe nerimwe dzavhota dzichiti Paramende iparadzwe.

(3) Mutungamiriri weNyika, kuburikidza nekuzivisa ruzhinji rwevanhu, anokwanisa kuparadza Paramende kana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika raramba kupasisa Bhiri reMutemo wekugoverwa kwemari rinotaurwa nezvaro muchikamu 305 pasina kupa chikonzero chinonzwisisika.

(4) Pfungwa yekuparadza Paramende pachitevedzwa zviri muchikamu chidiki

(3) inogona kumbomiswa kuti iongororwe neDare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, kana nhengo chero ipi zvayo yeParamende yanyorera kuti dare iri riite izvozvo.

(5) Kunyorera kwakanangana nekuti pfungwa yekuparadza Paramende iongororwe neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kunofanira kuitwa mukati memazuva manomwe mushure mekunge pfungwa iyi yaziviswa, uye—

(a)

Dare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rinofanira kupa mutongo mukati memazuva gumi nemana mekutambira chikumbiro ichi; uye

(b) panguva yekumirira mutongo weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, pfungwa yekuparadza Paramende inomborega kukosheswa. 144 Sarudzo huru dzinoitwa mushure mekuparadzwa kweParamende

(1) Kana Paramende isina kumbobvira yavhota kuti iparadzwe kuchitevedzwa chikamu 143(2), Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuzivisa ruzhinji rwevanhu kuti kwava kuitwa sarudzo, otaura mazuva esarudzo huru, idzo dzinoitwa munguva yakatarwa muchikamu 158.

(2) Kana—

(a)

Paramende yavhotera kuti iparadzwe kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 143(2);

(b) Mutungamiriri weNyika aparadza Paramende kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 143(3);

(c)

Mutungamiriri weNyika aparadza Paramende zvichitevera vhoti yekushaya chivimbo neParamende kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 109(4); kana kuti

(d) Paramende ikanzi yakaparadzwa zvichitevera vhoti yekushaya chivimbo neParamende kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 109(5); Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuzivisa ruzhinji rwevanhu kuti kwava kuitwa sarudzo, otaura mazuva esarudzo huru mukati memazuva makumi mapfumbabwe mushure mekunge Paramende yavhotera kuparadzwa kwayo kana kuti Mutungamiriri weNyika aparadza Paramende.

(3) Mazuva esarudzo huru inoitwa kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (1) kana (2) anofanira kudzikwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekunge ataura neKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe. 145 Musangano wekutanga weParamende mushure mekuitwa kwesarudzo huru

(1) Musangano wekutanga weParamende mushure mekuitwa kwesarudzo huru unofanira kuitwa panguva nezuva rinotarwa neMutungamiriri weNyika, asi zuva racho harifaniri kunge riri kunze kwemazuva makumi matatu kubvira panguva yekunge akavhoterwa kuva Mutungamiriri weNyika atanga basa sezviri muchikamu 94.

(2) Kusvikira Mutungamiriri weSeneti kana kuti Mutauriri asarudzwa, zvichienderana nekuti ndezvipi zvinofanira kuitika, musangano wekutanga weDare reParamende unofanira kutungamirirwa neMunyori weParamende. 146 Misangano nenguva dzekuzorora Dare rimwe nerimwe reParamende rinoona kuti misangano yaro yekutanga inoitwa rinhi uye kuti inenge yakareba sei, pasingatariswi musangano wekutangisisa nenguva dzawo dzekuzorora, asi—

(a)

Mutungamiriri weNyika anogona kudaidza Paramende panguva chero ipi zvayo kuti iite basa rakakosha;

(b) mazuva zana nemakumi masere haafaniri kupfuura Dare rimwe nerimwe reParamende risati raita musangano. 147 Kusendekwa kwemaBhiri eMitemo, zvishuvo, matsamba ekutsutsumwa nezvimwewo zvichitevera kuparadzwa kweParamende Panoparadzwa Paramende, zvese zvinenge zvisati zvapedziswa panguva iyoyo zvinobva zvangomira uye maBhiri eMitemo, zvishuvo, matsamba ekutsutsumwa nezvimwe zvese hazvizokosheswi. GANHURO 9 Nyaya dzakasiyana-siyana dzine cheKuita neParamende 148 Kodzero nekudzivirirwa kweParamende

(1) Mutungamiriri weSeneti, Mutauriri uye nhengo dzeParamende dzinotaura zvadzinoda muParamende uye mumakomiti ese eParamende uye, kunyange vachifanira kutevedzera mitemo yemuDare rimwe nerimwe, havasungwi kana kuiswa mujeri kana kubhadhariswa pane chero zvipi zvazvo zvavanenge vataura, vaendesa kana kuturira kuParamende kana kukomiti yayo chero ipi zvayo.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unokwanisa—

(a)

kuita kuti pave nerumwe rusununguko nemamwe masimba eParamende, nhengo dzayo uye vashandi veParamende;

(b) kutsanangura kuti zvii zvingatorwe sekuzvidza Paramende, uko kunoitwa nenhengo dzeParamende kana nevamwewo vanhu; uye

(c)

kuti pave nekodzero yevanhu vanenge varwadziwa nezvinenge zvataurwa pamusoro pavo muParamende yekupindura, kuburikidza neMutauri kana Mutungamiriri weSeneti, zvichienderana nekuti ndiani ari kutungamirira;

asi hapana Mutemo unogona kubvumidza Paramende kana nhengo dzayo nevashandi vayo kuti irange munhu anenge apara mhosva, kunze kwefaindi zvichitevera kuita zvisingatarisirwi kana kuzvidza Paramende. 149 Kodzero yekutumira tsamba yekutsutsumwa kuParamende

(1) Mugari wese nemunhu wese ava kugara muZimbabwe zviri pamutemo ane kodzero yekutumira tsamba yekutsutsumwa kuParamende kuti itarise nyaya chero ipi zvayo yaine simba pamusoro payo, kusanganisira kudzika, kunatsurudza kana kudzima mutemo.

(2) Masvitsirwo anoitwa tsamba kuParamende uye danho rinotorwa neParamende painoona tsamba, zvinofanira kutsanangurwa muMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa. 150 Panosangana Paramende Paramende inokwanisa kusangana panzvimbo dzaisingambosanganiri ichizviitira ruzhinji rwevanhu, kuchengetedzeka kana kuti ichiona zviri nyore kwairi. 151 Komiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa

(1) Paramende inofanira kusarudza komiti inonzi Komiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa nechinangwa che—

(a)

kuongorora mashandiro eParamende;

(b) kugadzira Mitemo inofanira kutevedzwa;

(c)

kuongorora nekuva nechekuita panyaya dzese dzine chekuita neParamende; uye

(d) kuita zvimwe zvese zvainonzi iite neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana neMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa, kana nemumwewo mutemo.

(2) Komiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa inofanira kunge ine Mutauriri, Mutungamiriri weSeneti uye nhengo dzinotevera dzeParamende—

(a)

Mutevedzeri weMutauriri;

(b) Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weSeneti;

(c)

Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemari uye mamwe Makurukota maviri anosarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika;

(d) Mutungamiriri weBasa reHurumende muDare rimwe nerimwe;

(e)

Mutungamiriri weDare Rinopikisa muDare rimwe nerimwe;

(f)

Mutariri mukuru wemapato ezvematongerwo enyika ese akamiririrwa muDare rimwe nerimwe;

(g) Mutungamiriri weDare reMadzishe enyika yese;

(h) Nhengo mbiri dzisiri Makurukota eHurumende kana Vatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende, mumwe chete wacho ari Seneta akasarudzwa kuti apinde mukomiti neMutungamiriri weSeneti uye mumwe chete ari nhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika yakasarudzwa neMutauriri; uye

(i)

Nhengo sere dzisiri Makurukota eHurumende kana Vatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende, vana vacho vakasarudzwa kuti vapinde mukomiti neSeneti uye vana vakasarudzwa neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(3) Nhengo dzeKomiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa dzinofanira kusarudzwa kana kudomwa nekukurumidza zvichitevera musangano wekutanga weChikamu chimwe nechimwe cheParamende uye dzinofanira kusarudzwa nenzira inonyatsotaridza mapato ezvematongerwo enyika akamiririrwa uye uwandu hwevarume nevakadzi vari muZvikamu zviviri zveParamende.

(4) Komiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa inosarudzwa kuti ishande neParamende imwe chete kusvikira panoperera kushanda kweParamende iyoyo.

(5) Komiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa inotungamirirwa neMutauriri uye kana asipo, neMutungamiriri weSeneti.

(6) Mashandiro eKomiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa anofanira kutsanangurwa muMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(7) Pese zvapo panosara chinzvimbo chisina munhu muKomiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa panofanira kusarudzwa kana kudomwa nhengo nekukurumidza kuti itore chinzvimbo ichi. 152 Komiti inoona nezvemitemo muParamende

(1) Nekukurumidza kunogoneka, uye panotanga misangano yeParamende, Komiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa inofanira kuumba komiti inozivikanwa seKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende, iyo inenge ine nhengo dzeParamende dzinosvika nhatu uye dzisiri Makurukota eHurumende kana Vatevedzeri veMakurukota eHurumende.

(2) Nhengo zhinji dzeKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende dzinofanira kunge dziri vanhu vanokwanisa kushanda muZimbabwe semagweta kunze kwekunge pasina vanhu vakadaro vakawanda vanokwanisa kuiswa muKomiti iyi.

(3) Komiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende inofanira kuongorora—

(a)

Bhiri reMutemo rese kunze kwekunge riri Bhiri reMutemo weBumbiro reMitemo, uye risati ravhoterwa neSeneti kana neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika;

(b) Bhiri reMutemo rese rinenge ranatsurudzwa mushure mekuongororwa neKomiti, uye risati ravhoterwa neSeneti kana neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika;

(c)

mutemo wese unoburitswa muGazette;

(d) Bhiri reMutemo rese rinenge raendeswa kuKomiti neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana Gurukota reHurumende; kana kuti

(e)

Bhiri reMutemo rese rinenge raendeswa kuKomiti naani zvake ane masimba ekugadzira mutemo iwoyo;

uye inofanira kuzivisa Paramende kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana Gurukota reHurumende kana mumwewo ane masimba, kuti zvakatarwa

muBhiri reMutemo, kana mumwewo mutemo, zvinopikisana here, kana kuti mushure mekudzikwa kwemutemo, zvichazopikisana here nechero chipi zvacho chakanyorwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(4) Mushure mekuongorora mutemo chero upi zvawo, kusanganisira unenge uchiri kugadzirwa, Komiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende inofanira kuzivisa Paramende kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana Gurukota reHurumende kana mumwewo ane masimba, kuti zvakatarwa mumutemo uyu, kana kuti kana mutemo uyu ukadzikwa, uchazova nesimba rinopfuura reMutemo weParamende here kana kuti kwete.

(5) Mutemo weParamende kana Mitemo inofanira kutevedzwa inokwanisa kupa Komiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende mamwe mabasa. 153 Mari inotambirwa neMutungamiriri weSeneti, Mutauriri weParamende nenhengo dzeParamende

(1) Mari inotambirwa neMutauriri uye Mutungamiriri weSeneti—

(a)

inofanira kudzikwa neMutemo weParamende uye inobva muHomwe yemuunganidzwa wemari yeHurumende;

(b) haifaniri kuderedzwa paanenge ari pachinzvimbo; uye

(c)

inofanira kuramba ichibhadharwa kwaari mushure mekuparadzwa kweParamende kusvikira asisina chinzvimbo chake.

(2) Mari inotambirwa nenhengo dzeParamende inofanira kudzikwa neMutemo weParamende. 154 Munyori weParamende nevamwe vashandi

(1) Komiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa, zvichitevera kupihwa mvumo neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, inofanira kusarudza mushandi anozivikanwa seMunyori weParamende kuti aite basa rekuona mashandiro eParamende emazuva ese, asi achitevedza Mitemo inofanira kutevedzwa uye achitungamirirwa neMutauriri.

(2) Munyori weParamende anoshanda kwemakore matanhatu uye anogona kunzi ashande zvakare kwenguva yakafanana neiyi.

(3) Munyori weParamende anofanira kusiya chinzvimbo chake—

(a)

kana, mushure mekurudziro yeKomiti yeMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa, nhengo dzinopfuura chikamu chepakati nepakati dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika dzabvuma kuti Munyori anofanira kubviswa;

(b) kana Munyori achitarisirwa kusiya chinzvimbo chake nekuda kwekuti ava nhengo yeParamende; kana kuti

(c)

ashanda seMunyori kwemakore gumi nemaviri.

(4) Komiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa inofanira kudoma vamwe vashandi veParamende sezvainoona zvakakodzera.

(5) Munyori weParamende nevamwe vashandi veParamende—

(a)

vanosarudzwa kuchitevedzwa zvakatarwa, izvo zvinoongororwa neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa nguva nenguva; uye

(b) vashandi vanoshanda neruzhinji rwevanhu asi havasi vashandi veHurumende.

CHITSAUKO 7 Sarudzo GANHURO 1 Urongwa neZvinotevedzerwa paKuitwa kweSarudzo 155 Zvinofanira kutevedzwa pasarudzo

(1) Sarudzo, idzo dzinofanira kuitwa nguva nenguva, nemareferenda anoitwa pachitevedzerwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, dzinofanira—

(a)

kuva nerunyararo, dzakasununguka uye dzakaenzanirana;

(b) kuitwa munhu achivhota ari pake ega;

(c)

kunge dziri dzevanhu vabva zera vese uye dzichikoshesa mavhoti ese zvakaenzana; uye

(d) kunge dzisina mhirizhonga kana mamwewo maitiro akaipa musarudzo.

(2) Nyika inofanira kutora matanho ese akakodzera, kusanganisira ezvemitemo, kuitira kuona kuti zvinofanira kutevedzwa pasarudzo zvakatarwa muchikamu chidiki

(1) zvazadzikiswa, zvikuru sei, inofanira—

(a)

kuona kuti vagari vemunyika vese vanokwanisa kuvhota, zvichireva kuona kuti vagari vemunyika vanotaurwa nezvavo muNhamatidzwa 4 vanyoreswa sevavhoti;

(b) kuona kuti mugari wemunyika wese anokwanisa kuvhota musarudzo kana mureferenda awana mukana wekuvhota uye hurumende inofanira kuona kuti vakaremara vese nevanoda rubatsiro vavhota;

(c)

kuona kuti mapato ese ezvematongerwo enyika nevanokwikwidza musarudzo kana mureferenda vawana zvese zvavanoda neruzivo rwese runoita kuti vakwikwidze nepavanokwanisa;

(d) kuwanisa mapato ese ezvematongerwo enyika nevanokwikwidza musarudzo kana mureferenda mukana wakaenzana wekushandisa zvese zvinoshambadza mashoko, zvingave zvehurumende kana kuti zvisiri zvehurumende; uye

(e)

kuita kuti makakava ese akanangana nesarudzo agadziriswe nekukasika.

156 Kuitwa kwesarudzo nemareferenda Pasarudzo nereferenda chero ipi zvayo, Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kuona kuti—

(a)

nzira chero ipi zvayo yashandiswa pakuvhota hainetsi, inonyatsoshanda nemazvo, inokwanisa kuita kuti zvabuda zviongororwe, haisi zinyekenyeke, uye yakajeka;

(b) zvinenge zvabuda musarudzo kana mureferenda zvinoziviswa kuvanhu chiriporipocho mushure mekunge sarudzo dzapera; uye

(c)

matanho akakodzera atorwa—

(i) kuti pasave nemhirizhonga yakanangana nesarudzo kana mamwe maitiro akaipa akanangana nesarudzo; uye

(ii) pakunyatsochengetedza zvinenge zvashandiswa pakuvhota.

157 Mutemo weSarudzo

(1) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti paitwe sarudzo nemareferenda akatarwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino, uyezve unofanira kuona nezvinotevera—

(a)

kuganhurwa kwemiganhu yenharaunda dzekuvhotera nemawadhi nguva nenguva uye zvichienderana nechikamu 161;

(b) kunyoresa vanhu kuti vavhote uye kudoma zvinodikanwa kuti vanhu vanyorese mumagwaro emazita evavhoti;

(c)

zvinofanira kutevedzwa nemapato ezvematongerwo enyika, vanokwikwidza, uye vamwewo vanhu vane chekuita nesarudzo kana mareferenda;

(d) nzira yesarudzo yekuti uwandu hwezvinzvimbo zvinokwikwidzwa huenderane neuwandu hwevanhu vari munzvimbo pakusarudza nhengo dzeSeneti sezviri muchikamu 120(1)(a) uye zvigaro zvenhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvakachengeterwa vanhukadzi sezviri muchikamu 124(1)(b), uye zvinotevedzerwa pakutsiva vanenge vabva pazvigaro izvi uyezve kuti ndezvipi zvigaro zvinofanira kutsiviva nevanhu—

(i) vemapato ezvematongerwo enyika akafanana neeavo vaimbova nezvigaro izvi; uye

(ii) vakafanana neavo vaimbova nezvigaro izvi panyaya yekuva vanhurume kana vanhukadzi.

(e)

kusarudza vamiririri vevakaremara kuchitevedzwa chikamu 120(1)(d);

(f)

kuita sarudzo dzemakanzuru ematunhu makuru, emaguta neemaruwa kana munharaunda;

(g) kupikiswa kunoitwa zvinenge zvabuda musarudzo.

(2) Nzira yesarudzo yekuti uwandu hwezvinzvimbo zvinokwikwidzwa huenderane neuwandu hwevanhu vari munzvimbo yakatsanangurwa muchikamu chidiki

(1)(d) inofanira kuona kuti vanhukadzi vamiririrwa zvakaenzanirana nevanhurume pamaSeneta akataurwa nezvawo muchikamu 120(1)(a).

(3) Mutemo weSarudzo unofanira kuita kuti kudomwa kwevachazokwikwidza musarudzo chero ipi zvayo vadomwe mushure memazuva gumi nemana kana kupfuura kubvira panenge paziviswa kuti sarudzo dzichaitwa. Sarudzo dzinofanira kuzoitwa kubva pamazuva makumi matatu zvichienda kumusoro kubva pazuva rekudomwa kwevachazokwikwidza musarudzo idzi.

(4) Hapana natsurudzo dzinogona kuitwa paMutemo weSarudzo, kana mumwewo mutemo unenge waumbwa pasi peMutemo weSarudzo kunze kwekuti Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo yabvunzwa pfungwa dzayo uye chero zvipi zvazvo zvinenge zvakurudzirwa neKomisheni iyi zvakosheswa.

(5) Kana zvichinge zvaziviswa kuti kuchava nesarudzo, hapana shanduko ine chekuita nesarudzo ichaitwa paMutemo weSarudzo kana pane mumwewo mutemo, ichakosheswa pakuitwa kwesarudzo iyi.

GANHURO 2 Nguva yeKuitwa kweSarudzo 158 Nguva yekuitwa kweSarudzo

(1) Sarudzo huru inofanira kuitwa kuitira kuti vanhu vavhote kusati kwapfuura—

(a)

mazuva makumi matatu nguva yemakore mashanu akatarwa muchikamu 143 isati yasvika;

(b) mazuva makumi mapfumbamwe kubva panenge Paramende yabvumira kuparadzwa kuchitevedzwa chikamu 143(2) kana kuti;

(c)

mazuva makumi mapfumbamwe kubva pakuparadzwa kweParamende, kana Paramende yaparadzwa kuchitevedzwa chikamu 109(4) kana (5) zvichitevera vhoti yekushaya chivimbo neParamende.

(2) Sarudzo dzekumaruwa nematunhu dzinofanira kuitwa pamwe chete nesarudzo dzeMutungamiriri weNyika nedzeParamende.

(3) Sarudzo dzekutsiva nhengo dzeParamende nenhengo dzemaruwa nematunhu dzinofanira kuitwa mukati memazuva makumi mapfumbamwe zvichitevera kuzaruka kwezvinzvimbo, kunze kwekunge zvinzvimbo zvazaruka mukati memwedzi mipfumbamwe yakatarisana nesarudzo huru, zvichireva kuti zvinzvimbo zvinenge zvisina vanhu zvinoramba zvakadaro kusvikira sarudzo huru yaitwa. 159 Kutsiva zvinzvimbo zvinenge zvisisina vanhu Pese zvapo panozaruka chinzvimbo chemuhurumende chakavapo kuburikidza neBumbiro reMitemo rino uye chinopihwa munhu kuburikidza nesarudzo, kunze kwehofisi inotaurwa nezvayo muchikamu 158, vane basa rekuronga nezvesarudzo vanofanira kuita kuti sarudzo iitwe mukati memazuva makumi mapfumbamwe kuitira kuisa mumwe munhu pachinzvimbo ichi.

GANHURO 3 Kuganhurwa kweMatunhu eSarudzo 160 Uwandu hwematunhu nemawadhi

(1) Nechinangwa chekusarudza nhengo dzeParamende, Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kuganhura Zimbabwe kuti ive mumatunhu esarudzo mazana maviri negumi.

(2) Nechinangwa chekusarudza nhengo dzinomirira makanzuru ekumaruwa neemunharaunda, Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kuganhura nzvimbo dzemudzinharaunda kugadzira mawadhi, zvichienderana neuwandu hwenhengo dzinofanira kusarudzwa kuti dzimiririre nzvimbo idzi. 161 Kuganhurwa kwematunhu esarudzo

(1) Kamwe chete mumakore gumi, pazuva kana munguva inotarwa neKomisheni, uye mushure mekunge kuverengwa kwevanhu munyika kuchangobva kuitwa, Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kuganhura matunhu esarudzo.

(2) Kana kuganhurwa kwematunhu esarudzo kwapedza kuitwa asi kwasara nguva isingasvike mwedzi mitanhatu kubva pazuva rekuitwa kwesarudzo huru, miganhu inenge yaganhurwa iyi haishandiswi pasarudzo idzi uye miganhu yakange iripo kuganhurwa kutsva uku kusati kwaitwa, ndiyo inoshandiswa.

(3) Nepese panogoneka, miganhu yematunhu inofanira kuitwa nenzira yekuti panguva yekuganhurwa, uwandu hwevakanyoresa kuvhota huve hwakaenzana mudunhu rimwe nerimwe remuZimbabwe.

(4) Nepese panogoneka, miganhu yemawadhi inofanira kuitwa nenzira yekuti panguva yekuganhurwa, uwandu hwevakanyoresa kuvhota huve hwakaenzana muwadhi imwe neimwe yemudunhu.

(5) Pakutara—

(a)

miganhu yemawadhi, Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kuona kuti hapana wadhi inopatsanurwa ichiiswa munharaunda mbiri kana kupfuura;

(b) miganhu yematunhu esarudzo, Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kuona kuti hapana wadhi inopatsanurwa ichiiswa mumatunhu esarudzo maviri kana kupfuura.

(6) Pakuganhura Zimbabwe ichiiswa mumawadhi nematunhu esarudzo, Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kuongorora, munzvimbo chero ipi zvayo—

(a)

mamiriro enzvimbo iyi;

(b) zvinoshandiswa pakufambisa mashoko munzvimbo iyi;

(c)

kupararira kwakaita vanhu vakanyoresa kuvhota munzvimbo iyi;

(d) nharaunda chero ipi zvayo inoda kuongororwa maererano nevanhu vakanyoresa kuvhota;

(e)

kana kuganhurwa kuchitevera kumwezve kwakamboitika, kuganhurwa kwematunhu esarudzo kuripo panguva iyi; uye

(f)

uwandu hwevanhu vari munzvimbo iyi;

uye kuti ongororo dzese idzi dzinyatsoshanda, Komisheni inogona kusakoshesa nyaya yekuti matunhu esarudzo nemawadhi zvinofanira kuva neuwandu hwevanhu vanovhota hwakaenzana, asi hapana dunhu resarudzo kana wadhi yemunharaunda iri kutaurwa nezvayo inofanira kunge ine vanhu vakanyoresa kuvhota vakawanda kana kushomeka nezvikamu zvemakumi maviri kubva muzana kana zvichienzaniswa nemamwe matunhu esarudzo kana kuti mawadhi.

(7) Mushure mekuganhura mawadhi nematunhu esarudzo, Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kuendesa ripoti kuMutungamiriri weNyika inenge ine—

(a)

mawadhi nematunhu esarudzo ese uye mazita netsananguro yemiganhu yazvo;

(b) mepu kana mamepu anoratidza mawadhi nematunhu esarudzo aya; uye

(c)

rumwewo ruzivo kana zvimwewo zvinoonekwa sezvakakodzera neKomisheni;

uye Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuita kuti ripoti iyi iendeswe kuParamende mukati memazuva manomwe.

(8) Mukati memazuva gumi nemana ripoti iyi yaendeswa kuParamende—

(a)

Mutungamiriri weNyika anokwanisa kudzorera ripoti iyi kuKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe kuti iongorore chero chipi zvacho chinenge chichifanira kutariswa;

(b) rimwe rematare eParamende rinokwanisa kutaura kuti ripoti iyi idzorerwe kuKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe kuti iongorore chero chipi zvacho chinenge chichifanira kutariswa, uye kana izvi zvaitika, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kudzorera ripoti iyi kuKomisheni kuti iongorore chinhu ichi.

(9) Kana ripoti yadzorerwa kuKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (8), Komisheni iyi inofanira kuongorora zvakare chero chipi zvacho chinenge chanzi chiongororwe asi zvinenge zvazonzi zviitwe neKomisheni ndizvo zvinozoitwa.

(10) Nekukurumidza kwese kunogoneka uye mushure mekutevedza zviri muzvikamu zvidiki (7) ne(9), Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kutumira ripoti yekupedzisira kuMutungamiriri weNyika.

(11) Mukati memazuva gumi nemana kubva pakutambira ripoti yeKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuburitsa chiziviso muGazette achizivisa mazita nemiganhu yemawadhi neyematunhu esarudzo sekuganhurwa kwazvinenge zvaitwa neKomisheni.

(12) Kana pane kusaenderana pakati petsanagudzo yemiganhu yewadhi kana dunhu resarudzo chero ripi zvaro nemepu kana mamepu anenge agadzirwa neKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe, tsanagudzo ndiyo inofanira kukosheswa.

CHITSAUKO 8 Utongi neMatare eDzimhosva GANHURO 1 Mashandiro eMatare eDzimhosva 162 Masimba evatongi Masimba evatongi anobva kuvanhu vemuZimbabwe uye anoshandiswa kuburikidza nematare edzimhosva anoti—

(a)

Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo;

(b) Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro;

(c)

Dare redzimhosva repamusoro;

(d) Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi;

(e)

Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri;

(f)

Matare edzimhosva anoshanda nemamejasitiriti;

(g) Matare edzimhosva anoona nezvemitemo yechivanhu; uye

(h) mamwe matare edzimhosva anodzikwa kana kuti anoshanda ari pasi peMutemo weParamende 163 Utongi

(1) Utongi hwemuZimbabwe hunosanganisira—

(a)

2013

Mukuru wevatongi, Mutevedzeri weMukuru wevatongi nevamwe vatongi veDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo;

(b) vatongi veDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro;

(c)

Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva repamusoro nevamwe vatongi vedare iri;

(d) Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi nevamwe vatongi vedare iri;

(e)

Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri nevamwe vatongi vedare iri; uye

(f)

vanenge vachitungamirira matare edzimhosva anoshanda nemamejasitiriti; matare edzimhosva anoona nezvemitemo yechivanhu nemamwe matare edzimhosva anodzikwa kana kuti anoshanda ari pasi peMutemo weParamende.

(2) Mukuru wevatongi ndiye mutungamiriri wemabasa ezveutongi hwese uye ndiye zvakare anoona nezveDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo uyezve Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro.

(3) Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva repamusoro ndiye anoona nezvedare iri.

(4) Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi ndiye anoona nezvedare iri.

(5) Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri ndiye anoona nezvedare iri. 164 Kuzvimiririra kweutongi

(1) Matare edzimhosva emitemo anoshanda akazvimiririra uye anongotevedza Bumbiro reMitemo rino chete nemutemo wenyika, uye anofanira kuushandisa asina kwaakarerekera, nekukurumidza uye nemazvo uye pasina kutya, dzvene kana tsvete.

(2) Kuzvimiririra, kushanda pasina kwaakarerekera uye kushanda nemazvo kwematare edzimhosva ndiwo musimboti wekushanda zvakanaka kwemutemo uye utongi hunokoshesa kodzero dzevanhu, uye nekudaro—

(a)

Hurumende kana mapoka nevamiririri vehurumende padanho chero ripi zvaro, kana mumwewo munhu, hazvingakanganisi kushanda kwematare edzimhosva;

(b) Hurumende, kuburikidza nemitemo kana mamwe matanho, inofanira kubatsira nekuchengetedza matare edzimhosva kuti agone kuzvimiririra, kushanda asina kwaakarerekera, kuva nechiremera, kusvikika, nekushanda nemazvo uye kuona kuti anotevedza zvaanofanira kutevedza zvakatarwa muchikamu 165.

(3) Mutongo chero upi zvawo unodzikwa nedare redzimhosva unosunga Hurumende nevanhu vese uye mapoka ehurumende anoenderana nawo uye, unofanira kukosheswa.

(4) Hapana chiri muchikamu chino chinofanira kutorwa sekuti chiri kutadzisa Mutemo weParamende kupa nhengo yeutongi rimwe basa rekuita risiri rekupa mitongo, ndokunge kuita basa kusingakanganisi kuzvimiririra kwenhengo iyoyo pakuita basa rayo rekupa mitongo uyezve kuitwa kwebasa iri kusingakanganisiwo kuzvimiririra kwemabasa ezveutongi ese.

165 Zvinofanira kutevedzwa mumabasa ezveutongi

(1) Pakushandisa masimba avo ezveutongi, vatongi vanofanira kutungamirirwa nezvinofanira kutevedzwa zvinotevera—

(a)

ruenzaniso runofanira kuwaniswa kuvanhu vese, zvisinei nechinzvimbo chavo muupenyu;

(b) ruenzaniso harufaniri kunonotswa, uye nekudaro, vatongi vanofanira kuita basa ravo nemazvo uye vachikurumidza;

(c)

basa rinoitwa nematare edzimhosva rakakosha chaizvo pakuchengetedza kodzero dzevanhu nerusununguko rwavo uye kutevedzerwa kwemutemo.

(2) Vatongi, vari mumwe nemumwe kana kuti pamwe chete, vanofanira kuremekedza nekukoshesa chinzvimbo chavo sechivimbo cheruzhinji rwevanhu mavari, voedza kuwedzera kuzvimiririra kwavo kuti ruzhinji rwevanhu rurambe rune chivimbo nemashandiro evatongi.

(3) Pakupa mutongo, mutongi anofanira kuita izvi akasununguka uye pasina kukanganiswa kana kusundwa zvisina kodzero, kuti ape mutongo wake.

(4) Vatongi havafaniri—

(a)

kupinda mune zvematongerwo enyika chero zvipi zvazvo;

(b) kutora chinzvimbo kana kuva nhengo yebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro;

(c)

kutsvaga mari kana kubvisa mari ichipihwa kubato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro; kana

(d) kuenda kumisangano yezvematongerwo enyika.

(5) Vatongi havafaniri kukumbira kana kutambira chipo, zvimwewo zvavanopihwa, chikwereti kana rubatsiro runogona kukanganisa basa ravo reutongi kana kupa chiratidzo chinoita sekuti vatongi havashandi nemazvo.

(6) Vatongi vanofanira kukoshesa basa ravo reutongi kupfuura zvimwe zvese, uye havafaniri kuita chero chipi zvacho chinokanganisa basa ravanotarisirwa kuita.

(7) Vatongi vanofanira kutora matanho akakodzera ekuchengetedza nekuvandudza ruzivo rwavo rwebasa, mashandiro avo neunhu hwavo, uye zvikuru sei, vanofanira kugara vachiziva zvinhu zvitsva zvinenge zvichiitika kumutemo wenyika uye wepasi rese. 166 Dare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo

(1) Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo idare rakakosha chaizvo uye rinoumbwa nevanhu ava—

(a)

Mukuru wevatongi neMutevedzeri weMukuru wevatongi; uye

(b) vamwe vatongi vashanu veDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo;

(2) Kana pachidikanwa mutongi anombofanobata chigaro muDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kwechinguva chidiki, Mukuru wevatongi anogona kusarudza mutongi kana kuti aimbova mutongi kuti ashande semutongi wedare iri kwenguva iyoyo.

(3) Nyaya dzinounzwa kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo—

(a)

dziri maererano nekukanganiswa kwekodzero dzemunhu nerusununguko rwake zvakakoshesesa zvakatarwa muChitauko 4 kunofungidzirwa kuti

kwakaitika, kana kuti dzakanangana nekusarudzwa kweMutungamiriri weNyika kana Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, dzinofanira kunzwikwa nevatongi vese veDare iri;

(b) dzinenge dzisiri nyaya dzataurwa nezvadzo mundima (a) dzinofanira kunzwikwa nevatongi veDare iri vanosvika vatatu kana kupfuura; asi Mutemo weParamende kana mirawo yeDare iri inogona kuita kuti nyaya dzinenge dzichida kudzikirwa mutongo dzisati dzanyatsopera kutongwa dzinzwikwe nemutongi mumwe chete weDare iri kana kudarika.

(4) Mutongi kana aimbova mutongi anosarudzwa kuti ashande kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (2) anogona kuramba achishanda muDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo nguva yaakanzi ashande yapfuura nechinangwa chekupedzisa nyaya dzakatanga panguva yaainge akafanobata chigaro. 167 Masimba eDare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo

(1) Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo—

(a)

ndiro dare redzimhosva rekupedzisira panyaya dzese dzine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo uye zvarinotaura panyaya idzi zvinosunga mamwe matare edzimhosva ese;

(b) rinopa mutongo panyaya dzeBumbiro reMitemo chete kana dzimwewo dzakarerekera kuBumbiro reMitemo, zvikuru sei zvinenge zvaunzwa kuDare iri pasi pechikamu 131(8)(b) nendima 9(2) muNhamatidzwa 4; uye

(c)

ndiro rinopa mutongo wekupedzisira pamusoro pekuti nyaya ine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo here kana kuti inodyidzana here nemutongo une chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo.

(2) Kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro remitemo chete ndiro rinogona—

(a)

kutaura nezvekuti mutemo chero upi zvawo uri kugadzirwa unoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino here kana kuti kwete, asi rinongogona kuita izvi chete kana mutemo waendeswa kwariri kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino;

(b)

kunzwa nekupa mutongo pamakakava ane chekuita nekusarudzwa kweMutungamiriri weNyika;

(c)

kunzwa nekupa mutongo pamakakava ane chekuita nekuti mumwe munhu anokodzera here kuva Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika; kana kuti

(d)

kuona kuti Paramende kana Mutungamiriri weNyika atadza kuzadzikisa basa rake sezvinotarisirwa muBumbiro reMitemo here kana kuti kwete.

(3) Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rinotaura mashoko ekupedzisira maererano nekuti Mutemo weParamende kana kuti zvinenge zvaitwa neMutungamiriri weNyika zvinoenderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo here kana kuti kwete, uye rinofanira kutaura kuti chese chinenge chanzi hachishandi nerimwe dare, hachishandi zvechokwadi here, zvinenge zvataurwa nedare iri zvisati zvatanga kushanda.

(4) Mutemo weParamende unokwanisa kuita kuti Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rishandise masimba aro eutongi, uye nechinangwa ichi, unogona kupa Dare iri simba rekugadzira mitemo yedare.

(5) Mitemo yeDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo inofanira kubvumidza munhu, kana zvichiitirwa kuwanisa ruenzaniso, uye ane mvumo kana kuti asina mvumo yeDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo—

(a)

kuunza nyaya ine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo;

(b) kuendesa nyaya yake iri kutariswa nerimwewo dare kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo;

(c)

kumira seshamwari yedare redzimhosva.

168 Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro

(1) Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro idare rakakosha kwazvo uye rinosanganisira—

(a)

Mukuru wevatongi neMutevedzeri weMukuru wevatongi;

(b) vatongi vaviri kana kupfuura veDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro; uye

(c)

vamwe vatongi chero vapi zvavo vanowedzerwa vanenge vasarudzwa pasi pechikamu chidiki (2).

(2) Kana pachidikanwa kuti mumwe mutongi awedzerwe kuti amboshanda kwechinguva chidiki muDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro, Mukuru wevatongi anogona kusarudza mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusoro kana kuti aimbova mutongi kuti ashande semutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro kwenguva iyoyo.

(3) Mutongi, kana aimbova mutongi, anosarudzwa kuti ashande kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (2), anogona kuramba achishanda muDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo nguva yaakanzi ashande yapfuura nechinangwa chekupedzisa nyaya dzakatanga panguva yaainge akafanobata chigaro. 169 Masimba edare redzimhosva repamusorosoro

(1) Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro ndiro dare rekupedzisira muZimbabwe, kunze kwekuti nyaya dziri kutongwa dzichifanira kutongwa neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo.

(2) Zvichienderana nechikamu chidiki (1), Mutemo weParamende unogona kuwedzera masimba ekutonga kuDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unogona kuita kuti Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro rishandise masimba aro eutongi, uye, nechinangwa ichocho, unogona kupa dare iri simba rekugadzira mitemo yedare.

(4) Mitemo yedare redzimhosva inogona kupa munyori weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro mabasa nemasimba chero api zvawo edare iri panyaya dzine chekuita nekukanganisirana mukurarama—

(a)

kuraira zvinofanira kuitwa munyaya dzinenge dzisingakwikwidzwi, kunze kwekunge kuita izvi kuchikanganisa mamiriro kana machengeterwo evana;

(b) kusarudza zvekuita panyaya dzichiri kutanga kana dzinenge dzichida kudzikirwa mutongo idzo dzisati dzanyatsopera kutongwa, kusanganisira zvese zvarakanyorerwa kuti ritaure mafambiro azvo, asi kwete nyaya dzinokanganisa rusununguko rwemunhu chero upi zvake; asi mitemo yacho inofanira kupa munhu wese zvake anenge ane chekuita nezvinenge zvataurwa nemunyori wedare, mukana wekuti nyaya yake iongororwe nemutongi

weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro, uyo anogona kuzvitsigira, kuzvinatsurudza, kumbozviisa padivi kana kupa mumwewo mutongo waanoona wakakodzera. 170 Dare redzimhosva repamusoro

(1) Dare redzimhosva repamusoro idare rakakosha kwazvo uye rinosanganisira—

(a)

Mukuru wevatongi, mutevedzeri weMukuru wevatongi neMutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva repamusoro; uye

(b) vamwe vatongi veDare redzimhosva repamusoro vanosarudzwa nguva nenguva. 171 Masimba edare redzimhosva repamusoro

(1) Dare redzimhosva repamusoro—

(a)

ndiro dare rinotonga nyaya dzese dzevanenge vachikakavadzana nedzeavo vanenge vapara mhosva munyika yese yeZimbabwe;

(b) rine simba rekuongorora mashandiro ematare anoshanda nemamejasitiriti, nemamwewo matare madiki uye kuongorora mitongo inenge yadzikwa nematare aya;

(c)

rinogona kuzeya nyaya dzine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo, kusiya kwedzinofanira kuzeyiwa neDare reDzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo; uye

(d) rine simba rekushandura mitongo inenge yadzikwa nemamwe matare sezvarinotarisirwa kuita neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unogona kuita kuti Dare redzimhosva repamusoro rishandise masimba aro eutongi, uye, nechinangwa ichocho, unogona kupa dare iri simba rekugadzira mitemo yedare.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unogona kuita kuti Dare redzimhosva repamusoro rikamurwe-kamurwe kuita zvikamu zvakawanda zvinotarisa nyaya dzakasiyana-siyana neunyanzvi, asi chikamu chimwe nechimwe chinenge chaumbwa saizvozvo chinofanira kushandisa masimba anongoshandiswawo neDare redzimhosva repamusoro panyaya chero ipi zvayo inenge yaunzwa kuchikamu ichi.

(4) Mitemo yedare redzimhosva inogona kupa munyori weDare redzimhosva repamusoro masimba panyaya dzine chekuita nekukanganisirana mukurarama—

(a)

ekutaura zvinofanira kuitwa munyaya dzinenge dzisingakwikwidzwi, kunze kwekunge kuita izvi kuchikanganisa chinzvimbo nekuchengetwa kwevana;

(b) ekusarudza zvekuita panyaya dzichiri kutanga kana dzinenge dzichida kudzikirwa mutongo idzo dzisati dzanyatsopera kutongwa, kusanganisira zvese zvarakanyorerwa kuti ritaure mafambiro azvo, asi kwete nyaya dzinokanganisa rusununguko rwemunhu chero upi zvake; asi mitemo yacho inofanira kupa munhu wese zvake anenge ane chekuita nezvinenge zvataurwa nemunyori wedare, mukana wekuti nyaya yake iongororwe nemutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusoro, uyo anogona kuzvitsigira, kuzvinadzurudza, kumbozviisa padivi kana kupa mumwewo mutongo waanoona wakakodzera. 172 Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi

(1) Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi idare rakakosha kwazvo uye rinosanganisira—

(a)

Mutongi Mukuru; uye

(b) vamwe vatongi veDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi vanosarudzwa nguva nenguva. [Chikamu ichi chakagadziriswa neChikamu 3 cheMutemo 10 wa2017]

(2) Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi rine simba pamusoro penyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi uye nedzemashandiro, sekupihwa masimba kwarinoitwa neMutemo weParamende.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unogona kuita kuti Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi rishandise masimba aro eutongi, uye, nechinangwa ichocho, unogona kupa dare iri simba rekugadzira mitemo yedare. 173 Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri

(1) Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri idare rakakosha kwazvo uye rinosanganisira—

(a)

Mutongi Mukuru; uye

(b) vamwe vatongi veDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri vanosarudzwa nguva nenguva. [Chikamu chakagadziriswa neChikamu 4 cheMutemo 10 wa2017]

(2) Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri rine simba pamusoro penyaya dzeUtungamiriri sekupihwa masimba kwarinoitwa neMutemo weParamende.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unogona kuita kuti Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri rishandise masimba aro eutongi, uye, nechinangwa ichocho, unogona kupa dare iri simba rekugadzira mitemo yedare. 174 Mamwe matare edzimhosva

(1) Mutemo weParamende unogona kutaura nezvemaumbirwo, mhando yevanhu vanoshandira uye panotangira nekuperera utongi hwe—

(a)

matare edzimhosva emamejasitiriti kuti atonge nyaya dzevanokakavadzana pakurarama nevanopara mhosva;

(b) matare edzimhosva anoona nezvemitemo yechivanhu ane basa zvikuru sei, rekuona nezvemashandiro emitemo yechivanhu;

(c) mamwe matare edzimhosva ari pasi peDare redzimhosva repamusoro; uye

(d) mamwewo matare edzimhosva madiki anotonga nekuzeya nyaya uye anoyananisa vanhu.

(2) Zvichienderana nechikamu 188(4) cheBumbiro reMitemo, mihoro, dzimwe mari dzekushanda kana zvimwewo zvingapihwe vatongi vemuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvevashandi nevashandirwi pamwe chete neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzehutungamiriri vari pazvinzvimbo izvi kana kuti vari kufanobata zvinzvimbo izvi pazuva rekutanga kushandiswa kweBumbiro rino haizoderedzwi. [Chikamu chakagadziriswa neChikamu 5 cheMutemo 10 wa2017

175 Masimba ematare edzimhosva panyaya dzakanangana neBumbiro reMitemo

(1) Kana dare rataura kuti mutemo chero upi zvawo kana kuti zvinenge zvaitwa chero zvipi zvazvo neMutangamiriri weNyika kana kuti neParamende hazvienderani neBumbiro reMitemo, zvarinenge rataura izvozvo hazvina simba kusvikira zvatsigirwa neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo.

(2) Dare redzimhosva rinotaura kuti zvimwe zvinhu hazvisi pamutemo kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (1) rinogona kufanopa mutongo, kana kumbosunungura ari kunenedzerwa mhosva, kana kumbomisa kutongwa kwenyaya, iyi richimirira zvinozotaurwa neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo pamusoro pemashandiro emutemo kana zvimwewo zvinenge zvichida kuzeyiwa.

(3) Munhu wese zvake anokwanisa kunyunyuta kana kukumbira Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kuti ritsigire kana kupikisa zvinenge zvichinzi hazvienderani neBumbiro reMitemo kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (1).

(4) Kana panyuka chinhu chine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo mudare redzimhosva, anenge achitungamirira kutongwa kwenyaya yacho anokwanisa iye ega, kana kuti akumbirwa neane chekuita nenyaya iyi, kuti aendese nyaya iyi kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, kunze kwekuti achiona kuti chikumbiro chaitwa hachina maturo kana kuti ndechedambe.

(5) Mutemo weParamende kana mitemo yematare inofanira kuti chese chinenge chanzi nerimwewo dare, risiri Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, hachienderani neBumbiro reMitemo, kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (1), chitariswe neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo.

(6) Pakuzeya nyaya ine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo yarine simba rekutonga, dare rinogona—

(a)

kutaura kuti mutemo chero upi zvawo kana chimwe chiitiko chisingaenderani neBumbiro reMitemo hazvishandi, zvichienderana nekuti zvinopikisana zvakadii neBumbiro reMitemo;

(b) kudzika mutongo wakaenzanirana, kusanganisira mutongo wekuti nguva iyo chinhu chisingaenderani neBumbiro reMitemo chakatanga kusaenderana naro inotangira papi uye kudzika mutemo unombomisa kushanda kwechinhu ichi, pachikosheswa kana kusakosheswa zvimwe zvinhu, kuitira kuti ane ruzivo rwakakwana pamusoro pechinhu ichi, kana mutemo uyu, awane nguva yekuugadzirisa. 176 Masimba agara aripo uye asingashanduki ematare anoti rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro nerepamusoro Matare anoti Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro neDare redzimhosva repamusoro, ane masimba agara aripo uye asingashanduki ekuchengetedza nekufambisa basa ravo uye ekugadzira mutemo unoshanda munyika kana wechivanhu, pachikosheswa nyaya yekuti pave neruenzaniso nemitemo iri muBumbiro reMitemo rino.

GANHURO 2 Kusarudzwa kweVatongi neUrefu hweNguva dzaVanofanira kuva paZvinzvimbo 177 Zvinodikanwa kuti munhu akwanise kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo

(1) Munhu anokodzera kusarudzwa kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kana ari mugari wemuZimbabwe, ane makore ekuberekwa makumi mana kana kupfuura uye achinyatsonzwisisa mitemo yakanangana neBumbiro reMitemo, uyezve ane zvinotevera—

(a)

ari kana kuti akambova mutongi wedare redzimhosva rinokwanisa kutonga nyaya dzese dzekukakavadzana kwevanhu nekuparwa kwemhosva munyika inoshandisa mutemo wechiRoma nechiDhachi kana weChirungu, uye Chirungu uriwo mutauro unoshandiswa zviri pamutemo; kana kuti

(b) kwemakore anosvika kana kupfuura gumi nemaviri akatevedzana kana kuti asina kutevedzana, ange achikodzera kushanda segweta—

(i) muZimbabwe; kana kuti

(ii) munyika inoshandisa mutemo wechiRoma nechiDhachi kana weChirungu, uye Chirungu uriwo mutauro unoshandiswa zviri pamutemo;

uye parizvino achikodzera kuva gweta.

(2) Kuti asarudzwe kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, munhu anofanira kunge ari munhu akagwinya uye akakodzera kushanda semutongi. 178 Zvinodikanwa kuti munhu akwanise kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro

(1) Munhu anokwanisa kusarudzwa kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro kana ari mugari wemuZimbabwe, ane makore ekuberekwa makumi mana kana kupfuura, uyezve aine zvinotevera—

(a)

ari kana kuti akambova mutongi wedare redzimhosva rinogona kutonga nyaya dzese dzekukakavadzana kwevanhu nekuparwa kwemhosva munyika inoshandisa mutemo wechiRoma nechiDhachi kana weChirungu, uye Chirungu uriwo mutauro unoshandiswa zviri pamutemo; kana kuti

(b) kwemakore anosvika kana kupfuura gumi akatevedzana kana asina kutevedzana, ange achikodzera kushanda segweta—

(i) muZimbabwe; kana kuti

(ii) munyika inoshandisa mutemo wechiRoma nechiDhachi kana weChirungu, uye Chirungu uriwo mutauro unoshandiswa zviri pamutemo; uye parizvino achikodzera kuva gweta.

(2) Kuti asarudzwe kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro, munhu anofanira kunge ari munhu akagwinya uye akakodzera kushanda semutongi. 179 Zvinodikanwa kuti munhu akwanise kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusoro, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri

(1) Munhu anokwanisa kusarudzwa kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusoro, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri kana ane makore ekuberekwa makumi mana kana kupfuura, uye ane zvinotevera—

(a)

ari kana kuti akambova mutongi wedare redzimhosva rinokwanisa kutonga nyaya dzese dzekukakavadzana kwevanhu nekuparwa kwemhosva munyika inoshandisa mutemo wechiRoma nechiDhachi kana weChirungu, uye Chirungu uriwo mutauro unoshandiswa zviri pamutemo; kana kuti

(b) kwemakore anosvika kana kupfuura manomwe akatevedzana kana asina kutevedzana, ange achikodzera kushanda segweta—

(i) muZimbabwe; kana kuti

(ii) munyika inoshandisa mutemo wechiRoma nechiDhachi kana weChirungu, uye Chirungu uriwo mutauro unoshandiswa zviri pamutemo;

(iii) ari mugari wemuZimbabwe ari munyika inoshandisa mutemo wechiRoma nechiDhachi kana weChirungu, uye Chirungu uriwo mutauro unoshandiswa zviri pamutemo; uye parizvino achikodzera kuva gweta.

(2) Kuti asarudzwe kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusoro, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri, munhu anofanira kunge ari munhu akagwinya uye akakodzera kushanda semutongi. 180 Kusarudzwa kwevatongi

(1) Mukuru Mukuru wevatongi, mutevedzeri weMukuru Mukuru wevatongi, Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva repamusoro uye vamwe vatongi vese vanosarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kuchitevedzwa chikamu chino.

(2) Mukuru Mukuru wevatongi, mutevedzeri weMukuru Mukuru wevatongi, Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva repamusoro uye vamwe vatongi vese vanosarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekumbonzwawo pfungwa dzeKomisheni inoona nezvemashandire ematare edzimhosva.

(3) Kana kusarudzwa kweMukuru Mukuru wevatongi, mutevedzeri weMukuru Mukuru wevatongi, Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva repamusoro kusingaenderani nezvinotsinhirwa neKomisheni inoona nezvemashandiro ematare edzimhosva, maringe nechitsauko chidiki (2), Mutungamiri weNyika achaita kuti Seneti iziviswe nekukurumidza: Nekuda kwekuti zvinyatsojeka, zvinoziviswa kuti zvinenge zvataurwa neMutungamiri weNyika hazvikwanisi kuva neanozopikisa.

(4) Pese pakakodzera kuti pasarudzwe mutongi asiri Mukuru Mukuru wevatongi, mutevedzeri weMukuru Mukuru wevatongi, Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva repamusoro, Komisheni inoona nezvemashandiro ematare edzimhosva inofanira—

(a) kushambadza chinzvimbo ichi; uye

(b) kuita kuti Mutungamiri weNyika neveruzhinji vadome vanhu vavanofunga kuti vakakodzera; uye

(c) kubvunza mibvunzo yekupinda basa kuvanhu vanenge vadomwa ava pajekerere; uye

(d) kunyora pasi mazita evanhu vatatu vanenge vabudirira kubva pavanhu vanenge vadomwa kuti vapinde pazvinzvimbo izvi; uye

(e) kuendesa mazita aya kuMutungamiri weNyika, uye zvichienderana nezvakapihwa muchitsauko chidiki (5), Mutungamiri weNyika anozosarudza munhu mumwe chete kuti apinde pachinzvimbo ichocho.

(5) Kana Mutungamiri weNyika aona kuti pamazita evanhu vanenge vasarudzwa zvichienderana nechitsauko chidiki (4)(e) hapana anenge akakodzera kupinda pachigaro ichi, Mutungamiri weNyika anozokumbira kuti Komisheni inoona nezvemashandiro ematare edzimhosva iunze mamwezve mazita evanhu vatatu vakakodzera, uye Mutungamiri weNyika anozosarudza munhu mumwe chete kubva pamazita iwayo kuti azopinda pachigaro ichocho.

(6) Mutungamiri weNyika anozoita kuti chiziviso chevanhu vese vanenge vagadzwa pazvinzvimbo zvichitevera chikamu chino chitsikiswe muGazeti.

(7) Zvigaro zveMutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi neMutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzehutungamiriri zvinofanira kupindwa nemumwe mutongi kana mumwewo kana neanofanobata chigaro chemutongi, zvichienderana nezvinenge zviripo, padare rakakodzera, uye vanhu vanozopinda pazvigaro izvi vanosarudzwa neMukuru Mukuru wevatongi mushure mekumbonzwawo pfungwa dzeKomisheni inoona nezvemashandiro ematare edzimhosva. [Chikamu chakagadziriswa neChikamu 6 cheMutemo 10 wa2017]

181 Kusarudzwa kwevanofanobata zvigaro zvevatongi

(1) Kana chigaro cheMukuru wevatongi chisina munhu kana kuti anofanira kunge ari pachinzvimbo ichi asingakwanisi kuita basa rake, Mutevedzeri weMukuru wevatongi ndiye anofanobata chinzvimbo chake, asi kana zvinzvimbo zvevaviri ava zvisina vanhu kana kuti vanofanira kunge vari pazvinzvimbo izvi vasingakwanisi kuita basa ravo, Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo ndiye anofanobata chigaro cheMukuru wevatongi.

(2) Kana chigaro che—

(a) Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva repamusoro;

(b) Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi; uye

(c) Mutongi Mukuru weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzehutungamiriri. [Chikamu chakagadziriswa neChikamu 7 cheMutemo 10 wa2017]

(3) Kana pachidikanwa kuti mumwezve mutongi abatsire kwechinguva chidiki muDare redzimhosva repamusoro, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi kana Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri, Mutungamiriri weNyika, achitevedzera zvinenge zvataurwa neKomisheni inoona nezveutongi, anokwanisa kusarudza aimbova mutongi kuti ashande semutongi wedare iri kwenguva isingapfuuri mwedzi gumi nemiviri, uye anokwanisa kunzi ashande zvakare kwenguva imwe cheteyo.

(4) Munhu anenge asarudzwa kuti ashande kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki

(3) anokwanisa kuramba achishanda semutongi nguva yaakanzi ashande yapfuura nechinangwa chekupedzisa nyaya dzakatanga panguva yaainge akambofanobata chigaro. 182 Kusarudzwa kwemamejasitiriti nedzimwewo nhengo dzeutongi Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti pasarudzwe mamejasitiriti nevamwewo vanoita zveutongi asi vasiri vatongi, asi—

(a) mamejasitiriti anofanira kusarudzwa neKomisheni inoona nezvevatongi;

(b) vanoita zveutongi asi vasiri mamejasitiriti kana vatongi vanofanira kusarudzwa zvichitevera kurudziro yeKomisheni inoona nezvevatongi;

(c) kusarudzwa kwevanhu ava kunofanira kuitwa zviri pachena uye pasina kutya, tsvete, dzvene, chizivano kana rusarura. 183 Vashandi vemumatare edzimhosva havafaniri kusarudzwa kuti vashande mumatare edzimhosva maviri kana kupfuura Kunze kwekuti pasingatariswi zvakatarwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino, munhu haafaniri kusarudzwa kuti ave mutongi wematare edzimhosva maviri kana kupfuura.

184 Kusarudzwa kwevatongi kunofanira kutaridza mamiriro evanhu vemunyika Kusarudzwa kwevatongi kunofanira kutaridza kusiyana-siyana kwevanhu vemuZimbabwe pamwe chete neuwandu hwevanhukadzi nevanhurume. 185 Mhiko yekutanga basa

(1) Asati atanga basa rake, Mukuru wevatongi uye mutevedzeri weMukuru wevatongi anofanira kuita mhiko yebasa pamberi peMutungamiriri weNyika kana mumwewo munhu anenge apihwa mvumo neMutungamiriri weNyika sezvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(2) Asati atanga basa, mutongi asiri Mukuru wevatongi kana mutevedzeri weMukuru wevatongi anofanira kuita mhiko yebasa pamberi peMukuru wevatongi kana mumwewo mutongi mukuru anenge aripo sezvakatarwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(3) Mitemo yeParamende inoita kuti mamejasitiriti nevamwe vashandi vezveutongi avo vasiri vatongi vasarudzwe, inofanira kugadzira mhiko inoitwa nenhengo dzeutongi idzodzo. 186 Urefu hwenguva yekutonga yevatongi

(1) Vatongi veDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo vanoshanda kwemakore asingazowedzerwi, uye asingapfuuri gumi nemashanu asi—

(a)

vanofanira kusiya basa kana vasvitsa makore makumi manomwe; uye

(b) mushure mekusiya basa nekuda kwekuti nguva yavo yakwana, vatongi ava vanokwanisa kusarudzwa kuti vave vatongi veDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro kana veDare redzimhosva repamusoro, nekuda kwavo zvavo, kana vachikodzera kuita basa iri.

(2) Vatongi veDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro uye veDare redzimhosva repamusoro vanoshanda kubvira pazuva ravanogadzwa pachigaro kusvikira vava nemakore makumi manomwe, apo pavanofanira kusiya basa.

(3) Munhu anokwanisa kusarudzwa kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro kana weDare redzimhosva repamusoro kwechinguva chidiki asi kana munhu asarudzwa saizvozvo, uye asingambofanobata chigaro, anobva angoregedza kuva mutongi kana asvitsa makore makumi manomwe kunyange nguva yake yekushanda isati yakwana.

(4) Kunyange zvazvo mutongi achinge asiya basa kana kuti asvitsa makore makumi manomwe, kana kuti sezvinotarisirwa kuMutongi weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kana mutongi anotaurwa nezvake muchikamu chidiki (3), asvika kumagumo kwenguva yaanofanira kushanda, mutongi uyu anokwanisa kuramba achiita basa rake nechinangwa chekupedzisa nyaya dzakatanga kuunzwa kudare paainge ari mutongi.

(5) Mutongi anokwanisa kusiya chinzvimbo chake panguva chero ipi zvayo kuburikidza nekunyora tsamba yeziviso kuMutungamiriri weNyika, iyo inoendeswa kwaari neKomisheni inoona nezveutongi.

(6) Chinzvimbo chemutongi hachifaniri kuparadzwa panguva yaanenge ari pabasa. 187 Kubviswa kwevatongi pazvinzvimbo zvavo

(1) Mutongi anokwanisa chete kubviswa pachinzvimbo chake nekuda kwe—

(a)

kutadza kuita basa rake nekuda kwekurwara kana kukanganisika pfungwa;

(b) kutadza basa zvakanyanya; kana

(c) kuita zvisingatarisirwi zvachose pabasa rake; uye mutongi haakwanisi kubviswa pachinzvimbo chake kunze kwekuti achibviswa zvinoenderana nechikamu chino.

(2) Kana Mutungamiriri weNyika aona kuti nyaya yekuti Mukuru wevatongi abviswe pachinzvimbo chake inofanira kuongororwa, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuumba dare rinoongorora nyaya iyi.

(3) Kana Komisheni inoona nezveutongi yakurudzira Mutungamiriri weNyika kuti nyaya yekuti Mukuru wevatongi abviswe pachinzvimbo chake inofanira kuongororwa, Mutungamiriri anofanira kuumba dare rinoongorora nyaya iyi.

(4) Dare rinoumbwa pasi pechikamu chino rinofanira kunge rine nhengo dzinosvika kana kupfuura nhatu dzinosarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika uye panhengo idzi–

(a) mumwe chete wadzo (kana kupfuura) anofanira kunge ari munhu—

(i) akambova Mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro kana weDare redzimhosva repamusoro muZimbabwe; kana kuti

(ii) ari kana kuti akambova mutongi wedare redzimhosva rinokwanisa kutonga nyaya dzese dzekukakavadzana kwevanhu nekuparwa kwemhosva munyika inoshandisa mutemo wechiRoma nechiDhachi kana weChirungu, uye Chirungu uriwo mutauro unoshandiswa zviri pamutemo;

(b) mumwe chete wavo (kana kupfuura) anofanira kusarudzwa kubva pamazita evanhu vatatu kana kupfuura vava nemakore manomwe kana kupfuura vari magweta uye, vanenge vasarudzwa nesangano rinoumbwa pasi peMutemo weParamende, rinomiririra magweta emuZimbabwe.

(5) Sangano rinotaurwa nezvaro muchikamu chidiki (4) (b) rinofanira kubuda nemazita anotaurwa nezvavo muchikamu chidiki ichi kana ratumwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kuita izvozvo.

(6) Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kusarudza nhengo imwe chete yedare rinoumbwa pasi pechikamu chino kuti atungamirire dare iri.

(7) Dare rinoumbwa pasi pechikamu chidiki (2) kana (3) rinofanira kuongorora nyaya yekuti mutongi obviswa pachinzvimbo chake here uye, mushure mekuita izvozvo, rinofanira kuzivisa Mutungamiriri weNyika zvarinenge rawana, rotaura kuti mutongi anofanira kubviswa here kana kuti kwete.

(8) Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuita zvinenge zvakurudzirwa nedare iri, kuchitevedzwa chikamu chidiki (7).

(9) Dare rinoumbwa pasi pechikamu chino ringori nekodzero nemasimba zvakafanana nezvenhengo dzinosarudzwa pasi peMutemo wemakomisheni wekuongorora nyaya [Chitsauko 10:07], kana mumwewo mutemo unotsiva uyu.

(10) Kana nyaya yekubviswa kwemutongi pachinzvimbo yaendeswa kudare rinoongorora sezvakatarwa muchikamu chino, mutongi iyeye anombomiswa basa kusvikira Mutungamiriri weNyika, akurudzirwa nedare, amudzosa pabasa kana kuti amubvisa zvachose.

(11) Mutemo weParamende unokwanisa kupa Komisheni inoona nezveutongi kana dare rinoumbwa pasi pechikamu chino, masimba ekuti mutongi chero upi zvake aunze matsamba ekuongororwa utano neboka rinoumbirwa izvozvo, kuitira kuona kuti utano hwake hwenyama nehwepfungwa hwakamira sei.

188 Mashandiro, mubhadharo uye urefu hwenguva yekuva pabasa kwevashandi vemumatare edzimhosva

(1) Vatongi vanofanira kuwaniswa muhoro, dzimwe mari dzekushanda kwavo nezvimwewo uye izvi zvese zvinodzikwa neKomisheni inoona nezvevatongi uye yapihwa mvumo neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekunge anzwawo pfungwa dzeGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemutemo uye, zvakurudzirwa neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemari.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutaura nezvemashandiro uye muripo wevanoita zveutongi asi vasiri vatongi, uye unofanira kuona kuti kukwidziridzwa, kuchinjwa nzvimbo yekushandira, uye kuburitswa basa kana mamwewo matanho ekurangwa anotorwa kwavari zvinoitika—

(a)

zvichitevera mvumo yeKomisheni inoona nezveutongi; uye

(b) nenzira isina tsvete, iri pachena, yakajeka uye pasina kutya, chizivano kana kusarudza.

(3) Muhoro, imwewo mari nezvimwewo zvinowanikwa nevatongi zvinobva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende.

(4) Muhoro, imwewo mari nezvimwewo zvinowanikwa nevatongi hazvifaniri kuderedzwa pavanenge vachiri pazvigaro. GANHURO 3 Komisheni Inoona nezveUtongi 189 Kudzikwa kweKomisheni inoona nezvemabasa eutongi nenhengo dzekomisheni iyi

(1) Kune komisheni inoona nezveutongi inosanganisira—

(a)

Mukuru wevatongi;

(b) Mutevedzeri weMukuru wevatongi;

(c)

Mutongi mukuru weDare redzimhosva repamusoro;

(d) mutongi mumwe chete anosarudzwa neDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro, Dare redzimhosva repamusoro, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi uye Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri;

(e)

Gweta Guru reHurumende;

(f)

Mukuru wemamejasitiriti;

(g) Sachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende;

(h) magweta matatu anomiririra vanhu uye ava nemakore manomwe kana kudarika ari magweta uyezve anenge asarudzwa nesangano rinoumbwa pasi pemutemo weParamende, rinomiririra magweta emuZimbabwe;

(i)

muzvinafundo kana mudzidzisi mukuru wezvemutemo anosarudzwa nesangano rinomiririra ruzhinji rwevadzidzisi vemutemo vemayunivhesiti emuZimbabwe uye kana kusina sangano rakadaro, vanenge vasarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika;

(j)

munhu mumwe chete ava nemakore manomwe kana kudarika achishanda semunhu anoona nezvekufambiswa kwemari pakambani kana muHurumende, uyo anosarudzwa nesangano rinoumbwa pasi pemutemo weParamende, rinomiririra vanhu ivavo; uye

(k) munhu mumwe chete ava nemakore manomwe kana kudarika achiita basa rakanangana nevashandi pakambani kana muHurumende, anosarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Mukuru wevatongi, kana kuti paanenge asipo, mutevedzeri weMukuru wevatongi ndiye anotungamirira misangano yese yeKomisheni inoona nezveutongi, uye kana vese vasipo pamusangano chero upi zvawo, vanenge varipo vanosarudza mumwe chete wavo kuti atungamirire musangano iwoyo.

(3) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezveutongi dzataurwa nezvadzo mundima dzinoti (d), (h), (i), (j) ne (k) dzechikamu chidiki (1) dzinosarudzwa kuti dzishande kwemakore matanhatu chete sezvo pasina mumwe mukana wekuti dzisarudzwezve kuti dzishande zvakare. 190 Mabasa eKomisheni inoona nezveutongi

(1) Komisheni inoona nezveutongi inogona kupa Hurumende mazano pamusoro pechinhu chero chipi zvacho chine chekuita neutongi kana kutongwa zviri pachena kwevanhu, uye Hurumende inofanira kukoshesa zvainenge yaudzwa.

(2) Komisheni inoona nezveutongi inofanira kukurudzira uye kuita kuti pave nekuzvimiririra uye kutambira kutsanangura mashandiro kweutongi uyezve, kuti pave nekutonga kwakanaka, kunonyatsoshanda uye kuri pachena muZimbabwe, uye ine masimba ese anodikanwa pakuita kuti zvese izvi zvivepo.

(3) Komisheni inoona nezveutongi, mushure mekupihwa mvumo neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemutemo, inokwanisa kugadzira mitemo nechinangwa chero chipi zvacho chiri muchikamu chino.

(4) Mutemo weParamende unokwanisa kupa Komisheni inoona nezveutongi basa rakanangana nekupinza basa, kuranga uye kuona muripo nemashandiro evanhu vanoshandira Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro, Dare redzimhosva repamusoro, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzevashandi nevashandirwi, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvenyaya dzeUtungamiriri uye mamwewo matare. 191 Kuita basa zvakaenzanirana uye zviri pachena kweKomisheni inoona nezveutongi Komisheni inoona nezveutongi inofanira kuita basa rayo nenzira yakaenzanirana, isina tsvete uye iri pachena.

GANHURO 4 Zvakasiyana-siyana 192 Mutemo unofanira kushandiswa Mutemo unofanira kushandiswa nematare edzimhosva muZimbabwe mutemo wakatanga kushanda pazuva rawakanzi wava kushanda, pachitariswa zvakare kunatsurudzwa kwawakazoitwa. 193 Kutonga mhosva dzinenge dzaparwa kwematare Matare edzimhosva anotevera ndiwo chete anokwanisa kana kubvumirwa kutonga mhosva dzinenge dzaparwa—

(a)

2013

Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro, Dare redzimhosva repamusoro uye matare anoshanda nemamejasitiriti;

(b) dare rinotonga mhosva pasi pemutemo wekugadzirisa vanhu, zvichisvika pakakodzera kuti munhu arangwe zvichienderana nemurango waanofanira kutemerwa. CHITSAUKO 9 Zvinofanira Kutevedzwa Pakutungamirirwa kweRuzhinji rweVanhu 194 Zvinofanira kukosheswa pakutungamirirwa kweruzhinji rwevanhu

(1) Kutungamirirwa kweruzhinji rwevanhu pachidanho chero chipi zvacho chehurumende, kusanganisira mapoka nevamiririri veNyika nehurumende, mapoka ari pasi pehurumende nemamwe mapoka anoona nezveruzhinji rwevanhu kunofanira kutungamirirwa kuchikoshesa kodzero dzeruzhinji rwevanhu dzakatarwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino, kusanganisira zvinotevera—

(a)

mashandiro akanaka epamusorosoro anofanira kukurudzirwa uye kuitwa nguva dzese;

(b) kushandiswa kwemari nehumwe upfumi nenzira yakanaka, pasina kupfachura uye zvinonyatsoburitsa shanduko, kunofanira kukurudzirwa;

(c)

kutungamirirwa kweruzhinji rwevanhu kunofanira kuva nechinangwa chekuunza budiriro;

(d) zvinofanira kuwaniswa ruzhinji rwevanhu zvinofanira kuwaniswa nenzira isingarerekeri divi rimwe chete, yakaenzanirana uye isingadzvanyiriri vamwe vanhu;

(e)

zvido zveruzhinji rwevanhu zvinofanira kugadziriswa nenguva yakafanira uye ruzhinji rwevanhu runofanira kukurudzirwa kuti ruve nechekuita pakugadzirwa kweMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa;

(f)

kutungamirirwa kweruzhinji rwevanhu kunofanira kutsanagurwa kuParamemende uye kuruzhinji rwevanhu;

(g) mapoka nevamiririri vehurumende padanho chero ripi zvaro, vanofanira kushanda pamwe chete;

(h) kuitira zvinhu pachena kunofanira kukurudzirwa kuburikidza nekuwanisa ruzhinji rwevanhu mashoko neruzivo zvinonzwisisika uye muchokwadi nenguva yakakodzera;

(i)

kushanda zvakanaka nevashandi uye mweya wekuvakwidziridza pabasa ravo, kuti vanyatsoshanda nepese pavanokwanisa, zvinofanira kukurudzirwa;

(j)

kutungamirirwa kweruzhinji rwevanhu kunofanira kunyatsomiririra vanhu vakasiyana-siyana vemuZimbabwe;

(k) kupinzwa nekudzidziswa basa kwevanhu uye kukwidziridzwa kwavo kunofanira kuitwa pachitariswa unyanzvi hwavo nenjere dzavo, zvisina tsvete, zvine ruenzaniso pakati pevanhurume nevanhukadzi, uye kunofanira kusanganisirawo vakaremara; uye Nyika inofanira kutora matanho, kusanganisira ezvemutemo, ekukurudzira zvese izvi.

(2) Kuiswa pazvinzvimbo kwevanhu pachidanho chero chipi zvacho chehurumende, kusanganisira mapoka nevamiririri vehurumende, mapoka ari pasi pehurumende, nemamwe mapoka anoona nezveruzhinji rwevanhu, kunofanira kuitwa pachitariswa unyanzvi hwevanhu ava. 195 Mabhizimisi ari pasi pehurumende

(1) Makambani kana mamwewo mabhizimisi ari pasi pehurumende, kana kuti anotungamirwa nehurumende, anofanira kutevedza zvese zviri muchikamu 194(1) asi, pamusoro paizvozvo, oita basa rawo nenzira inoita kuti arambe achishanda zvakanaka uye, achitevedza mitemo yekushanda kwemakambani.

(2) Makambani uye mamwewo mapoka anotaurwa nezvawo muchikamu chidiki (1) anofanira kuvamba nzira yekutenga nayo zvinhu yakajeka, iri pachena uye inobvumidza kukwikwidzana. 196 Mabasa evashandi vemumapazi ehurumende nezvinofanira kutevedzwa muUtungamiriri

(1) Simba rinopihwa mushandi weHurumende rinotaridza chivimbo cheruzhinji rwevanhu maari uye rinofanira kushandiswa nenzira—

(a)

inoenderana nezvinangwa zveBumbiro reMitemo rino uye zvakatarwa mariri;

(b) inotaridza kukosheswa kwevanhu uye chido chekuvabatsira kwete kuvatonga; uye

(c)

inokwidziridza chivimbo chevanhu pachinzvimbo chinoshandirwa nemushandi weHurumende.

(2) Vashandi veHurumende vanofanira kunyatsozvibata, pavanenge vachiita zvebasa uye pavanenge vasingaiti zvebasa kuitira kuti pasave nemakakatanwa pakati pezvavanoita muupenyu hwavo nezvavanoita pabasa ravo, uye havafaniri kuita zvinoshoresa zvinzvimbo zvavo.

(3) Vashandi vehurumende vanotungamirira vamwe vanofanira kuita zvinotevera, izvo zvinotarisirwa paUtungamiriri—

(a)

kukoshesa pfungwa dzevamwe nekusarerekera divi rimwe chete pakuongorora zvinhu;

(b) kuvimbika pakuita zvavanotarisirwa kuitira ruzhinji rwevanhu;

(c)

kutsanangurira ruzhinji rwevanhu zvakanangana nemashandiro uye nekusarudza zvekuita; uye

(d) kutevedzera mitemo nekuzvipira pakushanda neruzhinji rwevanhu. 197 Urefu hwenguva yekuva pazvinzvimbo kwevatungamiriri mumabhizimisi ehurumende Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutara urefu hwenguva yekuva pazvinzvimbo kwevakuru vakuru kana kuti vatungamiriri vemapoka ari pasi pehurumende nemamwe makambani eHurumende kana anotongwa neHurumende. 198 Mitemo yekuti zviri muChitsauko 9 zviitike Mutemo weParamende unofanira kudzika matanho ekuti zviri muChitsauko chino zviitike, kusanganisira matanho—

(a)

2013

anoita kuti vashandi veHurumende vataure kuti upfumi hwavo nezvavanazvo zvakamira sei;

(b) anodzika zvinofanira kutevedzwa nevashandi veHurumende pakushanda kwavo;

(c)

anotsanangura mashandiro akanaka emakambani, ayo anofanira kutevedzerwa nemakambani eHurumende kana anotongwa neHurumende;

(d) anotaura kurangwa kunoitwa vanhu vanotyora mitemo yakatarwa muChitsauko chino kana zvimwewo zvinofanira kutevedzwa kana zvakatarwa mundima (b). CHITSAUKO 10 Kushandira Hurumende 199 Kushandira hurumende

(1) Pane boka rimwe chete revashandi veHurumende, rinoita basa rekuona mafambiro enyika yeZimbabwe.

(2) Vashandi veHurumende vanosanganisira vanhu vanoshandira Hurumende kunze kwe—

(a)

nhengo dzezvikamu zvinochengetedza nyika uye zvimwewo zvinogona kuumbwa;

(b) vatongi, mamejesatiriti nevamwewo vanhu vanotungamirira matare edzimhosva anodzikwa neMutemo weParamende;

(c) nhengo dzeMakomisheni anoumbwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino;

(d) vashandi veParamende; uye

(e) mumwe munhu chero upi zvake wekuti chinzvimbo kana basa rake zvinonzi, neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana Mutemo weParamende, hazvisi chikamu chevashandi veHurumende.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutsanangura mamiriro, Utungamiriri, mitemo, unhu uye, zvichienderana nechikamu 203, mashandiro evashandi veHurumende. 200 Mashandiro evashandi veHurumende

(1) Vashandi veHurumende vanofanira kushanda sezvakatarwa muBumbiro Remitemo rino uye zvinoenderana nemitemo yenyika.

(2) Hapana mushandi weHurumende anotarisirwa kuita zvisiri pamutemo.

(3) Hapana mushandi weHurumende anotarisirwa, pakuita basa rake—

(a)

kurerekera divi rimwe chete;

(b) kukurudzira zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro;

(c)

kukanganisa zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro, rinenge richiita zviri pamutemo; kana

(d) kukanganisa kodzero dzakakosha nerusununguko rwemunhu chero upi zvake.

(4) Vashandi veHurumende havafaniri kuva nhengo dzinomira-mira kana kutora zvinzvimbo zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro.

(5) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti vashandi veHurumende vasave nebato rezvematongerwo enyika ravanotsigira.

201 Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvevashandi veHurumende Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kusarudza Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvevashandi veHurumende. 202 Maumbirwo nenhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende

(1) Kune Komisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende inosanganisira—

(a)

sachigaro nemutevedzeri wasachigaro; uye

(b) dzimwe nhengo dzisiri pasi pembiri asi dzisingapfuuri shanu; dzinosarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende dzinofanira kusarudzwa kuburikidza neruzivo rwadzo neunyanzvi hwekutungamirira nekuwanisa ruzhinji rwevanhu zvarwunoda. 203 Basa reKomisheni inoona nezvevashandi vehurumende

(1) Komisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende inoita zvinotevera—

(a)

kupinza mabasa vashandi veHurumende vakakodzera uye vanogona kushanda muHurumende;

(b) zvichienderana nezviri muchikamu 65(5), kudzika mitemo yakanangana nemashandiro evashandi veHurumende, kusanganisira mari yavanotambira nezvimwe zvavanowana nekuripwa;

(c)

kuona mashandiro anoita masimba ekuranga vashandi veHurumende;

(d) kuongorora nekugadzirisa zvichemo zvevashandi veHurumende zviri maererano nezvimwe zviitiko kana kuti zvinhu zvinenge zvisina kuitwa;

(e)

kutora matanho ekunyatsoita kuti mashandiro neupenyu hwevashandi veHurumende zvinyatsoita zvakanaka;

(f)

kuona kuti vashandi veHurumende vanyatsoita basa ravo nemazvo uye zvisina kurerekera divi rimwe chete;

(g) kuzivisa Mutungamiriri weNyika kana Gurukota reHurumende nezvenyaya chero ipi zvayo inoenderana nevashandi veHurumende;

(h) kukurudzira vashandi veHurumende vese kuti vatevedze zvakakosha zvavanofanira kuita zviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(i)

kuita zvimwe zvese zvinonzi Komisheni iite neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Komisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende, mushure mekupihwa mvumo neGurukota reHurumende rinooona nezvevashandi vehurumende, inokwanisa kudzika mirawo yakananga nechero chipi zvacho chakatarwa muchikamu chidiki (1).

(3) Komisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende inofanira kuita basa rayo ichitevedza mirawo yese yakanyorwa, yainopihwa neGurukota reHurumende rinooona nezvevashandi vehurumende.

(4) Pakutara mari inotambirwa nevashandi veHurumende uye zvimwewo zvavanowana nekuripwa, Komisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende inofanira kuita izvi yapihwa mvumo neMutungamiriri weNyika, iyewo audzwa zvekuita neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemari, uye mushure mekunzwawo zvinofungwa neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvevashandi veHurumende.

204 Vamiriri veZimbabwe kune dzimwe nyika Mutungamiriri weNyika anokwanisa kusarudza vamiriri veZimbabwe kune dzimwe nyika kana kuti kumasangano enyika dzepasi rese, uyezve anokwanisa kubvisa vanhu ava pazvigaro izvi panguva chero ipi zvayo. 205 Vanyori vakuru vemapazi ehurumende

(1) Vanyori Vakuru veMapazi eHurumende vanosarudzwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekunge ataura neKomisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende.

(2) Munyori Mukuru anokwanisa kunge ari pachinzvimbo kwemakore mashanu, uye vanyori ava vanokwanisa kunzi vashande zvakare kwenguva iyoyo kamwe chete chete, ndokunge vachinyatsogona basa ravo, vachishanda nemazvo uye pachionekwa zvavanoburitsa. CHITSAUKO 11 Mabasa eKuchengetedzwa Kwenyika GANHURO 1 Mitemo yeZvakasiyana-siyana 206 Kuchengetedzwa kwenyika

(1) Zvinangwa zvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe zvinofanira kuratidza chido chevanhu veZimbabwe chekugara mune zvakaenzanirana, murusununguko, murunyararo nemukuwirirana, pasina kutya uyezve, pachiva nebudiriro.

(2) Kuchengetedzwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe kunofanira kuwaniswa nenzira inoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino uye nemutemo wenyika.

(3) Zvikuru sei, zvese zvine chekuita nekuti pave nekuchengetedzwa kwenyika zvinofanira kuitwa pachikosheseswa—

(a)

kodzero dzakakoshesesa nerusununguko, uye zvese zvinokurudzira zvido zvevanhu zvakadzikwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino; uye

(b) kutevedzwa kwemitemo yenyika. 207 Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika

(1) Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe vanosanganisira—

(a)

mauto;

(b) mapurisa;

(c)

vanoita utikitivha muchivande;

(d) vanoona nezvekuchengetwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa; uye

(e)

boka chero ripi zvaro rinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika, rinoumbwa pasi peMutemo weParamende.

(2) Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika vanoshanda vachitevedza zvinodikanwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino, Mutungamiriri weNyika uye Dare reMakurukota eHurumende uye mabasa avo anoongororwa neParamende.

(3) Nhengo dzinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika dzinofanira kuratidza kusiyana kwakaita vanhu vemuZimbabwe vanobva kwakasiyana-siyana. 208 Mashandiro evanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika

(1) Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika vanofanira kuita basa ravo vachitevedza Bumbiro reMitemo rino uye mutemo wenyika.

(2) Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika havafaniri kuita zvinotevera pabasa ravo—

(a)

kuita basa vachiratidza kurerekera divi rimwe chete;

(b) kusimudzira zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro;

(c)

kukanganisa zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro,rinenge richiita zviri pamutemo; kana

(d) kukanganisa kodzero dzakakoshesesa nerusununguko zvemunhu chero upi zvake.

(3) Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika havafaniri kuva nhengo dzinomira-mira kana kutora zvinzvimbo zvebato kana sangano rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro.

(4) Nhengo dzezvikamu zvinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika hadzifaniri kushandira munzvimbo dzinoshandirwa neruzhinji rwevanhu kunze kwekuti iri nguva yematambudziko akaipisisa munyika. 209 Dare rinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika

(1) Kune Dare rinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika rinosanganisira Mutungamiriri weNyika-, uyo anova sachigaro, Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika uye Makurukota eHurumende nenhengo dzinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika, idzo dzinogona kudomwa muMutemo weParamende.

(2) Mabasa eDare rinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika ndeanoti—

(a)

kugadzira gwara rekuchengetedzwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe;

(b) kuudza uye kupanga mazano kuMutungamiriri weNyika maererano nenyaya dzine chekuita nekuchengetedzwa kwenyika; uye

(c)

kuita mamwe mabasa chero api zvawo anogona kupihwa neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Vatungamiriri vezvikamu zvinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika vanofanira kuudza Dare rinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika nezvemamiriro ekuchengetedzeka kweZimbabwe, pese apo Dare iri rinenge richida kuziva nezvezvinhu izvi. 210 Nzira dzisina kwadzakarerekera dzinoshandiswa pazvichemo Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti pave nenzira isina kwayakarerekera yekutambira nekuongorora zvichemo zveruzhinji rwevanhu maererano nezvakaipa zvinenge zvaitwa nenhengo dzezvikamu zvinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika, uyewo nekugadzirisa uipi hwese hunovapo nekuda kwezvinenge zvaitwa nenhengo idzi.

GANHURO 2 Mauto 211 Mauto

(1) Mauto eZimbabwe anosanganisira anoshandisa zvombo ari pasi pamwe chete neanozvishandisa ari muchadenga, kana mamwewo mauto anoumbwa pasi peMutemo weParamende.

(2) Mauto eZimbabwe ndiwo chete mauto anoshanda zviri pamutemo muZimbabwe.

(3) Mauto anofanira kukoshesa kodzero nemhando dzerusununguko dzakakoshesesa dzevanhu vese uye kusarerekera divi rimwe chete, kushanda nenyika yese, kuva nerudo nenyika, kushanda basa nemazvo uye kuzviisa pasi pehurumende yenyika inosarudzwa kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino.

(4) Mauto anofanira kuzvininipisa uye kutevedza mutemo nguva dzese.

(5) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutsanangura mamiriro, mashandiro, Utungamiriri, mitemo, kuzvibata kwakanaka uyewo kukwidziridzwa pabasa kana kudzikiswa kwenhengo dzemauto uye, zvichienderana nezviri muchikamu 218, zviri pamusoro pemashandiro enhengo dzeMauto. 212 Basa reMauto Basa reMauto nderekuchengetedza nyika yeZimbabwe, vanhu vayo, kuchengetedzeka kwayo senyika nekwezvido zvayo uye miganhu yayo, pamwe chete nekukoshesa Bumbiro reMitemo rino. 213 Kutumwa basa nekutumirwa kuhondo kweMauto

(1) Kuchitevedzwa zviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino, Mutungamiriri weNyika, seMukuru mukuru weMauto ndiye chete ane simba—

(a) rekuudza Mauto basa rekuita; kana kuti

(b) rekudzika mashandiro eMauto.
