(2) Zvichitevera mvumo yeMutungamiriri weNyika, Mauto anokwanisa kupihwa basa muZimbabwe—

(a) rekudzivirira Zimbabwe;

(b) rekubatsira Mapurisa pakuita kuti ruzhinji rwevanhu ruchengetedze mutemo; kana kuti

(c) rekubatsira Mapurisa nevamwewo vanoona nezveruzhinji rwevanhu kana pachinge paitika tsaona kana dambudziko guru.

(3) Zvichitevera mvumo yeMutungamiriri weNyika, Mauto anokwanisa kupihwa basa kunze kweZimbabwe—

(a)

rekuchengetedza runyararo mune dzimwe nyika pasi peSangano remubatanidzwa wenyika dzepasi rese, kana mamwewo masangano epasi rese, kana emudunhu anowanikwa Zimbabwe iri nhengo yawo;

(b) rekudzivirira miganhu yeimwe nyika;

(c) rekuzadzikisa zvinotarisirwa kunyika yeZimbabwe nedzimwe nyika dzepasi rese; kana kuti

(d) pakuita kuti pave nekuchengetedzeka kwenyika yeZimbabwe kana zvido zvayo.

(4) Zvichitevera chisungo chezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzese dzeParamende, pamusangano wemubatanidzwa weSeneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, Paramende inogona kutaura kuti kutumirwa kunze kweZimbabwe kwemauto kusaitwe.

(5) Kana Paramende yataura kuti kutumirwa kunze kweZimbabwe kwemauto ngakusaitwe, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kutora matanho ese anogoneka ekudzosera mauto munyika, achikoshesesa kuchengeteka kwemauto nezvombo zveZimbabwe. 214 Kutsanangura kutumwa basa kweMauto nevari mune zvematongerwo enyika Kana Mauto akatumwa basa—

(a)

muZimbabwe kuti abatsire pakuchengetedzwa kwemutemo; kana kuti

(b) kunze kweZimbabwe; Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuita kuti Paramende iziviswe, nekukurumidza uye ichiudzwa zvese zvakakosha, nezvikonzero zvekutuma Mauto basa uye—

(i) kana atumwa basa muZimbabwe, nzvimbo yaanenge achishandira ari;

(ii) kana atumwa basa kunze kweZimbabwe, nyika yaanenge achishandira ari. 215 Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveMauto Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kudoma Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveMauto. 216 Kutungamirirwa kweMauto

(1) Mutemo weParamende unokwanisa kuita kuti—

(a)

Mauto atungamirirwe neMutungamiri mumwe chete; kana kuti

(b) chikamu chimwe nechimwe cheMauto, kana kuti zviviri kana kupfuura zviri pamwe chete, zvitungamirirwe nemumwewo Mutungamiriri.

(2) Mutungamiriri weMauto wese uye Mutungamiriri wechikamu cheMauto wese, anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekunzwa pfungwa dzeGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveMauto.

(3) Vatungamiriri veMauto neVatungamiriri vezvikamu zveMauto vanosarudzwa kuti vashande kwenguva isingadariki makore mashanu uye munhu haafaniri kushanda pane chimwe chete chezvinzvimbo izvi kanopfuura kaviri.

(4) Munhu anenge amboshanda seMutungamiriri wechikamu cheMauto anokwanisa kushanda zvakare seMutungamiriri weMauto, asi Munhu anenge amboshanda seMutungamiriri weMauto haakwanisi kushanda zvakare seMutungamiriri wechikamu cheMauto, kana kutungamirira chimwe chikamu chero chipi zvacho chinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika.

(5) Mutungamiriri weMauto wese, uye Mutungamiriri wechikamu cheMauto wese anofanira kuita basa reutungamiriri hwake achitevedza gwara rakanyorwa neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemauto, richitevedzera zvinenge zvataurwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

217 Maumbirwo nenhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezveMauto

(1) Kune Komisheni inoona nezveMauto, inosanganisira sachigaro, uyo anofanira kunge ari sachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende, uye dzimwe nhengo mbiri kana kudarika asi dzisingapfuuri nhanhatu, dzinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezveMauto dzinofanira kusarudzwa nekuda kweruzivo rwadzo kana unyanzvi munyaya dzezveUtungamiriri, mafambisirwo ebasa, zvakanangana nehondo, ruzivo rwebasa kana kuti kungokodzera kwadzo kuti dzidomwe, uye—

(a)

chikamu chepakati nepakati chenhengo idzi chinofanira kunge chiri chevanhu vasiri, uye vasina kumbova, Mauto;

(b) mumwe chete wadzo kana kudarika anofanira kunge akambenge ane chinzvimbo chikuru cheMauto kamwe chete kana kupfuura uye kwenguva inosvika makore mashanu. 218 Basa reKomisheni inoona nezveMauto

(1) Komisheni inoona nezveMauto inoita zvinotevera—

(a) kudoma vanhu vakadzidzira basa uye vanonyatsorigona kuti vatore mabasa

kana zvinzvimbo muChiuto;

(b) kudzika nekugadzira mitemo yemashandiro enhengo dzeMauto, kusanganisira

muhoro, dzimwe mari dzavanotambira nezvimwe zvavanowana;

(c) kuona kuti mararamiro neutungamiri hweMauto zvakamira sei uye kuita kuti

Mauto arambe achiita basa nemazvo uye zvemhando yepamusoro;

(d) kuona kuti nhengo dzeMauto dzaita zvinoenderana nechikamu 208;

(e) kuita kuti pave nekudyidzana uye kunzwisisana pakati peMauto neruzhinji rwevanhu;

(f) kupanga Mutungamiriri weNyika uye Gurukota reHurumende mazano ane chekuita nenyaya chero ipi zvayo ine chekuita neMauto; uye

(g) kuita rimwe basa rese rinopihwa Komisheni iyi neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Komisheni inoona nezveMauto, mushure mekupihwa mvumo neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemauto, inokwanisa kudzika mitemo nechinangwa chekuita zvakatarwa muchikamu chidiki (1).

(3) Pakutara muhoro wenhengo dzemauto uye zvimwewo zvavanowana nekuripwa, Komisheni inoona nezveMauto inofanira kuita izvi yapihwa mvumo neMutungamiriri weNyika, iyewo audzwa zvekuita neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemari, uye mushure mekunzwawo zvinofungwa neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveMauto. GANHURO 1 Mapurisa 219 Mapurisa nebasa rawo

(1) Kune Mapurisa anoita zvinotevera—

(a)

kuferefeta, kuongorora nekudzivirira mhosva;

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

kuona nezvekuchengetedzeka kwenyika yeZimbabwe; kuchengetedza upenyu neupfumi hwevanhu; kuita kuti pave nekutevedzwa kwemutemo; uye kutevedza Bumbiro reMitemo rino nekushandisa mutemo pasina kutya kana tsvete.

(2) Mapurisa anofanira kuita basa rawo achishanda pamwe chete ne—

(a)

chikamu chese chinoita zveutikitivha muchivande, chinoumbwa nemutemo;

(b) boka chero ripi zvaro rinoumbwa nemutemo nechinangwa chekuferefeta, kuongorora nekudzivirira dzimwe mhando dzemhosva; uye

(c) mapoka emudunhu neepasi rese anoumbwa kuti apedze kuparwa kwemhosva.

(3) Mapurisa anofanira kunge asina divi raakarerekera, achishanda nenyika yese zvayo, ane rudo nenyika, achishanda basa nemazvo uye achizviisa pasi pehurumende yenyika inosarudzwa kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino.

(4) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutsanangura mamiriro, mashandiro, Utungamiriri, mitemo, kuzvibata zvine unhu uye kukwidziridzwa kana kudzikisirwa pabasa kwemapurisa, zvichienderana nechikamu 223, mashandiro eMapurisa. 220 Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveMapurisa Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kudoma Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveMapurisa. 221 Mukuru mukuru wemapurisa

(1) Mapurisa anotungamirirwa neMukuru-mukuru wemapurisa, uyo anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika, mushure mekunzwa pfungwa dzeGurukota rinoona nezvemapurisa.

(2) Mukuru-mukuru weMapurisa anodomwa kunzi ashande kwemakore mashanu, ayo anokwanisa kungowedzerwa kamwe chete.

(3) Munhu anenge amboshanda seMukuru-mukuru weMapurisa haakwanisi kudomwa zvakare kuti atungamirire chimwe chikamu chero chipi zvacho chinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika.

(4) Mukuru-mukuru weMapurisa anofanira kuita Utungamiriri hwake achitevedza gwara rakanyorwa neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveMapurisa, richitevedzera zvinenge zvataurwa neMutungamiriri weNyika. 222 Maumbirwo nenhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezveMapurisa

(1) Kune Komisheni inoona nezveMapurisa inosanganisira sachigaro, uyo anofanira kunge ari sachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende, uye dzimwe nhengo mbiri kana kudarika asi dzisingapfuuri nhanhatu, dzinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezveMapurisa dzinofanira kusarudzwa nekuda kweruzivo rwadzo kana unyanzvi munyaya dzezvekuchengetedzwa kwemutemo, zveUtungamiriri, mafambisirwo ebasa, ruzivo rwebasa kana kuti kungokodzera kwadzo kuti dzidomwe, uye—

(a)

chikamu chepakati nepakati chenhengo idzi chinofanira kunge chiri chevanhu vasiri, uye vasina kumbova, Mapurisa;

(b) mumwe chete kana kudarika wenhengo idzi anofanira kunge akambenge ane chinzvimbo chikuru muChipurisa kamwe chete kana kupfuura uye kwenguva inosvika makore mashanu. 223 Basa reKomisheni inoona nezveMapurisa

(1) Komisheni inoona nezveMapurisa inoita zvinotevera—

(a)

kudoma vanhu vakadzidzira basa uye vanonyatsorigona kuti vatore mabasa kana zvinzvimbo muChipurisa;

(b) kudzika nekugadzira mitemo yemashandiro enhengo dzeMapurisa, kusanganisira muhoro, dzimwe mari dzavanotambira nezvimwe zvavanowana;

(c)

kuona kuti mararamiro neutungamiri hweMapurisa zvakamira sei uye kuita kuti arambe achiita basa nemazvo uye zvemhando yepamusoro;

(d) kuona kuti nhengo dzeMapurisa dzaita zvinoenderana nechikamu 208;

(e)

kuita kuti pave nekudyidzana uye kunzwisisana pakati peMapurisa neruzhinji rwevanhu;

(f)

kuzivisa Mutungamiriri weNyika uye Gurukota reHurumende nezvenyaya chero ipi zvayo ine chekuita neMapurisa; uye

(g) kuita rimwe basa rese rinopihwa Komisheni iyi neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Komisheni inoona nezveMapurisa, mushure mekupihwa mvumo neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveMapurisa, inokwanisa kudzika mitemo nechinangwa chekuita zvakatarwa muchikamu chidiki (1).

(3) Pakutara muhoro wenhengo dzeMapurisa uye zvimwewo zvavanowana nekuripwa, Komisheni inoona nezveMapurisa inofanira kuita izvi yapihwa mvumo neMutungamiriri weNyika, iyewo audzwa zvekuita neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemari, uye mushure mekunzwawo zvinofungwa neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveMapurisa. GANHURO 4 Vanoita Utikitivha Muchivande 224 Kuumbwa kwechikamu chevanoita utikitivha muchivande

(1) Chikamu cheNyika chero chipi zvacho chevanoita utikitivha muchivande, chisiri boka reutikitivha hweMauto kana Mapurisa, chinofanira kuumbwa nemutemo kana kuti zvabva kuMutungamiriri weNyika kana Dare reMakurukota eHurumende.

(2) Chikamu cheNyika chero chipi zvacho chevanoita utikitivha muchivande chinofanira kunge chisina divi rachakarerekera, chichishanda nenyika yese zvayo, chine rudo nenyika, chichishanda basa nemazvo uye chichizviisa pasi pehurumende yenyika inosarudzwa kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino. 225 Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvevanoita utikitivha muchivande Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kudoma Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvevanoita utikitivha muchivande.

226 Utungamiriri hwevanoita utikitivha muchivande

(1) Chikamu chinoita utikitivha muchivande chinofanira kutungamirirwa neMukuru-mukuru wevanoita utikitivha muchivande, uyo anofanira kudomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kuti ashande kwemakore mashanu, uye anogona kunzi ashande zvakare kwemamwe makore mashanu kamwe chete.

(2) Mukuru mukuru wevanoita utikitivha muchivande anofanira kuita Utungamiriri hwake achitevedza zvakanyorwa neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvevanoita utikitivha muchivande, richitevedzera zvinenge zvataurwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(3) Munhu anenge amboshanda seMukuru mukuru wevanoita utikitivha muchivande haakwanisi kudomwa zvakare kuti atungamirire chimwe chikamu chero chipi zvacho chinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika. GANHURO 5 Vanoona nezveKuchengetedzwa neKudzorerwa Mugwara kweVasungwa 227 Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa uye basa ravo

(1) Kune Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa vanofanira kuita zvinotevera—

(a)

kuchengetedza ruzhinji rwevanhu kubva kuvapari vemhosva, kuburikidza nekuvaisa muusungwa uye kuumba unhu hutsva, kwevanenge vabatwa nemhosva nevamwewo vanofanira kuiswa muusungwa zviri pamutemo, uyezve nekuzovadzosera zvakare munyika kuti vagarisane nevamwe; uye

(b) kutungamirira nzvimbo dzinochengeta vapari vemhosva.

(2) Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa vanofanira kunge vasina divi ravakarerekera, vachishanda nenyika yese zvayo, vane rudo nenyika, vachishanda basa nemazvo uye vachizviisa pasi pehurumende yenyika inosarudzwa kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino

(3) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutsanangura mamiriro, mashandiro, Utungamiriri, mitemo, kuzvibata zvine unhu uye kukwidziridzwa kana kudzikisirwa pabasa kweVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa, uye zvichienderana nechikamu 231, mashandiro eVanoona nezvekuchengetwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa. 228 Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kudoma Gurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa. 229 Mukuru mukuru weVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa

(1) Vanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa vanotungamirirwa neMukuru mukuru wavo, uyo anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekunzwa pfungwa dzeGurukota rinoona nezveVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa.

(2) Mukuru mukuru weVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa anodomwa kunzi ashande kwemakore mashanu, uye anogona kunzi ashande zvakare kwenguva iyi kamwe chete.

(3) Munhu anenge amboshanda seMukuru mukuru weVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa haakwanisi kudomwa zvakare kuti atungamirire chimwe chikamu chero chipi zvacho chinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa kwenyika.

(4) Mukuru mukuru weVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa anofanira kuita Utungamiriri hwake achitevedza zvakanyorwa neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa, richitevedzera zvinenge zvataurwa neMutungamiriri weNyika. 230 Maumbirwo nenhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa

(1) Kune Komisheni inoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa inosanganisira sachigaro, uyo anofanira kunge ari sachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende, uye dzimwe nhengo mbiri kana kudarika asi dzisingapfuuri nhanhatu, dzinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezveVanoona nezvekuchengetwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa dzinofanira kusarudzwa nekuda kweruzivo rwadzo kana unyanzvi pane zvekuchengetedzwa kwemutemo, Utungamiriri hwadzo, kana ruzivo rwebasa kana kuti kungokodzera kuti dzidomwe kwadzo, uye—

(a)

chikamu chepakati nepakati chenhengo idzi chinofanira kunge chiri chevanhu vasiri, uye vasina kumbova, nhengo dzeVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa;

(b) mumwe chete kana kudarika wenhengo idzi anofanira kunge akambenge ane chinzvimbo chikuru mubasa reVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa kamwe chete kana kupfuura uye kwenguva inosvika makore mashanu. 231 Basa reKomisheni inoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa

(1) Komisheni inoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa inoita zvinotevera—

(a)

kudoma vanhu vakadzidzira basa uye vanonyatsorigona kuti vatore mabasa kana zvinzvimbo mubasa reVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa;

(b) kudzika nekugadzira mitemo yemashandiro enhengo dzeVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa, kusangisira muhoro, dzimwe mari dzavanotambira nezvimwe zvavanowana;

(c)

kuona kuti mararamiro neutungamiri hweVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa zvakamira sei uye kuita kuti varambe vachiita basa nemazvo uye zvemhando yepamusoro;

(d) kuona kuti nhengo dzeVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa dzaita zvinoenderana nezviri muchikamu 208;

(e)

kuita kuti pave nekudyidzana uye kunzwisisana pakati peVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa neruzhinji rwevanhu;

(f)

kuzivisa Mutungamiriri weNyika uye Gurukota reHurumende nezvenyaya chero ipi zvayo ine chekuita neVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa; uye

(g) kuita rimwe basa rese rinopihwa Komisheni iyi neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Komisheni inoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa, mushure mekupihwa mvumo neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezveVanoona nezvekuchengetwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa, inokwanisa kudzika mitemo nechinangwa chekuita zvakatarwa muchikamu chidiki (1).

(3) Pakutara muhoro wenhengo dzeVanoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa uye zvimwewo zvavanowana nekuripwa, Komisheni inoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa inofanira kuita izvi yapihwa mvumo neMutungamiriri weNyika, iyewo audzwa zvekuita neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvemari, uye mushure mekunzwawo zvinofungwa neGurukota reHurumende rinoona nezvekuchengetedzwa nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa. CHITSAUKO 12 Makomisheni akaZvimiririra Anotsigira Gwara Rinotevedza Zvido zveRuzhinji rweVanhu GANHURO 1 Zvakasiyana-siyana 232 MaKomisheni akazvimiririra Aya ndiwo Makomisheni akazvimiririra—

(a)

Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe;

(b) Komisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe;

(c)

Komisheni inoona nezvevanhurume nevanhukadzi muZimbabwe;

(d) Komisheni inoona nezvekufambiswa kwemashoko muZimbabwe; uye

(e)

Komisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana munyika.

233 Zvinangwa zvemaKomisheni akazvimiririra MaKomisheni akazvimiririra ane zvinangwa zvakasiyana-siyana zvinotevera, izvo zvinowedzeredzwa nezvimwe zvaanopihwa, zvakanangana neKomisheni imwe neimwe—

(a)

kukurudzira nekusimbisa kodzero dzevanhu nezvido zveruzhinji rwevanhu;

(b) kuchengetedza kuzvitonga nezvido zvevanhu;

(c)

kukurudzira kushandiswa kwemitemo iri muBumbiro reMitemo;

(d) kukurudzira kuitira zvinhu pachena nekuitwa kwemabasa nemazvo mumapazi eHurumende;

(e)

kuchengetedza kutevedzwa kwezvido zveruzhinji rwevanhu neHurumende uye mapoka ese pamwe chete nevamiririri veHurumende padanho chero ripi zvaro, uyezve nemapoka anoshanda ari pasi peHurumende; uye

(f)

kuona kuti zvakaipa zvinoitirwa vanhu zvagadziriswa.

234 Vashandi vemaKomisheni akazvimiririra Zvichienderana nezvinodikanwa pamutemo, makomisheni akazvimiririra ane simba rekupinza vanhu basa nekuona zviri maererano nemashandiro avanofanira kuita. 235 Rusununguko rwemaKomisheni akazvimiririra

(1) Makomisheni akazvimiririra—

(a)

akazvimiririra uye haatungamirirwi kana kurairwa chero naani zvake;

(b) anofanira kuita basa rawo zvinoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino; uye

(c)

anofanira kuita basa rawo zvisina kutya, tsvete kana ruvengo;

kunyangwe zvazvo achifanira kupa tsananguro inogutsa yemashandiro awo kuParamende.

(2) Hurumende nemapoka ese nevamiririri veHurumende padanho chero ripi zvaro vanofanira, kuburikidza nemitemo nemamwe matanho, kubatsira makomisheni akazvimiririra kuti achengetedze rusununguko rwawo, kusarerekera kudivi rimwe chete, kururama, nekuita basa nemazvo.

(3) Hakuna munhu anotarisirwa kukanganisa mashandiro emaKomisheni akazvimiririra. 236 Nhengo dzemaKomisheni akazvimiririra hadzifaniri kupinda mune zvematongerwo enyika

(1) Mukuita basa kwadzo, nhengo dzemakomisheni akazvimiririra hadzifaniri—

(a)

kurerekera divi rimwe chete;

(b) kukurudzira zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro;

(c)

kukanganisa zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro rinenge richiita zviri pamutemo; kana

(d) kukanganisa kodzero dzakakoshesesa nerusununguko rwemunhu chero upi zvake.

(2) Pavanopinda basa muKomisheni yakazvimiririra, vanhu vanenge vari nhengo dzebato kana sangano rezvematongerwo enyika vanofanira kusiya unhengo hwavo nekukasika, uye kunyangwe zvidii, vanofanira kusiya unhengo uhu mukati memazuva makumi matatu ekupinda basa.

(3) Kana nhengo yeKomisheni yakazvimiririra—

(a)

ikave nhengo yebato kana sangano rezvematongerwo enyika; kana kuti

(b) munhu akange ari nhengo yebato kana sangano rezvematongerwo enyika pakupinda basa muKomisheni akaramba ari nhengo yebato kana sangano iri kwemazuva makumi matatu ekunge apihwa basa; anobva angorega kuva nhengo yeKomisheni iyi chiriporipocho. 237 Kugadzwa nekubviswa pazvinzvimbo kwenhengo dzemaKomisheni akazvimiririra

(1) Pakusarudzwa kwevanhu vanogona kugadzwa senhengo dzeKomisheni yakazvimiririra chero ipi zvayo, Komiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa inofanira kuita izvi—

(a)

kushambadzira chinzvimbo ichi;

(b) kukoka veruzhinji kuti vadome mazita evanhu vanokwanisa kutora chinzvimbo ichi;

(c)

kubvunzurudza vanenge vachida kutora chinzvimbo ichi ruzhinji rwevanhu ruchinzwa;

(d) kugadzira gwaro rine uwandu hwemazita evanhu vanodikanwa kuti vasarudzwe pachinzvimbo ichi hwakakodzera; uye

(e)

kuendesa gwaro remazita iri kuMutungamiriri weNyika;

(2) Nhengo yeKomisheni yakazvimiririra inogona kubviswa pachigaro nekuda kwezvikonzero izvi chete—

(a)

kutadza kuita basa rayo nekuda kwekurwara kwemuviri kana kukanganisika pfungwa;

(b) kutadza basa zvakanyanya;

(c)

kubatwa nemhosva yekuita zvisingatarisirwi zvachose pabasa rayo;

(d) kunge isisakodzeri kupihwa chigaro muKomisheni inenge ichitaurwa nezvayo.

(3) Zvinotevedzwa pakubviswa kwevatongi pazvigaro ndizvo zvinotevedzwawo pakubviswa pachigaro kwenhengo yeKomisheni yakazvimiririra. GANHURO 2 Komisheni inoona nezveSarudzo muZimbabwe 238 Kudzikwa kweKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe nenhengo dzeKomisheni iyi

(1) Kune komisheni ichazivikanwa seKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe uye ichange ine vanhu ava—

(a)

sachigaro anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekubvunza pfungwa dzeKomisheni inoona nezveUtongi neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa; uye

(b) dzimwe nhengo sere dzinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kubva pamazita asiri pasi pegumi nemaviri evanhu vakasarudzwa neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(2) Sachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe anofanira kunge ari mutongi kana kuti akamboshanda semutongi kana kuti ari munhu akakodzera kuva mutongi.

(3) Kana kudomwa kwasachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo kusingawirirani nemaonero eKomisheni inoona nezveUtongi, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuita kuti veKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa vaziviswe nekukasika.

(4) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe dzinofanira kunge dziri vagari vemuZimbabwe uye dzinosarudzirwa kuvimbika nekuva nemakore akawanda pamabasa uyezve nekuda kwekugona kwavo basa muHurumende kana mumakambani akazvimirira.

(5) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe dzinopihwa basa kwemakore matanhatu uye dzinogona kudzorerwa pabasa kwemamwe makore matanhatu, asi hakuna munhu anobvumirwa kushandira Komisheni kana amboita nhengo kwemakore gumi nemaviri akatevedzana kana kuti asina kutevedzana.

239 Mabasa eKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inoita mabasa anotevera—

(a)

kugadzirira, kuitisa, nekutungamirira kuitwa kwe—

(i) sarudzo dzekutsvaga Mutungamiriri weNyika nenhengo dzeParamende;

(ii) sarudzo dzemakanzuru ematunhu makuru, makanzuru emaguta nemakanzuru ekumaruwa neenharaunda;

(iii) sarudzo dzenhengo dzeDare reMadzishe sezvakapihwa nechikamu 285; uye

(iv) mareferenda; uye kuona kuti sarudzo idzi nemareferenda zvaitwa nekukasika, zvakasununguka, zvakaenzanirana, zviri pachena uye kuchitevedzwa zviri pamutemo.

(b) kutungamirira sarudzo dzeMutungamiriri weSeneti nedzeMutauriri uye kuona kuti sarudzo idzi dzaitwa nekukasika uye kuchitevedzwa zviri pamutemo;

(c)

kunyoresa vanhu vachavhota;

(d) kunyora mazita mumagwaro evavhoti kana kuongorora mabasa ekuunganidzwa kwemazita;

(e)

kuona kuti magwaro emazita evavhoti achengetedzwa zvakanaka;

(f)

kutara miganhu yematunhu esarudzo, mawadhi nemimwe miganhu inodiwa pakuitwa kwesarudzo;

(g) kugadzira, kudhinda, kuendesa kunzvimbo dzakasiyana-siyana mapepa ekuvhotesa, kubvumira rudzi rwemabhokisi ekuvhotera rwuchashandiswa, kutenga mabhokisi ekuvhotera, kugadzira nekufambisa basa panzvimbo dzekuvhotera;

(h) kudzidzisa vanhu maererano nezvekuvhota nekutungamirira kuitwa kwebasa iri;

(i)

kunyoresa vanoona nezvekufamba kwesarudzo nemareferenda;

(j)

kuudza vanhu vanoshandira Hurumende kana makanzuru maitirwo ebasa kuitira kuti sarudzo kana referenda zviitwe nekukasika, nenzira kwayo yakasununguka, yakaenzanirana uye iri pachena; uye

(k) kutambira nekuongorora zvichemo zvinobva kuruzhinji rwevanhu uye kutora matanho pamusoro pezvichemo nenzira inoonekwa yakakodzera neKomisheni. 240 Vasingabvumirwe kupihwa basa muKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe Pamusoro pevanhu vataurwa nezvavo muchikamu 320(3), vanhu vanotevera havabvumirwi kupihwa basa muKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe—

(a)

vashandi vehurumende, kusiya kwevatongi;

(b) vashandi vemakanzuru ematunhu makuru, maguta nemakanzuru ekumaruwa nenharaunda; uye

(c)

nhengo nevashandi vemapoka ezvemitemo nemapoka anoshanda ari pasi peHurumende.

241 Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kupa ripoti maererano nesarudzo Nemareferenda Pamusoro peripoti yainofanira kupa kana kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 323, Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe inofanira kukurumidza uye kuburikidza neGurukota rakakodzera, kupa ripoti kuParamende maererano nesarudzo kana referenda imwe neimwe. GANHURO 3 Komisheni inoona nezveKodzero dzeVanhu muZimbabwe 242 Kudzikwa kweKomisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe nenhengo dzeKomisheni iyi

(1) Kune komisheni ichazivikanwa seKomisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe inoumbwa nevanhu ava—

(a)

sachigaro anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekubvunza pfungwa dzeKomisheni inoona nezveUtongi neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa; uye

(b) dzimwe nhengo sere dzinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kubva pamazita asiri pasi pegumi nemaviri evanhu vakasarudzwa neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(2) Sachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe anofanira kunge ari munhu ava nemakore manomwe kana kupfuura akakodzera kushanda segweta muZimbabwe.

(3) Kana kudomwa kwasachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe kusingawirirani nemaonero eKomisheni inoona nezveUtongi, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuita kuti veKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa vaziviswe nekukasika.

(4) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe dzinofanira kusarudzirwa kuvimbika, ruzivo nekunzwisisa zvekodzero, nemakore mazhinji ekushanda mune zvekukurudzira kodzero dzevanhu. 243 Mabasa eKomisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe

(1) Komisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe inoita mabasa anotevera—

(a)

kukurudzira ruzivo nekuremekedzwa kwekodzero dzevanhu nerusununguko pamatanho ese munyika;

(b) kukurudzira kuchengetedzwa, kuvandudzwa nekuwaniswa kwekodzero dzevanhu nerusununguko;

(c)

kuongorora, kutarisisa nekuona kuti kodzero dzevanhu nerusununguko zviri kutevedzwa here;

(d) kutambira nekuongorora zvichemo zvinobva kuruzhinji pamwe chete nekutora matanho anoonekwa akakodzera neKomisheni pamusoro pezvichemo izvi;

(e)

kuchengetedza ruzhinji rwevanhu kubva mukushandisirwa simba nekutungamirirwa zvisizvo neHurumende, mapazi ehurumende pamwe chete nevanoshanda mumapazi aya;

(f)

2013

kuongorora mashandiro emapazi ese ehurumende kana vanhu, pese panenge panzi pane kukanganiswa kwekodzero dzevanhu nerusununguko zviri muSumbu reKodzero;

(g) kuona kuti pawanikwa nzira dzakakodzera dzekugadzirisa vakanganisirwa, kusanganisira kukurudzira kusungwa nekutongwa kwevanokanganisa kodzero nerusununguko;

(h) kuraira Komishina Mukuru wemapurisa kuti aferefete nyaya dzevanofungirwa kuti vari kupara mhosva dzine chekuita nekukanganiswa kwekodzero dzevanhu nerusununguko uye kuti ape ripoti kuKomisheni iyi pamusoro pezvinenge zvabuda mukuferefeta kwake;

(i)

kupa Paramende mazano angabatsira pakukurudzira kodzero dzevanhu nerusununguko;

(j)

kuita tsvakurudzo panyaya dzine chekuita nekodzero dzevanhu nekuenzaniswa pamberi pemutemo; uye

(k) kushanyira nekuoongorora—

(i) majeri, nzvimbo dzeusungwa, nzvimbo dzinogarwa nevapoteri nedzimwe dzakadaro; uye

(ii) nzvimbo dziri kuchengeterwa vanorwara nepfungwa kana vane pfungwa dzisinganyatsoshanda zvakanaka;

kuitira kuona magariro ari kuita vakachengeterwa munzvimbo idzi uye kuti vape mazano pamusoro pemagariro iwayo kuGurukota rinoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa munzvimbo idzi

(2) Komishina Mukuru wemapurisa anofanira kuteerera chero zvipi zvaanenge anzi aite neKomisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu pasi pechikamu chidiki (1)(h). 244 Maripoti anotambirwa uye anoitwa neKomisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe

(1) Komisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu inogona kuda kuti munhu, boka kana vamiriri vanoshandira Hurumende kana rimwewo boka—

(a)

vazivise Komisheni nezvematanho avakatora kuzadzikisa kodzero nerusununguko rwevanhu sezvakapihwa muSumbu reKodzero; uye

(b) vape Komisheni ruzivo rwainoda kuti igadzire ripoti chero ipi zvayo inofanira kuendeswa kuboka chero ripi zvaro reboka renyika dzemudunhu rekuchamhembe kweAfrica kana kuboka renyika dzepasi rese, pasi pechibvumirano chero chipi zvacho chakanyoreranwa neZimbabwe nedzimwe nyika.

(2) Pamusoro peripoti yainofanira kuendesa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 323, Komisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu inogona, kuburikidza neGurukota rakakodzera, kupa maripoti kuParamende pamusoro penyaya dzine chekuita nekodzero dzevanhu nerusununguko, dzainoona kuti dzakakodzera kuziviswa kuParamende. GANHURO 4 Komisheni inoona nezveVanhukadzi neVanhurume muZimbabwe 245 Kudzikwa kweKomisheni inoona nezveVanhukadzi neVanhurume muZimbabwe nenhengo dzeKomisheni iyi

(1) Kune komisheni ichazivikanwa seKomisheni inoona neZvevanhukadzi neVanhurume inoumbwa nevanhu ava—

(a)

sachigaro anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekubvunza pfungwa dzeKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa; uye

(b) nhengo sere, pakati padzo pane—

(i) nomwe dzinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kubva pamazita asiri pasi pegumi nemaviri evanhu vakasarudzwa neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa; uye

(ii) imwe chete inosarudzwa neDare reMadzishe, inoiswa pachigaro neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezvevanhukadzi nevanhurume muZimbabwe dzinofanira kusarudzwa nekuda kwekuvimbika kwadzo, ruzivo rwadzo nekunzwisisa nyaya dzevanhukadzi nevanhurume mukurarama, mune zvetsika nemagariro, zveupfumi nezvematongerwo enyika uye vanhurume nevanhukadzi vanofanira kumiririrwa zvakaenzana muKomisheni iyi. 246 Mabasa eKomisheni inoona nezvevanhukadzi nevanhurume muZimbabwe Komisheni inoona nezvevanhukadzi nevanhurume ine mabasa anotevera—

(a)

kuongorora nyaya dzine chekuita nekuenzaniswa kwevanhukadzi nevanhurume kuitira kuti kuenzaniswa kwevanhurume nevanhukadzi kwakapihwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino kuzadzikiswe;

(b) kuongorora kutyorwa kwekodzero dzine chekuita nezvevanhurume nevanhukadzi kunogona kunge kuchiitika;

(c)

kutambira nekuongorora zvichemo zvinobva kuruzhinji rwevanhu pamwe chete nekutora matanho anoonekwa seakakodzera neKomisheni maererano nezvichemo izvi;

(d) kuita tsvakurudzo panyaya dzine chekuita nevanhukadzi nevanhurume nekurarama zvakanaka, nekupa mazano ezvingada kugadziriswa pamitemo nemaitiro anoita kuti pave nerusarura munyaya dzevanhukadzi nevanhurume;

(e)

kupa mazano kumapazi ehurumende nemamwe akazvimiririra maererano nematanho angatorwa kuti pave nekuenzaniswa pakati pevanhukadzi nevanhurume;

(f)

kukurudzira zvirongwa zvekusimudzirwa kwevakange vakasarira kuitira kuti pave nekuenzaniswa pakati pevanhukadzi nevanhurume;

(g) kukurudzira kusungwa nekutongwa kwevanotyora kodzero dzine chekuita nevanhukadzi nevanhurume;

(h) kuona kuti panotyorwa kodzero dzine chekuita nevanhukadzi nevanhurume pagadziriswa zvakakodzera; uye

(i)

kuita zvese zvakakodzera pakukurudzira kuenzaniswa pakati pevanhukadzi nevanhurume.

247 Maripoti anoitwa neKomisheni inoona nezvevanhukadzi nevanhurume muZimbabwe Pamusoro peripoti yainofanira kuendesa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 323, Komisheni inoona nezvevanhukadzi nevanhurume muZimbabwe inogona, kuburikidza neGurukota rakakodzera, kupa maripoti kuParamende pamusoro penyaya dzine chekuita nevanhukadzi nevanhurume, dzainoona kuti dzakakodzera kuziviswa kuParamende.

GANHURO 5 Komisheni inoona nezveKufambiswa kweMashoko muZimbabwe 248 Kudzikwa kweKomisheni inoona nezvekufambiswa kwemashoko muZimbabwe nenhengo dzeKomisheni iyi

(1) Kune Komisheni ichazivikanwa seKomisheni inoona nezvekufambiswa kwemashoko muZimbabwe inoumbwa nevanhu ava—

(a)

sachigaro anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekubvunza pfungwa dzeKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa; uye

(b) dzimwe nhengo sere dzinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kubva pamazita asiri pasi pegumi nemaviri evanhu vakasarudzwa neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(2) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezvekufambiswa kwemashoko muZimbabwe dzinofanira kusarudzwa nekuda kwekuvimbika kwadzo uye kugona kwadzo kutungamirira uye ruzivo nekunzwisisa nyaya dzezvekodzero dzevanhu nekushanda kwakanaka, kunoyemurika munyaya dzezvekufambiswa kwemashoko. 249 Mabasa eKomisheni inoona nezvekufambiswa kwemashoko muZimbabwe

(1) Komisheni inoona nezvekufambiswa kwemashoko muZimbabwe ine mabasa anotevera—

(a)

kukoshesa, kukurudzira nekuvandudza rusununguko rwemapepanhau nedzimwe nzira dzekufambiswa kwemashoko;

(b) kukurudzira nekusimbisa mashandiro akanaka uye unhu hunoyemurika mumapepanhau nedzimwe nzira dzekufambiswa kwemashoko;

(c)

kuongorora kutepfenyurwa kwenhau, uko kunofanira kubatsira ruzhinji rwevanhu, zvikuru sei kuona kuti kusiyana kwemaonero evanhu vari munyika yeZimbabwe kwakosheswa;

(d) kuumba nekukurudzira kuumbwa kweMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa nevanhu vanoshanda kumapepanhau nekune mamwe mabasa ekufambiswa kwemashoko;

(e)

kutambira nekuongorora zvichemo zvinobva kuruzhinji rwevanhu, uye kuti pakakodzera patorwe matanho ekurangwa kwakakodzera kwevatapi venhau nevamwe vanoshanda kumapepanhau kana kunhepfenyuro vanenge vatyora mutemo kana zvimwe zvinotarisirwa pamashandiro avo.

(f)

kuona kuti vanhu vemuZimbabwe vawana mashoko akaenzanirana anobva kunzvimbo dzakawanda;

(g) kukurudzira kushandiswa nekuvandudzwa kwemitauro yese yakatambirwa kuti ndiyo inoshandiswa muZimbabwe;

(h) kukurudzira kushandiswa kwemichina mitsva mumabasa ezvekufambiswa kwemashoko;

(i)

kukurudzira kukwikwidzana nekuburitswa kwepfungwa dzakasiyanasiyana mumabasa ezvekufambiswa kwemashoko; uye

(j)

kuita tsvakurudzo munyaya dzine chekuita nerusununguko rwezvekufambiswa kwemashoko nerwekutaura zvaunoda, uye kana takanangana nenyaya iyi, kukurudzira kushandurwa kwemitemo.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unogona kupa simba kuKomisheni inoona nezvekufambiswa kwemashoko muZimbabwe kuti—

(a)

iongorore nekubvunzurudza zvine chekuita—

(i) nemashandiro kana mamiriro ezvinhu chero api zvawo angangokanganisa rusununguko rwekufambiswa kwemashoko; uye

(ii) nemashandiro evemapepanhau, vanodhinda nevanofambisa mashoko kuburikidza nemichina nevenhepfenyuro;

uye

(b) kutora matanho kana kukurudzira kutorwa kwematanho ekugadzirisa vatapi venhau kana vamwe vanhu vanoshanda mune zvekufambiswa kwemashoko vanenge vawanikwa vachityora mutemo chero upi zvawo kana mamwe mashandiro avanotarisirwa.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unogona kuti pave neMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa mumabasa ezvemapepanhau nedzimwe nzira dzekufambiswa kwemashoko. 250 Maripoti eKomisheni inoona nezvekufambiswa kwemashoko muZimbabwe Pamusoro peripoti yainofanira kuendesa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 323, Komisheni inoona nezvekufambiswa kwemashoko muZimbabwe inogona, kuburikidza neGurukota rakakodzera, kupa maripoti kuParamende pamusoro penyaya dzine chekuita nekufambiswa kwemashoko, dzainoona kuti dzakakodzera kuziviswa kuParamende. GANHURO 6 Komisheni inoona nezveRunyararo neKuyanana Munyika 251 Kudzikwa kweKomisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana muZimbabwe nenhengo dzeKomisheni iyi

(1) Kwenguva inosvika makore gumi mushure mezuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino, kuchava nekomisheni ichazivikanwa seKomisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana kwevanhu muZimbabwe inoumbwa nevanhu ava—

(a)

sachigaro anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekubvunza pfungwa dzeKomisheni inoona nezveKutongwa kwedzimhosva neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa; uye

(b) dzimwe nhengo sere dzinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kubva pamazita asiri pasi pegumi nemaviri evanhu vakasarudzwa neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(2) Sachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana kwevanhu muZimbabwe anofanira kunge ari munhu ava nemakore manomwe kana kupfuura akakodzera kushanda segweta muZimbabwe.

(3) Kana kudomwa kwasachigaro weKomisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana muZimbabwe kusingawirirani nemaonero eKomisheni inoona nezveUtongi, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuita kuti veKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa vaziviswe nekukasika.

(4) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana muZimbabwe dzinofanira kusarudzirwa kuvimbika, ruzivo nekunzwisisa uye kuva nenguva refu vachishanda mune zvekuyananisa, kudzivirira nekugadzirisa kupesana, uye zvekuyanana mushure mekurwisana kana zvekuunza runyararo.

252 Mabasa eKomisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana Komisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana muZimbabwe inoita mabasa anotevera—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

(f)

(g)

(h)

(i)

(j)

kuona kuti pava neruenzaniso, kuregererana nekuyanana mushure mekurwisana kana kusawirirana; kuvamba nekuparura zvirongwa zvinokurudzira kuregererana, kubatana nekuva pamwe chete muZimbabwe uye kupedza kusawirirana murunyararo; kuunza kuyanana munyika kuburikidza nekukurudzira kuti vanhu vataure chokwadi maererano nezvakaitika kare nekuita kuti pave nekugadziriswa kwezvakakanganisika uye kuwaniswa kweruenzaniso; kugadzira zvinofanira kutevedzwa nemasangano kana mapoka munyika yese kuitira kuti pave nekutaurirana pakati pemapato ezvematongerwo enyika, dzinharaunda, masangano nemamwe mapoka, kuitira kudzivirira kupesana nekusawirirana mune ramangwana; kuvamba zvirongwa zvinoita kuti vanhu vakatambudzwa nekurwadziswa uye kuitirwa zvimwe zvakaipa vawaniswe ruyamuro nerutsigiro rwekuti vagadzikane; kutambira nekuongorora zvichemo zvinobva kuruzhinji rwevanhu, uye kuti zvichemo izvi zvigadziriswe neKomisheni nenzira yakakodzera; kuumba nzira dzekukurumidza kuona pese pangangopesana vanhu nekutora matanho ekudzivirira kupesana uku; kuita chimwe chinhu chero chipi zvacho pakuedza kudzivirira kupesana uye kukurudzira runyararo; uye kunyaradza nekuyananisa vari kupesana munharaunda, mumasangano, mapoka nevanhuwo zvavo. kupa mazano maererano nemitemo inoita kuti rubatsiro, kusanganisira kunyorwa pasi kwezvakaitika, rwupiwe kuvanhu vakakanganiswa nekurwa, zvirwere nezvimwe zvinokanganisa runyararo.

253 Maripoti eKomisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana Pamusoro peripoti yainofanira kuendesa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 323, Komisheni inoona nezverunyararo nekuyanana muZimbabwe inogona, kuburikidza neGurukota rakakodzera, kupa maripoti kuParamende pamusoro penyaya dzine chekuita nerunyararo nekuyanana munyika, dzainoona kuti dzakakodzera kuziviswa kuParamende. CHITSAUKO 13 Mapoka anorwisa Uwori neKuparwa kweMhosva GANHURO 1 Komisheni inoona nezveKurwisana neUwori muZimbabwe 254 Kudzikwa kweKomisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe nenhengo dzeKomisheni iyi

(1) Kune Komisheni ichazivikanwa seKomisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe inoumbwa nevanhu ava—

(a)

sachigaro anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekubvunza pfungwa dzeKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa; uye

(b) dzimwe nhengo sere dzinodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kubva pamazita asiri pasi pegumi nemaviri evanhu vakasarudzwa neKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(2) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe dzinofanira kusarudzirwa kuvimbika kwadzo uye ruzivo nenguva refu mumabasa ekutungamirira kana kutonga kana kuferefeta kuparwa kwemhosva kana kuti nekuda kwezvavangori zvinoita kuti vave vakakodzera kupihwa basa iri, uye—

(a)

nhengo imwe chete kana kupfuura inofanira kuva munhu ava nemakore manomwe kana kupfuura akakodzera kushanda segweta muZimbabwe;

(b) nhengo imwe chete kana kupfuura inofanira kuva munhu akakodzera kushanda semunhu akadzidzira zvekuongorora kushandiswa kwemari nemazvo muZimbabwe, uye ane makore manomwe kana kupfuura ari pabasa rekuongorora zvemari iri; uye

(c)

nhengo imwe chete kana kupfuura inofanira kuva munhu ane makore anosvika gumi kana kupfuura achiita basa rekuferefeta zvekuparwa kwedzimhosva.

255 Mabasa eKomisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe

(1) Komisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe inoita mabasa anotevera—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

(f)

(g)

(h)

kuferefeta nekuburitsa pachena zvine chekuita nehuwori mumabasa eHurumende neemakambani akazvimirira; kurwisana nehuwori, kuba, kusashandiswa zvakanaka kwemari, kusashandiswa zvakanaka kwesimba nehumwe unhu husina kunaka mumabasa eHurumende neemakambani akazvimirira. kukurudzira kuvimbika, kushandiswa kwemari zvakanaka nekuitira zvinhu pachena mumabasa eHurumende nemakambani akazvimirira; kutambira nekuongorora zvichemo zvinobva kuruzhinji rwevanhu uye kutora matanho akakodzera maererano nezvichemo izvi; kuraira Komishina Mukuru wemapurisa kuti aferefete nyaya dzepanofungirwa kuti pane huwori uye kuzivisa Komisheni zvinenge zvabuda mukuferefeta uku; kuendesa nyaya kuBoka rinoona nezvekuferefetwa nekutongwa kwemhosva; kutsvaga rubatsiro kubva kumapurisa eZimbabwe nemamwe mapoka anoferefeta nyaya muNyika; uye kupa mazano kuHurumende nevamwe vanhu maererano nematanho anofanira kutorwa kuitira kusimbaradza kuvimbika, kutsanangura mashandiro, nekudzivirira mashandiro akaipa muHurumende nemumakambani akazvimirira.

(2) Mukuru wemapurisa anofanira kuita zvese zvaanenge anzi aite neKomisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe sezvakapihwa pasi pechikamu chidiki

(1)(e).

(3) Kuburikidza nematanho ezvemitemo nemamwewo, Hurumende inofanira kuona kuti Komisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe ine simba

rekuti munhu asungwe nekutongwa kana achinge afungirwa zvine umboo kuti akaita zvehuwori, ari kushandisa simba zvisizvo, kana kuita zvimwewo zvakaipa zvinoenderana nezvinotariswa neKomisheni iyi. 256 Kushanda kwezvimwe zviri muChitsauko 12 pabasa reKomisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe Zvikamu 234, 235, 236 ne237 zvinoshanda kuKomisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe nenhengo dzayo zvichiita sekuti iKomisheni yakazvimiririra, yakadzikwa neChitsauko 12. 257 Maripoti eKomisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe Pamusoro peripoti yainofanira kuendesa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 323, Komisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori muZimbabwe inogona, kuburikidza neGurukota rakakodzera, kupa maripoti kuParamende maererano nenyaya dzine chekuita nemashandiro asina kunaka mumabasa eHurumende nemakambani akazvimirira, dzainoona kuti dzakakodzera kuziviswa kuParamende.

GANHURO 2 Chikamu chinoona nezveKutongwa kweMhosva 258 Kuumbwa nebasa reChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva Kune Chikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva icho chinoona nezvekufambiswa nekuitwa kwebasa rezvekutongwa kwemhosva chakamiririra Hurumende pamwe chete nekuita rimwe basa chero ripi zvaro rine chekuita nekutongwa kwemhosva. 259 Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva nevamwe vashandi veboka iri

(1) Kune mukuru anotungamirira Chikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva.

(2) Hofisi yemukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva ihofisi yeHurumende, asi haisi chikamu chevashandi veHurumende.

(3) Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva anodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekubvunza pfungwa dzeKomisheni inoona nezveUtongi kuchitevedzwa zvakafanira zvinotevedzwa pakudomwa kwemutongi.

(4) Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva anofanira kuva munhu anokodzera kuva mutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro.

(5) Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva achava pachigaro kwemakore matanhatu uye anokwanisa kunzi ashande kamwe chete zvakare mamwe makore matanhatu.

(6) Asati atanga basa iri, Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva anofanira kuita mhiko yebasa pamberi peMutungamiriri weNyika kana mumwe munhu anenge apihwa mvumo neMutungamiriri weNyika sekupihwa kwazvakaitwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(7) Mitemo inotevedzwa pakubviswa kwemutongi pachinzvimbo ndiyo inotevedzwawo pakubviswa kweMukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva pabasa.

(8) Mashandiro eMukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva, kusanganisira mari yaanotambira, zvinofanira kutsanangurwa muMutemo weParamende, asi mari yaanotambira haifaniri kudzikiswa panguva yaanenge ari pachinzvimbo.

(9) Mari inotambirwa nemukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva inobva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende.

(10) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti kuumbwe boka revachapinza mabasa vashandi vachabatsira Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva mubasa rake uye unofanira kuzivisa—

(a)

zvinodikanwa kuti munhu ave akakodzera kuita basa iri;

(b) mashandiro, unhu nekurangwa kwevanhu ava;

(c)

kuti, mukuita basa ravo, vanhu ava vanofanira kuva vakasununguka, vasingarerekeri divi rimwe chete uye vachitevedza mutemo nemashandiro avanenge vanzi vaite neMukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva;

(d) mamiriro nemarongerwo eChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva; uye

(e)

nezvekushanda kwakanaka pamwe chete nekugona basa kweChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva.

(11) Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva anogona kuraira Mukuru wemapurisa kuti aferefete uye kuzomuzivisa zvese, maererano nemaonero eMukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva, zvine chekuita nemhosva kana kufungirwa kuti pane mhosva, uye Mukuru wemapurisa anofanira kuita sezvaanenge arairwa. 260 Rusununguko rweMukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva

(1) Zvichienderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino, Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva—

(a)

akazvimiririra uye haatungamirirwi kana kurairwa naani zvake; uye

(b) anofanira kuita basa rake zvisina kurerekera kudivi rimwe chete, pasina kutya, tsvete, kana ruvengo.

(2) Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva anofanira kuumba nekuburitsa pachena mitemo yakasiyana-siyana yaanoshandisa pakutonga nekuongorora mhosva. 261 Mashandiro evashandi veChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva

(1) Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva nevashandi veBoka iri vanofanira kushanda nenzira inoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(2) Hakuna mushandi weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva anobvumirwa, pakuita kwake basa—

(a)

kurerekera divi rimwe chete;

(b) kukurudzira zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro;

(c)

2013

kukanganisa zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro, rinenge richiita zviri pamutemo; kana

(d) kukanganisa kodzero dzakakosha nerusununguko rwemunhu chero upi zvake.

(3) Vashandi veChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva havabvumirwi kuva nhengo kana kuva vabati vezvigaro mubato kana sangano chero ripi zvaro rezvematongerwo enyika.

(4) Mutemo weParamende unogona kutara zvimwe zvinoita kuti vashandi veChikamu chnoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva vasarerekera divi rimwe chete mune zvematongerwo enyika. 262 Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva anofanira kutura mashoko kuParamende kamwe chete pagore Kuburikidza neGurukota rakakodzera, Mukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva anofanira kutura mashoko enhoroondo kuParamende pagore rimwe nerimwe achizivisa mashandiro nemabasa ehofisi yake, uye ripoti iyi inofanira kuendeswa mukati memwedzi mitanhatu kubva pakutanga kwegore rinotevera riri kuturirwa nhoroondo. 263 Mamwe masimba ekutonga mhosva Mutemo weParamende unogona kupa masimba ekutonga mhosva kune vamwe vanhu vasiri veChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva, asi masimba aya haafaniri kutadzisa kana kupesana nemasimba eChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva akatsanangurwa muChikamu chino.

CHITSAUKO 14 Hurumende dzeMatunhu neMaguta NHANGANYAYA Kunyangwe zvazvo zvichidikanwa kuti pave ne:

(a) kuchengetedzwa kwekubatana kwevanhu vese muZimbabwe pamwe chete nekudzivirira kusabatana kwemhando dzese uye kutsaurana;

(b) kusununguka kwevagari vese nenharaunda dzese dzemuZimbabwe kupinda muzviitiko zvehurumende nenzira inokoshesa zvido zveruzhinji rwevanhu; uye

(c)

kugoverwa zvakaenzana kwezviwanikwa zvemunyika pamwe nekupinda kwevagari vedzinharaunda mukusarudza zvakakosha zvakanangana nebudiriro yenzvimbo dzavo;

panofanira kuva nekuparadzirwa kwemasimba nemabasa kuhurumende dzematunhu muZimbabwe.

GANHURO 1 Zvemavambo 264 Kuparadzirwa kwemasimba nemabasa ehurumende

(1) Pese pazvinoonekwa zvakakodzera, masimba nemabasa eHurumende anofanira kupihwa kumakanzuru ematunhu neemaguta, ayo anokwanisa kuita mabasa aya zvakanaka uye nemazvo.

(2) Zvinangwa zvekupihwa kwemasimba nemabasa ehurumende kumakanzuru ematunhu neemaguta ndeizvi—

(a)

kupa masimba ekutongwa kwenharaunda kuvanhu nekuwedzera mikana yekuti vawane zvavanoitawo mukushandiswa kwemasimba eHurumende pazvinhu zvine chekuita neupenyu hwavo;

(b) kukurudzira hurumende yeZimbabwe inotevedza zvido zveruzhinji rwevanhu, inoshanda zvinogutsa, inoitira zvinhu pachena, inotsanangura mashandiro ayo uye ine chisungo chimwe chete;

(c) kuchengetedza nekukurudzira runyararo, kubatana munyika nekuva pamwe chete muZimbabwe;

(d) kukoshesa kodzero yevagari vemunharaunda pakuzviitira zvinhu zvavo pachavo pamwe chete nekukurudzira budiriro yavo;

(e) kuti pave nekugoverana kwakaenzana kwezviwanikwa zvemunharaunda nezvemunyika yese; uye

(f) kuparadzira masimba, mari nezvimwe zviwanikwa zvichibva mumaoko eHurumende nechinangwa chekuumba hwaro hwemari hwakasimba mukanzuru imwe neimwe yeguta kana yekumaruwa. 265 Zvakakosha mukutongwa kwematunhu nemakanzuru

(1) Makanzuru ematunhu makuru, makanzuru emaguta nemakanzuru emaruwa neemunharaunda anofanira kuti mukushanda munzvimbo dzavo—

(a)

aone kuti basa riri kufamba zvakanaka kuburikidza nekuitwa nemazvo, kuitirwa pachena, kutsanangura mashandiro nekuva nechisungo chimwe chete;

(b) aite chete basa raakanzi aite neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana kuti neMutemo weParamende;

(c)

aite basa ravo nenzira inoita kuti asadarika miganhu yavo yezvenzvimbo, zvemari nemaitirwo emabasa, achikanganisa mashandiro emamwe mapazi ehurumende;

(d) ashande pamwe chete kunyanya nenzira idzi—

(i) kuzivisana nekubvunzana mazano panyaya dzingangodaro dzine chekuita navo ese;

(ii) kunzwanana nekushanda pamwe chete mumabasa avo;

(e)

achengetedze runyararo, kubatana nehumwe muZimbabwe;

(f)

achengetedze kurarama kweruzhinji rwevanhu kwakanaka; uye

(g) aone kuti pane kumiririrwa kwakaenzana kwevanhu vari munzvimbo dzavo.

(2) Nhengo dzese dzemakanzuru dzinofanira kusarudzwa nevanhu vakanyoresa kuti vavhote munzvimbo dzinomiririrwa nemakanzuru aya.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuwanisa nzira nemaitiro akakodzera ekufambiswa kwemabasa nekudyidzana pakati pehurumende yenyika nemakanzuru ematunhu makuru, emaguta, neemaruwa. 266 Mashandiro evashandi veHurumende dzematunhu makuru nemakanzuru

(1) Vashandi vemakanzuru ematunhu makuru, emaguta, neemaruwa vanofanira kushanda zvinoendarana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino uye nemutemo.

(2) Hapana mushandi wekanzuru yedunhu guru, yeguta kana yemaruwa anobvumirwa kuita izvi pabasa rake—

(a)

kurerekera divi rimwe chete;

(b) kukurudzira zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro;

(c)

kukanganisa zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro, rinenge richiita zviri pamutemo; kana

(d) kukanganisa kodzero dzakakosha nerusununguko rwemunhu chero upi zvake.

(3) Vashandi vemakanzuru ematunhu neemaguta nemakanzuru madiki havabvumirwi kuva nhengo kana vabati vezvinzvimbo mubato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro.

(4) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti pave nemitemo inoita kuti vashandi vemakanzuru ematunhu makuru, emaguta neemaruwa vasave nemapato ezvematongerwo enyika avanotsigira kana kurerekera kwaari.

GANHURO 2 Matunhu Makuru neMakanzuru eMatunhu Makuru neMaguta 267 Matunhu makuru nematunhu madiki eZimbabwe

(1) Nyika yeZimbabwe yakapatsanurwa mumatunhu makuru anoti—

(a)

Guta reBulawayo;

(b) Guta reHarare;

(c)

Dunhu reManicaland;

(d) Dunhu reMashonaland Central;

(e)

Dunhu reMashonaland East;

(f)

Dunhu reMashonaland West;

(g) Dunhu reMasvingo;

(h) Dunhu reMatabeleland North;

(i)

Dunhu reMatabeleland South; uye

(j)

Dunhu reMidlands;

uye miganhu yematunhu aya yakadzikwa muchisungo cheMutemo weParamende.

(2) Mutemo weParamende—

(a)

unofanira kuita kuti matunhu makuru apatsanurwe kuiswa mumatunhu madiki; uye

(b) unogona kuita kuti miganhu yematunhu makuru nematunhu madiki ishandurwe; zvichitevera kubvunza pfungwa dzeKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo munyika pamwe chete nevanhu vari mumatunhu anenge achida kushandurirwa miganhu. 268 Makanzuru ematunhu makuru

(1) Dunhu guru rimwe nerimwe rine kanzuru, kusiya kwematunhu emaguta. Kanzuru yedunhu guru rimwe nerimwe inoumbwa nevanhu ava—

(a)

sachigaro wekanzuru akasarudzwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 272;

(b) maSeneta akasarudzwa mudunhu iroro;

(c) madzishe maviri anova nhengo dzeSeneti akasarudzwa kubva mudunhu iroro kuburikidza nechikamu 120(1)(b);

(d) Mutungamiriri weDare reMadzishe neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weDare reMadzishe, kana nzvimbo

dzavanotonga dziri mudunhu iroro.

(e)

nhengo dzese dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika dzinomiririra nzvimbo dziri mudunhu iroro;

(f)

nhengo dzechikadzi dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika dzakasarudzwa kuburikidza nechikamu 124(1)(b) kubva mudunhu iroro;

(g) mameya naanasachigaro, kana rimwe zita ravangadanwa naro, vemakanzuru emadhorobha neemaruwa ari mudunhu iroro; uye

(h) vanhu gumi vakasarudzwa nenzira inoita kuti pave nekumiririrwa kwakaenzana sezvataurwa muchikamu chidiki (3);

(2) Munhu anokodzera kusarudzwa kupinda mukanzuru yedunhu sezvinotaurwa nechikamu chidiki (1)(f) kana achikodzera kusarudzwa senhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(3) Sarudzo dzemakanzuru ematunhu makuru dzinofanira kuitwa zvichienderana neMutemo weSarudzo, uyo unofanira kuita kuti makanzura ataurwa nezvawo muchikamu chidiki (1)(f) asarudzwe kuchitevedzwa zvinoita kuti pave nekumiririrwa kwakaenzana kwemapato ezvematongerwo enyika—

(a)

zvichibva pamavhoti akawanikwa pakuvhoterwa kwevanhu vaimiririra mapato ezvematongerwo enyika mudunhu iroro pasarudzo dzakaitwa dzekutsvaga vamiriri muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika; uye

(b) zvinoita kuti vamiririri vechirume nevechikadzi vanenge vachikwikwidza musarudzo vanyorwe mazita achipesaniswa uye gwaro rimwe nerimwe remazita richitanga nezita remunhukadzi.

(4) Chinzvimbo chenhengo yekanzuru yedunhu guru chataurwa mu—

(a)

ndima (b), (c), (e) kana (g) yechikamu chidiki (1) chinonzi hachisisina munhu kana mubati wacho asiya chigaro chake muParamende;

(b) ndima (d) yechikamu chidiki (1) chinonzi hachisisina munhu kana mubati wacho asisiri meya kana sachigaro wekanzuru mudunhu iroro;

(c)

ndima (f) yechikamu chidiki (1) chinonzi hachisisina munhu nekuda kwezvataurwa muchikamu 129, zvichiita sekuti munhu uyu aive nhengo yeParamende.

269 Makanzuru emaguta Guta rimwe nerimwe rine kanzuru yeguta inoumbwa nevanhu ava—

(a)

Tichitarisa guta reBulawayo, meya weguta reBulawayo anova sachigaro wekanzuru yeguta reBulawayo;

(b) Tichitarisa guta reHarare—

(i) Meya weguta reHarare, anova sachigaro wekanzuru yeguta reHarare; uye

(ii) meya kana sachigaro wedhorobha rinotevera guta guru pakukura mudunhu iroro ari iye mutevedzeri wasachigaro wekanzuru yeguta reHarare;

(c)

nhengo dzese dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika dzinomiririra nzvimbo dziri pasi pedunhu reguta iroro;

(d) vanhukadzi vanova nhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika vakasarudzwa kubva mudunhu reguta iri kuburikidza nezviri muchikamu 124(1)(b);

(e)

maSeneta akasarudzwa kubva mudunhu reguta iri; uye

(f)

mameya nevatevedzeri vemameya nanasachigaro nevatevedzeri vanasachigaro, kana mamwewo mazita avangadanwa navo mumakanzuru ese ari mudunhu reguta iri.

(2) Chinzvimbo chenhengo yekanzuru yataurwa nezvayo mu—

(a)

ndima (a) kana (b) dzechikamu chidiki (1) chinonzi hachisisina munhu kana mubati wacho asisiri meya, mutevedzeri wameya kana sachigaro, zvichienderana nechigaro chiri kutaurwa panguva iyoyo;

(b) ndima (c), (d) kana (e) dzechikamu chidiki (1) chinonzi hachisisina munhu kana mubati wacho asiya chinzvimbo chake chemuParamende;

(c)

ndima (f) yechikamu chidiki (1) chinonzi hachisisina munhu kana mubati wacho asisiri meya, mutevedzeri wameya, sachigaro kana mutevedzeri wasachigaro, zvichienderana nechigaro chiri kutaurwa panguva iyoyo, mukanzuru iri mudunhu reguta iroro.

270 Mabasa emakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta

(1) Kanzuru yedunhu guru kana yeguta ine basa rekukwidziridza magariro evanhu vayo mune zveupfumi hwedunhu rayo zvichisanganisira—

(a)

kuronga nekuita mabasa anosimudzira budiriro yeupfumi nekugara zvakanaka kwevanhu mudunhu rayo;

(b) kuronga nekuzadzikisa mabasa eurongwa hwehurumende mudunhu rayo;

(c)

kuronga nekutora matanho ekuchengetedza, kuvandudza nekushandisa zvine ungwaru, zviwanikwa zvemudunhu rayo;

(d) kukurudzira zvekushanyirwa kwedunhu iroro pamwe chete nekugadzirisa zvinoita kuti basa iri rifambe zvakanaka;

(e)

kuongorora mashandisirwo ezviwanikwa mudunhu rayo; uye

(f)

kuita chero rimwe basa kusanganisira rezvemitemo rinenge raturirwa kwairi kuburikidza neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutungamirira zvekudzikwa, zvekurongwa uye zvevashandi vemakanzuru emaguta nemashandiro avo.

(3) Nhengo dzekanzuru yedunhu guru kana yeguta dzinofanira kutsanangura mashandiro adzo, seboka kana mumwe nemumwe, kuvagari vedunhu radzo nekuHurumende. 271 Makomiti emakanzuru ematunhu makuru Kuti basa ravo rifambe zvakanaka, makanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta anogona kuumba makomiti asi komiti imwe neimwe inofanira kutungamirirwa nenhengo yataurwa nezvayo muchikamu 268(1)(f) kana 269(1)(f), zvichienderana nekuti ndezvipi zviri kudiwa. 272 Vanasachigaro vemakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta

(1) Pamusangano wekutanga mushure mesarudzo huru dzenyika, kanzuru yedunhu inofanira kusarudza sachigaro kubva pamazita evanhu vaviri vakakodzera anenge apihwa ne—

(a)

bato rezvematongerwo enyika rine zvinzvimbo zvakawanda kudarika zvemamwe muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika mudunhu iroro; kana kuti

(b) kana pasina bato rezvematongerwo enyika rakafanana neratsanangurwa mundima (a), bato rakawana mavhoti akawanda kudarika mamwe mudunhu iroro musarudzo dzekutsvaga nhengo dzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika;

(2) Munhu anokodzera kusarudzwa sasachigaro wekanzuru yedunhu kana achikodzera kusarudzwa senhengo yeSeneti.

(3) Hofisi yasachigaro wekanzuru yedunhu ihofisi yeHurumende, asi haisi chikamu chevashandi veHurumende.

(4) Asati atanga basa, sachigaro wekanzuru yedunhu guru anofanira kuita mhiko dzekuvimbika pabasa iri pamberi pemunyori wekanzuru yedunhu guru sekunyorwa kwazvakaitwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(5) Sachigaro wekanzuru yedunhu guru anogona kusiya basa kuburikidza nekuzivisa nezvekusiya basa kwake kukanzuru yedunhu guru achizvitaurira pachake.

(6) Sachigaro wekanzuru yedunhu guru anofanira kusiya basa—

(a)

musi unosangana kanzuru yedunhu guru kekutanga mushure mesarudzo huru;

(b) kana achinge anzi haachakwanisi kuramba ari nhengo yekanzuru yedunhu guru;

(c) kana paitwa chisungo nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo

dzese dzekanzuru yedunhu guru chekuti abve pachigaro; kana kuti

(d) kana abviswa pachigaro nedare revatongi rinotaurwa nezvaro muchikamu

chidiki (7).

(7) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutaura nezvekuumbwa kwedare revatongi rakazvimiririra kuti riite basa rekubvisa vanasachigaro vemakanzuru ematunhu pabasa, asi kubviswa pabasa uku kunofanira kuitika nekuda kweizvi chete—

(a)

kutadza kuita basa rake nekuda kwekurwara kwemuviri kana kukanganisika pfungwa;

(b) kutadza basa zvakanyanya;

(c)

kuita zvisingatarisirwi zvachose pabasa;

(d) kuwanikwa ane mhosva inoratidza kusavimbika, huwori kana kushandisa simba rehofisi yake zvisizvo; kana

(e)

2013

kutyora mutemo achida uye achiziva, kusanganisira mitemo yemakanzuru.

(8) Sachigaro wekanzuru yedunhu guru haasiyi basa kunze kwekunge aita zvakatsanangurwa mundima diki ino. 273 Zvakasiyana-siyana zvine chekuita nemakanzuru ematunhu makuru nemakanzuru emaguta

(1) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuva nechisungo chinowirirana neChitsauko chino kuti pave nekugadzirwa uye kuziviswa kwemashandiro anoita makanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta, zvikuru sei, zvine chekuita ne—

(a)

zvinofanira kutevedzwa nemakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta;

(b) mabasa anasachigaro vemakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta;

(c)

mashandiro nemari dzinotambiriswa nhengo dzemakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta; uye

(d) mapindiro ebasa, mashandiro nemabvisirwo evashandi vemakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta.

(2) Mutemo weSarudzo unofanira kuzivisa nenzira inoenderana neChitsauko chino, kuti mabasa ezvigaro zvenhengo dzemakanzuru ematunhu makuru akataurwa mundima 268(1)(f) pamwe chete nemahofisi anasachigaro vekanzuru dzematunhu makuru anofanira kutorwa—

(a)

nevanhu webato rezvematongerwo enyika rimwe chete nemunhu anga ari pachigaro kana pabasa iroro; uye

(b) kunze kwezvinzvimbo zvaanasachigaro, nemunhu akafanana neainge ari pachigaro ichi, zvichienderana nekuti aive munhurume kana munhukadzi here. GANHURO 3 Hurumende dzeMatunhu 274 Makanzuru emumadhorobha

(1) Madhorobha ese ane makanzuru ane basa rekumiririra vagari vemumadhorobha ese emuZimbabwe pamwe chete nekutungamirira urongwa hwebasa remagariro evanhu.

(2) Mabasa emumadhorobha anotungamirirwa nemakanzuru ane makanzura akasarudzwa nevanhu vakanyoresa kuti vavhote munzvimbo idzodzo uye makanzuru aya anotungamirirwa nameya akasarudzwa, kana sachigaro, kana rimwe zita ringapihwe munhu iyeye.

(3) Kunogona kuva nemhando dzemakanzuru dzakasiyana-siyana mumadhorobha akasiyana, uye nzvimbo mbiri kana dzinopfuura mbiri dziri mumadhorobha akasiyana dzinogona kuva pasi peutungamiri hwekanzuru imwe chete.

(4) Masarudzirwo evanhu vataurwa nezvavo muchikamu chidiki (2) akatsanangurwa muMutemo weSarudzo.

(5) Mutemo weParamende unogona kupa masimba makuru kuna meya kana sachigaro wekanzuru yedhorobha, asi meya kana sachigaro anenge apihwa masimba aya anofanira kunge asarudzwa nevanhu vakanyoresa kuti vavhote munzvimbo ine kanzuru iyoyo.

275 Makanzuru ekumaruwa

(1) Kune makanzuru ekumaruwa akagadzwa zvinoenderana nechikamu chino, aripo kumiririra nekutungamirira vanhu vari mumaruwa.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti—

(a)

pave nemakanzuru ekumaruwa;

(b) pasarudzwe, nevanhu vakanyoresa kuti vavhote mumaruwa iwayo, makanzura kuti atungamirire kuitwa kwemabasa mukanzuru sekunyorwa kwazvakaitwa mundima (a);

(c)

pasarudzwe vanasachigaro, kana mamwe mazita avangadanwa nawo, kuti vatungamirire kuitwa kwemabasa mumakanzuru sekunyorwa kwazvakaitwa mundima (b); uye

(d) kuziviswe zvinodikanwa kuti munhu akodzere kupinda mumakanzuru, zvakataurwa nezvazvo mundima (b).

(3) Kunogona kuva nemhando dzemakanzuru dzakasiyana-siyana mumaruwa akasiyana, uye nzvimbo mbiri kana dzinopfuura mbiri dziri mumaruwa akasiyana dzinogona kuva pasi peutungamiri hwekanzuru imwe chete. 276 Mabasa emakanzuru ekumaruwa

(1) Kuchitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino pamwe chete neMutemo weParamende chero upi zvawo, kanzuru ine mvumo yekutonga ichishandisa mazano ayo, nyaya dzine chekuita nevanhu vari munzvimbo yayakanzi itungamirire, uye ine masimba ese anodiwa pakuita izvi.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unogona kupa makanzuru mabasa anosanganisira—

(a)

simba rekugadzira mitemo, zvinobvumirwa nezvisingabvumirwi zvinoita kuti kutungamirirwa kwenzvimbo dzemaruwa kufambe zvakanaka;

(b) simba rekuripisa mari dzekufambiswa kwemabasa nemitero uye kutsvaga nzira dzekuunganidza nadzo mari dzinoita kuti mabasa nezvinangwa zvemakanzuru zvizadzikiswe. 277 Sarudzo dzevanopinda mumakanzuru

(1) Sarudzo dzemakanzura dzinofanira kuitwa—

(a)

takatarisa sarudzo dzemameya nemakanzura, pamwe chete nesarudzo huru dzenhengo dzeParamende nedzeMutungamiriri weNyika;

(b) takatarisa imwewo sarudzo, isiri sarudzo huru, inotwa nechinangwa chekutsiva vanhu vakange vari pazvinzvimbo, nekukurumidza kunogoneka, zvichitevera kuzaruka kwezvinzvimbo.

(2) Sarudzo dzemameya nanasachigaro vemakanzuru emaruwa, vasiri mameya kana vanasachigaro vanopihwa masimba asingapikiswe muchikamu 274(5), dzinofanira kuitwa pamusangano wekutanga wemakanzuru zvichitevera kuitwa kwesarudzo huru.

(3) Kunze kwezvakanyorwa muchikamu chidiki (2) kana muMutemo weParamende, mameya, vanasachigaro nemakanzura emumaruwa vanotanga basa pazuva rechipfumbamwe mushure mekuziviswa kwezvakabuda musarudzo huru umo makanzura aya akasarudzwa.

278 Urefu hwenguva inotarisirwa nhengo dzemakanzuru kuva pazvinzvimbo

(1) Chinzvimbo chameya, sachigaro kana kanzura chinonzi hachisisina munhu kana zviri muchikamu 129 zvaitika, sekuti ainge ari nhengo yedare reParamende; zvese zvinotaurwa nezvazvo pamusoro peMutauriri kana Mutungamiriri weSeneti muchikamu 129(1)(k) zvinongoshanda sezvazviri asi parizvino zvakanangana neGurukota rinoona nezvehurumende dzematunhu.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuzivisa maumbirwo edare revatongi rakazvimiririra rinoita basa rekubvisa pazvinzvimbo mameya, vanasachigaro nemakanzura, asi izvi zvinoitwa chete nekuda kweizvi—

(a)

kutadza kuita basa rake nekuda kwekurwara kwemuviri kana kukanganisika pfungwa;

(b) kutadza basa zvakanyanya;

(c) kuita zvisingatarisirwi zvachose pabasa;

(d) kuwanikwa nemhosva inoratidza kusavimbika, huwori kana kushandisa simba rehofisi yake zvisiri izvo; kana

(e) kutyora mutemo achida, uye achiziva, kusanganisira mitemo yemakanzuru.

(3) Meya, sachigaro kana kanzura wekanzuru yekumaruwa haasiyi chinzvimbo chake kunze kwekunge aita zvakatsanangurwa muchikamu chino. 279 Zvinotevedzwa pamashandiro emakanzuru Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuzivisa zvinofanira kutevedzwa pakushanda kwemakanzuru. CHITSAUKO 15 Madzishe 280 Utungamiriri hwemadzishe

(1) Utungamiriri, chiremerera nemabasa emadzishe pasi pemutemo tsika nemararamiro edu zvinokosheswa.

(2) Mutungamiriri akagadzwa nenzira yechivanhu aripo kuita mabasa ezvetsika, magariro neunhu zvinoitwa naShe, sadunhu kana sabhuku zvichienderana nezvinotevedzwa munzvimbo yake. 281 Zvinhu zvakakosha zvinofanira kutevedzwa nemadzishe

(1) Madzishe anofanira—

(a)

kuita basa nenzira inoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino pamwe chete nemitemo yeZimbabwe;

(b) kukoshesa tsika dzine chekuita neUtungamiriri hwemadzishe uye kuita basa rawo nechinangwa chekuzadzikisa zvinotarisirwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino pautungamiriri hwetsika nemagariro echivanhu; uye

(c) kubata nekutonga vanhu vese vari munzvimbo dzavanotonga zvakaenzanirana uye zvakaringana.

(2) Madzishe haafaniri—

(a)

kuva nhengo dzebato chero ripi zvaro rezvematongerwo enyika kana kupinda muzviitiko zvakarerekera kune zvematongerwo enyika;

(b) kuita zvinhu nenzira yakarerekera divi rimwe chete;

(c)

kukurudzira zvido zvebato rezvematongerwo enyika chero ripi zvaro; kana

(d) kukanganisa kodzero nerusununguko rwemunhu chero upi zvake. 282 Mabasa emadzishe

(1) Madzishe anofanira kuita mabasa aya munzvimbo dziri pasi pawo—

(a)

kuchengetedza nekukoshesa tsika, unhu nemagariro akanaka enharaunda dzawo, zvikuru sei, kukurudzira kugara kwakanaka mudzimhuri;

(b) kutora matanho ekuchengetedza tsika, magariro, nhoroondo nenhaka yezviwanikwa zviri munzvimbo, kusanganisira nzvimbo dzinoyera;

(c) kuita kuti pave nebudiriro;

(d) zvichienderana neMutemo weParamende, kuva vatariri vevhu renyika yavanotonga nekuchengetedza nharaunda;

(e) kupedza kusawirirana kungavepo pakati pevanhu vari munharaunda dzavo achitevedza mitemo yechivanhu; uye

(f) kuita chero rimwe basa raanenge apihwa kuburikidza neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Kunze kwekunge zvataurwa muMutemo weParamende, madzishe ane simba pamusoro pevhu renzvimbo dzavanotonga pamwe chete nevanhu vari munzvimbo idzodzo.

(3) Paanoita basa rawo, madzishe haasungirwi kutungamirirwa kana kuudzwa zvekuita nemumwe munhu chero upi zvake, kunze kwekunge kuchitevedzwa zvakanyorwa muMutemo weParamende.

(4) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuzivisa zvinobvumirwa nezvisingabvumirwi pamashandiro emadzishe. 283 Kugadzwa nekubviswa pachinzvimbo kwemadzishe Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuzivisa zvinotevera, zvichienderana netsika, magariro, unhu nezvinoitwa munharaunda yazvinenge zvichiitwa—

(a)

kugadzwa, kumiswa basa, kutsiviwa, pamwe chete nekubviswa kwemadzishe;

(b) kutangwa nekumutsiridzwa kweushe; uye

(c)

kugadziriswa kwekusawirirana kune chekuita nekugadzwa, kumiswa, kutsiviwa, pamwe chete nekubviswa kwemadzishe;

asi—

(i) kugadzwa, kubviswa nekumiswa basa kwemadzishe kunofanira kuitwa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekunge zvatsigirwa neDare reMadzishe redunhu guru, richishanda kuburikidza neDare reMadzishe renyika pamwe chete neGurukota rinoona nezvemadzishe, uye zvichiitwa nenzira yetsika dzinotevedzwa munharaunda iyoyo;

(ii) kusawirirana pamusoro pekugadzwa, kumiswa basa nekubviswa kwemadzishe kunofanira kugadziriswa neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekunge zvatsigirwa neDare reMadzishe redunhu richishanda neGurukota rinoona nezvemadzishe;

(iii) Mutemo unofanira kupa matanho ekuita kuti nyaya dzese idzi dzigadziriswe zvakaenzanirana uye pasina kupinza nyaya dzezvematongerwo enyika;

(iv) Mutemo unofanira kuwanisa matanho ekuchengetedza chiremera chematare emadzishe pamwe chete nerusununguko rwematare emadzishe kubva mukukanganiswa nezvine chekuita nezvematongerwo enyika. 284 Mari dzinotambiriswa madzishe nezvimwe zvavanowana

(1) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuzivisa mari nezvimwewo zvinotambiriswa madzishe uye izvi zvinotarwa pamwe chete nekupihwa mvumo neMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekunge zvatsigirwa neGurukota rezvemari richikurukura neGurukota rinoona nezvemadzishe.

(2) Mari inotambiriswa ishe inobhadharwa ichibva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa wemari yeHurumende.

(3) Mari inotambiriswa ishe haifaniri kudzikiswa panguva yaanenge ari pachinzvimbo cheushe kana kuti akafanobata chinzvimbo ichi. 285 Dare reMadzishe reNyika nematare eMadzishe ematunhu

(1) Kune Dare reMadzishe renyika yese rinoumbwa kuburikidza neMutemo weParamende, kuti rimiririre madzishe ese muZimbabwe.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti dunhu guru rimwe nerimwe, kunze kwemaguta, rive nedare remadzishe rinoumbwa nemadzishe emudunhu iroro.

(3) Nepanogoneka pese, madzishe ari mudunhu rimwe nerimwe anofanira kumiririrwa muDare reMadzishe renyika.

(4) Sarudzo dzeMutungamiriri, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri nenhengo dzeDare reMadzishe, dzinofanira kuitwa neKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe.

(5) Dare reMadzishe redunhu rimwe nerimwe rinofanira kusarudza, pachitevedzwa zviri muMutemo weSarudzo, madzishe anomiririra dunhu guru muSeneti sezvakapihwa muchikamu 120(1)(b).

(6) Mutungamiriri neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weDare reMadzishe renyika vanosarudzwa kuti vave pazvinzvimbo kwemakore mashanu uye anogona kunzi ashande zvakare kwenguva iyi kamwe chete, asi munhu anenge ari Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri kwemakore gumi anokwanisa kusarudzwa seMutungamiriri.

(7) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutaura zviri maererano nezvinotevera—

(a)

kusarudzwa kwemadzishe anopinda muDare reMadzishe renyika uye kukodzera kana kusakodzera kwashe kupinda musarudzo;

(b) mhiko yekutanga basa inoitwa nenhengo dzeDare reMadzishe reNyika nedzematare emadzishe ematunhu;

(c)

urefu hwenguva inobvumirwa kuti nhengo dzeDare reMadzishe renyika dzirambe dziri pazvigaro;

(d) mihoro, penjeni nezvimwewo zvinopihwa Mutungamiriri, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri pamwe chete nenhengo dzeDare reMadzishe renyika;

(e)

zvinofanira kutevedzwa pamisangano yeDare reMadzishe reNyika nepamisangano yematare emadzishe ematunhu; uye

(f)

kuumbwa kwehofisi inoshanda neDare reMadzishe renyika nemahofisi anoshanda nematare emadzishe ematunhu mukuita mabasa awo.

286 Mabasa eDare reMadzishe reNyika nematare eMadzishe ematunhu

(1) Dare reMadzishe reNyika nedare reMadzishe redunhu rimwe nerimwe zvinoshanda mabasa aya—

(a)

kuchengetedza, kukurudzira nekuvandudza tsika, magariro nezvechinyakare;

(b) kumiririra pfungwa dzemadzishe nekuona kuti rukudzo nekukosha kwezvechinyakare zvachengetedzwa;

(c)

kuchengetedza, kukurudzira nekupfuudzira mberi zvido zvemadzishe;

(d) kutarisa pfungwa nezvichemo zvinopirwa kwariri nemasadunhu, masabhuku nevamwe vatungamiriri vanoshanda nemadzishe;

(e)

kutsanangura nekusimbisa unhu hwakanaka mumadzishe, pamwe chete nekuvandudza ruzivo rwavo rwekutungamirira;

(f)

kubatsira pakugadzirisa kusawirirana kungavepo pakati pemadzishe, kana kune chekuita nemasadunhu, masabhuku nevamwe vatungamiriri vanoshanda nemadzishe;

(g) kuita chero rimwe basa raringapihwa kana raringasungirwa kuita neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuona kuti—

(a)

Dare reMadzishe renyika nematare emadzishe ematunhu ari kukwanisa kuita mabasa awo zvakasununguka uye nemazvo; uye

(b) vanhu vanoshandira Dare reMadzishe renyika nematare emadzishe ematunhu vaita basa ravo nemazvo uye zvisina kurerekera divi rimwe chete. 287 Komiti inoona nezvekuvimbika neunhu Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuti paumbwe, pave nenhengo, nezvinofanira kutevedzwa neKomiti yeMadzishe inoona nezvekuvimbika neunhu kuti iite zvinotevera—

(a)

kugadzira nekusimbisa kuvimbika neunhu mumashandiro emadzishe;

(b) kugadzirisa kusawirirana kungava pakati pemadzishe;

(c)

kutarisa mhosva, zvichemo nezvimwe zvingapomerwa madzishe. CHITSAUKO 16 Minda yeKurima

288 Tsananguro yeChitsauko 16 Muchitsauko chino— “minda yekurima” ine tsananguro yakapihwa muchikamu 72. 289 Zvakakosha zvinofanira kutevedzwa maererano neminda yekurima Nechinangwa chekugadzirisa kusaenzanirana kweuridzi hweivhu kwakaunzwa neutongi hwevaRungu, uye nekuda kuunza urongwa hutsva hwekugarisa vanhu patsva nekugoverwa zvakaenzana kwezviwanikwa zvenyika kuvanhu vese vemuZimbabwe, mitemo ine chekuita neminda yekurima inofanira kutevedza zvakakosha zvinotevera—

(a)

ivhu chiwanikwa chinoenda chichipera uye inhaka yevanhu vese venyika yeZimbabwe;

(b) kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 72, mugari wese wemuZimbabwe ane kodzero yekutenga, kuwana, kugara, kushandisa, kushandura uridzi, yekuisa mari, kurojesa kana kutengesa munda wekurima zvisinei nerudzi rwake kana ruvara rweganda rake;

(c) kupihwa nekugoverwa kweminda yekurima kunofanira kuitwa zvakaenzanirana uye pachikosheswa kuenzaniswa kwevanhurume nevanhukadzi nekusiyana-siyana kwezvinokosheswa nevagari vemunzvimbo;

(d) urefu hwenguva inogona kushandiswa munda nemunhu hunofanira kukurudzira kuwedzerwa kwegoho nekuiswa kwemari nenguva, muminda yekurima nezvizvarwa zveZimbabwe;

(e) kushandiswa kweminda yekurima kunofanira kuwedzera uwandu hwezvekudya, utano hwakanaka nezvekudya zvinovaka muviri uyewo kuwanisa mabasa, asiwo kuchichengetedza nharaunda nezviwanikwa kuitira vachararama mune ramangwana;

(f) hapana munhu achashaiswa kodzero yake yekushandisa munda kana yekugara pamunda wekurima, zvisina chikonzero. 290 Kuenderera mberi kwekodzero dzeHurumende paminda yekurima

(1) Minda yekurima yese—

(a)

yakanyorwa muNhamatidzwa 7 yeBumbiro reMitemo rakange riripo kare; kana

(b) yakatsanangurwa sekupihwa kwazvakaitwa muchikamu 16B(2)(a)(ii) kana

(iii) cheBumbiro reMitemo rakange riripo kare zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika; inoramba iri mumaoko eHurumende.

(2) Kupikisana chero kupi zvako kungavapo pakati pechinhu chero chipi zvacho chiri mu—

(a) chiziviso chakanyorwa muNhamatidzwa 7 yeBumbiro reMitemo rakange riripo kare; kana kuti

(b) chiziviso chine chekuita neminda yekurima chakataurwa nezvacho muchikamu 16B(2)(a)(ii) kana (iii) cheBumbiro reMitemo rekare; negwaro reuridzi nechipi zvacho chakakanganiswa chingadaro chiri muchiziviso ichi, hazvikanganisi kushanda kwechikamu chidiki (1) kana kushaisa Nyika uridzi hweminda yekurima inenge ichitaurwa nezvayo sekupihwa kwazvakaitwa muchikamu chidiki ichi. 291 Kuenderera mberi kwekodzero dzevanenge vari paminda yekurima Zvichienderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino, munhu chero upi zvake, zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, akange achishandisa kana kugara, kana kuti akange ane mvumo yekushandisa kana kugara pamunda wekurima chero upi zvawo kuburikidza nechibvumirano neHurumende, anoramba ane kodzero yekushandisa kana kugara pamunda iwoyo panguva iyoyo kana kuti mushure mezuva rakatarwa riri kutaurwa nezvaro, zvichienderana nezvakanyoreranwa kana chimwewo chibvumirano. 292 Kuchengeteka kweuridzi hwevanhu vagara vari paminda yekurima Hurumende inofanira kutora matanho akakodzera, kusanganisira ezvemitemo kuti ipe ruchengetedzo rweuridzi kumunhu wese anenge ari muridzi kana mugari wepamunda wekurima zviri pamutemo.

293 Kugoverwa kweminda yekurima neHurumende

(1) Hurumende inogona kugovera munda chero upi zvawo wekurima uri mumaoko ayo kune mumwe munhu kuti ave muridzi kana munhu wainorojesa kana mumwe wainopa imwe mvumo yekugara kana kushandisa. Asi kugoverwa kwerudzi chero rupi zvarwo, kunofanira kuitwa kuchitevedzwa zvakakosha zvinofanira kutevedzwa zviri muchikamu 289.

(2) Hurumende haigoni kugovera minda miviri kana kupfuura kumunhu mumwe chete nevana vake kana vanhu vaanochengeta.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutara zvinofanira kutevedzwa pakugoverwa nekupihwa kweminda yekurima neHurumende, uye mutemo chero upi zvawo wakadai unofanira kufambirana nezvakakosha zvinofanira kutevedzwa zviri muchikamu 289. 294 Kugoverwa kweminda yekurima nevaridzi vayo kana kuti vanogara pairi Zvichienderana nezvimwewo zvingadziviswa nemutemo, muridzi kana munhu anogara pamunda wekurima ane kodzero yekushandura uridzi, yekuubatisa pachikwereti, kuurojesa kana kupa mumwe kodzero yake pamunda wekurima. 295 Muripo wakanangana nekutorwa kweminda yekurima yaimbenge ine varidzi

(1) Chizvarwa chese cheZimbabwe chine munda wekurima wakatorwa neHurumende nguva iri kutaurwa nezvayo isati yasvika, chinofanira kuripwa neHurumende pamunda uyu, pachiripwazve zvivakwa nebudiriro yakaitwa pamunda uyu usati watorwa.

(2) Munhu chero upi zvake ane munda wekurima wakatorwa neHurumende zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, uyewo panguva iyoyo kodzero dzake kumunda uyu dzakange dzakavimbiswa kana kuchengetedzwa nechibvumirano chakaitwa neHurumende yeZimbabwe neHurumende yeimwe nyika, anofanira kuripwa neHurumende ichiripira munda uyu pamwe chete nezvivakwa uye budiriro yakaitwa pamunda uyu chero ipi zvayo, zvichienderana nechibvumirano ichocho.

(3) Munhu chero upi zvake, kusiya kwemunhu ataurwa nezvake muchikamu chidiki (1) kana (2), ane munda wekurima wakatorwa neHurumende zuva riri kutaurwa nezvaro risati rasvika, anofanira kubhadharwa neHurumende, muripo wakanangana nezvivakwa nebudiriro yakaitwa pamunda uyu chete panguva yawakatorwa.

(4) Muripo unoripwa pasi pezvindima zvidiki (1), (2) ne (3) unofanira kuongororwa nekuripwa zvinoenderana neMutemo weParamende. 296 Kudzikwa kweKomisheni inoona nezveminda muZimbabwe nenhengo dzekomisheni iyi

(1) Kune komisheni ichazivikanwa seKomisheni inoona nezveMinda inoumbwa nevanhu vanotevera—

(a)

sachigaro nemutevedzeri wasachigaro; uye

(b) vamwe vanhu vanobva pavaviri kusvika pavanomwe; vanodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(2) Nhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezveminda dzinofanira—

(a)

2013

kusarudzwa nekuda kwekuvimbika nekugona basa kwadzo, pamwe chete nekuziva nekunzwisisa zvakakosha mukushandiswa nekuchengetedzwa kweivhu; uye

(b) kutaridza kusiyana-siyana kwakaita vanhu vemuZimbabwe, zvikuru sei zvido zvematunhu akasiyana-siyana pamwe chete nekuenzaniswa kwevanhukadzi nevanhurume.

(3) Chikamu 237 chinoshanda pakubviswa pazvinzvimbo kwenhengo dzeKomisheni inoona nezveMinda muZimbabwe iyi ichionekwa seyakazvimiririra, sezvakatsanangurwa muChitsauko 12. 297 Mabasa eKomisheni inoona nezveminda

(1) Komisheni inoona nezveminda muZimbabwe ine mabasa anotevera—

(a)

kuona kuti pane kutsanangura mashandiro, kuenzanirana nekuitira zvinhu pachena mukushandiswa kweminda yekurima iri mumaoko eHurumende;

(b) kutarisa mashandisirwo eminda yekurima nguva nenguva;

(c)

kupa mazano kuHurumende maererano ne—

(i) kutorwa kweminda isiri yeHurumende kuti ishandiswe muurongwa hweHurumende;
