(ii) kuwaniswa kwemikana yakaenzana pakuwana kana kugara paminda yekurima, zvikuru sei— A. kubvisa mhando dzese dzerusarura rwakaipa, zvikuru sei rusarura runoitwa pakati pevanhurume nevanhukadzi; B. kusimbiswa kwemutemo chero upi zvawo unotaura uwandu hweminda yekurima yemunhu mumwe chete kana vanhu vepamba imwe chete;

(iii) mashandisirwo nemakuriro eminda yekurima;

(iv) kurerutswa kwezvinodiwa pakuwana nekushandura kodzero dzeminda;

(v) nzira dzine chekuita neurefu hweuridzi paminda; uye

(vi) kuripwa zvakaenzanirana kweminda yekurima nezvivakwa uye budiriro inenge yakaitwa paminda isati yatorwa nehurumende;

(vii) kugoverwa nekupfuudzwa kune mumwe kwekodzero dzeminda yekurima;

(d) kuongorora nekutonga nyaya dzezvichemo nekusawirirana kune chekuita nekutungamirirwa kwemabasa eminda uye kugoverwa kweminda yekurima.

(2) Mushure mekupihwa mvumo neGurukota rinoona nezveminda, Komisheni inoona nezveminda muZimbabwe inogona kudzika mitemo yakanangana nechimwe chezvinangwa zvakatsanangurwa muchikamu chidiki (1) chero chipi zvacho.

(3) Komisheni inoona nezveminda muZimbabwe inofanira kuita basa rayo ichitevedza mirairo yakanyorwa pasi chero ipi zvayo yainogona kupihwa neGurukota rinoona nezveminda.

(4) Mukuita basa rayo, Komisheni inoona nezveminda muZimbabwe inofanira kutungamirirwa nezvakakosha zvakatsanangurwa muchikamu 289.

(5) Kuburikidza nemitemo uye mamwewo matanho, Nyika nemapoka ayo ese, uye vamiririri vehurumende padanho chero ripi zvaro, zvinofanira kubatsira Komisheni inoona nezveminda mukuita basa rayo uye kuchengetedza rusununguko, kusarerekera divi rimwe chete, chiremerera nekushanda nemazvo kweKomisheni iyi.

(6) Kuburikidza nemitemo nemamwewo matanho akakodzera, Hurumende inofanira kuisa mitemo yakakodzera yekuona kuti—

(a)

Komisheni inoona nezveminda muZimbabwe inokwanisa kuita basa rayo nemazvo uye yakasununguka; uye

(b) vanhu vanoshandira Komisheni inoona nezveminda muZimbabwe vaita basa ravo nenzira yakanaka, yakaenzanirana uye isingarerekeri divi rimwe chete. CHITSAUKO 17 Zvine cheKuita neMari GANHURO 1 Kushandiswa kweMari 298 Zvinofanira kutevedzwa pakushandiswa kwemari yehurumende

(1) Zvinotevera izvi ndizvo zvinofanira kutevedzwa pane zvese zvine chekuita nemari yehurumende muZimbabwe—

(a)

panofanira kuva nekujeka netsananguro dzinogutsa panyaya dzese dzine chekuita nemari yehurumende;

(b) kushanda kwemari yehurumende kunofanira kubatsiridza budiriro yenyika, zvikuru sei—

(i) mutoro wekubatirwa mari yemitero unofanira kugoveranwa zvakaenzana;

(ii) mari inenge yaunganidzwa nenyika inofanira kugoverwa zvakaenzana pakati pehurumende yenyika nehurumende dzematunhu pamwe chete nemamwe mapazi ehurumende; uyewo

(iii) mari inofanira kushandiswa muzvinhu zvine chekuita nebudiriro yenyika yeZimbabwe, uye pakushandiswa kwemari apa panofanira kuonekwa kuti hapana here kukanganwikwa, vanhu nenzvimbo dzakasaririra;

(c)

mitoro nezvinowanikwa kubva mukushandiswa kwemari nezvimwe zviwanikwa, zvinofanira kugoverwa zvakaenzana pakati pevanhu varipo nhasi nevachauya mune ramangwana;

(d) mari yehurumende inofanira kushandiswa zvakajeka, zvine ungwaru, ichichengetedzwa uye nemazvo;

(e)

kushandiswa kwemari kunofanira kuitwa nehanya uye kupa nhoroondo yemashandisirwo emari kunofanira kujeka; uyewo

(f)

kukwereta mari kwehurumende nekunyorerana pasi kune chekuita nechikwereti chenyika kunofanira kuitwa nenzira yakajeka uye inobatsira nyika yeZimbabwe.

(2) Hakuna mitero icharipiswa vanhu kunze kwekunge chete zvabvumirwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana kuti neMutemo weParamende.

299 Basa reParamende pakuona nezvemari inounganidzwa neHurumende nekushandiswa kwayo

(1) Paramende inofanira kuongorora nekuona mashandisirwo emari neHurumende, maKomisheni ese nemapoka nevamiririri vehurumende pamatanho ese, kusanganisira mapoka anoona nezvemitemo, nzvimbo dzinoshanda dziri pasi pemasimba ehurumende, makanzuru ematunhu makuru, emaguta nemakanzuru ekumaruwa, kuitira kuona kuti—

(a)

mari yese inounganidzwa ive nenhoroondo yakajeka;

(b) mari yese yakashandiswa ionekwe kuti yakashandiswa nenzira kwayo; uyewo kuti

(c)

zvinofanira kucherechedzwa nekuitwa pamari yese yakagoverwa zvaitwa.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutaura nzira dzinoshandiswa neParamende pakuongorora nekuona mashandisirwo emari ataurwa muchikamu chidiki

(1). 300 Panogumira mari ingakweretwa neNyika, chikwereti chehurumende nevimbiso yekuzobhadhara chikwereti

(1) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutara panogumira—

(a)

mari kana zvimwe zvingakweretwa neNyika;

(b) chikwereti cheHurumende; uyewo

(c)

zvikwereti nezvimwe zvinovimbiswa neNyika kuti ichabadhara kana kuti ichazodzorera;

uye, panogumira mari kana zvingakweretwa hapafaniri kudarikidzwa pasina kupihwa mvumo neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutara zvinobvumirwa nezvinotarisirwa pakuvimbisa kweHurumende kubhadhara zvikwereti.

(3) Mukati memazuva makumi matanhatu mushure mekunge Hurumende yaita chibvumirano chekukwereta mari kana chekuvimbisa kuzobhadhara, Gurukota rinoona nezvemari rinofanira kuita kuti zvakabvumiranwa zviburitswe muGazette.

(4) Gurukota rinoona nezvemari rinofanira—

(a)

kaviri kana kupfuura pagore, kuudza Paramende mashandiro ezvinhu izvi—

(i) zvikwereti zvakatorwa neHurumende; uye

(ii) zvikwereti zvakavimbiswa kuzobhadharwa neHurumende;

(b) panguva imwe chete iyoyo inenge ichiziviswa nezvemari inofungidzirwa kuti ichaunganidzwa neinofungidzirwa kuti ichashandiswa kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika sekutaurwa kwazvakaitwa muchikamu 305, kutura muParamende mashoko akakwana maererano nechikwereti chenyika yeZimbabwe. 301 Kugoverwa kwemari pakati pehurumende dzematunhu makuru nematunhu madiki

(1) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuzivisa zviri maererano ne—

(a)

kugoverwa zvakaenzana kwemari dzekushandisa pakati pemakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta nemakanzuru ematunhu madiki; uye

(b) zvimwewo chero zvipi zvazvo zvingagoverwa kumatunhu makuru nekumakanzuru, pamwe chete nezvinotarisirwa pakugoverwa kwezvinhu izvi.

(2) Mutemo wakataurwa nezvawo muchikamu chidiki (1) unofanira kutarisa, kusanganisira zvimwewo—

(a)

zvido zvenyika;

(b) urongwa hungavapo maererano nechikwereti chenyika nezvimwe zvinofanira kuitwa nenyika;

(c)

zvinodikanwa nezvinobatsira hurumende yenyika, asi zvichisarudzwa nenzira yakakodzera;

(d) chido chekuti pave nezvinobatsira vanhu kusanganisira zvikoro nezvipatara, mvura, migwagwa, nzvimbo dzekutandarira pamwe chete nemagetsi kunzvimbo dzakasaririra;

(e)

kuvapo kwemari nekuitwa zvakanaka kwemabasa nemakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta nemakanzuru ekumaruwa.

(f)

zvebudiriro nezvimwe zvinodiwa nemakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta nemakanzuru ekumaruwa; uye

(g) kusaenzana mune zveupfumi kunowanikwa pakati pematunhu makuru uye mukati mematunhu aya.

(3) Mari isiri pasi pezvikamu zvishanu kubva muzana inenge yaunganidzwa nenyika mugore rezvekushandiswa kwemari chero ripi zvaro, inofanira kugoverwa kumatunhu makuru nemakanzuru ekumaruwa semugove wavo mugore iroro.

GANHURO 2 Homwe Yemuunganidzwa Wemari Yehurumende 302 Homwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende Pane Homwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende iyo inofanira kuiswa mari dzese dzinobhadhariswa vanhu, dzemitero nedzinenge dzakweretwa, kusanganisira dzimwe mari dzinowanikwa neHurumende dzichibva kwakasiyana-siyana kunze kwekunge Mutemo weParamende—

(a)

uchida kana kuti uchibvumira kuti mari iyoyo inge ichiiswa mune imwe homwe yakaumbwa yakanangana nechimwe chinhu; kana kuti

(b) uchibvumira bazi rakatambira mari iyi kuti riichengete, kana kuchengeta chimwe chikamu chayo kuitira kuti rigone kuishandisa pane zvinoda mari mukuitwa kwebasa raro. 303 Kutorwa kwemari kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende

(1) Hakuna mari ichatorwa kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende kunze kwekunge iri kuenda kundobhadhara zvinhu zvinenge zvabvumirwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Mari inenge yatorwa kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende inofanira chete kuendeswa kumunhu anofanira kubhadharwa.

(3) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kutaura nzira dzinofanira kushandiswa—

(a)

2013

pakutorwa kwemari kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende kana mune imwewo homwe yehurumende; uye

(b) pakuchengetwa nekushandiswa kwemari inenge iri muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende kana mune imwewo homwe yeHurumende. 304 Miripo inobviswa muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende

(1) Miripo yese inobhadhariswa pachikwereti chehurumende inofanira kutorwa muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende.

(2) Mari dzese dzinenge dzashanda pakuunganidzwa kwemari nekuita kuti Homwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende ishande zvakanaka dzinofanira kubva muHomwe iyi.

(3) Takatarisana nechikamu chidiki (1)— “miripo inobhadhariswa pachikwereti” inosanganisira mari inobereka, mari inobatwa semuripo pamari inenge yakweretwa, mari inoiswa padivi kuti izobatsira kubhadhara zvikwereti neimwe mari ingangotsvagwa pachishandiswa Homwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende sechivimbiso chekuti mari iyoyo ichazodzorerwa, pamwe chete nekuripwa nekudzikinurwa kwemari dzakakweretwa. GANHURO 3 Mvumo yeKushandiswa kweMari yeHomwe yemuUnganidzwa weMari yeHurumende 305 Kugoverwa kwemari kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende

(1) Gore rimwe nerimwe Gurukota rinoona nezvemari rinofanira kuturira kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika gwaro renhoroondo yemari inofungidzirwa kuti ichaunganidzwa pamwe chete neichashandiswa neHurumende mumwaka wezvemari unotevera.

(2) Mari inofungidzirwa kuti ichaunganidzwa pamwe chete neinofungidzirwa kuti ichashandiswa inofanira kuturirwa kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika sezvakataurwa muchikamu chidiki (1) pazuva rinosangana nhengo dzeDare iri mwaka wezvemari usati watanga kana kuti mazuva makumi matatu asati apfuura kubva panotangira mwaka wezvemari mumwe nemumwe, asi kana Paramende ikaparadzwa zvekuti zvinenge zvisisakwanisiki kuti nhoroondo yezvemari inofungidzirwa kuti ichaunganidzwa neinofungidzirwa kuti ichashandiswa iziviswe kuDare iri panguva iyoyo, zvinenge zvava kuzoturwa kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika mukati memazuva makumi matatu kubva pakasangana Dare iri kekutanga kubva parakaparadzwa.

(3) Chikamu chimwe nechimwe pane zvakadomwa pazasi chinofanira kuzivisa mari yachinofungidzira kuti chichawana pamwe chete nezvachinofungidzira kuti chichashandisa pa—

(a)

Komisheni imwe neimwe yakaumbwa kuburikidza neBumbiro reMitemo rino;

(b) hofisi yemukuru anoongorora kushandiswa kwemari yeHurumende;

(c)

Chikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva;

(d)

Dare reMadzishe; uyewo

(e)

boka chero ripi zvaro rakaumbwa kuburikidza neMutemo weParamende.

(4) Kana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika rikabvumirana nemari inofungidzirwa kuti ichaunganidzwa pamwe chete neinofungidzirwa kuti ichashandiswa mumwaka wezvemari, tisingatarisi mari inobuda kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino kana kuti pasi peMutemo weParamende, Gurukota rinoona nezvemari rinofanira kuita kuti Bhiri reMutemo wekugoverwa kwemari riparurwe muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, uye Bhiri reMutemo iri rinofanira—

(a)

kuti pave nemari ichatorwa muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende kuti ishandiswe kubhadhara zvinoda mari zvinenge zvabvumiranwa; uye

(b) kuti mari igoverwe kuzvinhu zvinenge zvakadomwa pane zvinofungidzirwa kuti zvichada mari, kuchitevedzwa zvikamu zvakasiyana zvinoda mari zvinenge zvabvumirwa.

(5) Kana mari inenge yagoverwa kuti ishandiswe pane zvakadomwa mumutemo wezvekugoverwa kwemari isingakwani, kana kuti mari ichidirwa zvinhu zvakanga zvisina kugoverwa mari, Gurukota rinoona nezvemari richaona kuti paturwa kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, imwe nhoroondo yezvemari inofungidzirwa kuti ichadiwa. Kana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika ratambira nhoroondo iyi, Gurukota rinoona nezvemari richaita kuti Bhiri reMutemo wekugoverwa kwemari rinotevera rekutanga, riparurwe kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kuitira kuti mari inodikanwa kubva kuHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende iburitswe. 306 Mvumo yekushandisa mari inenge isati yagoverwa

(1) Mutemo weParamende unogona kubvumira Mutungamiriri weNyika kuti ape mvumo yekuti kutorwe mari kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yehurumende yekushandisa kubhadhara zvinoda mari zvakanga zvisina kuzivikanwa kana kuti zvinoda mari yakawanda kupfuura yakange yagoverwa pasi pemutemo chero upi zvawo, asi—

(a)

mutemo haufaniri kubvumira kutorwa kwemari inodarika chikamu chimwe chete nechidimbu kubva muzana chemari yese yakagoveva pakugoverwa kwakapedzisirwa kuitwa muMutemo wekugoverwa kwemari;

(b) mari chero ipi zvayo inotorwa pasi peMutemo wekugoverwa kwemari uyu inofanira kusanganiswa pamwe chete nemari inozowedzerwa pane inofungidzirwa kuti ichaunganidzwa neinofungidzirwa kuti ichashandiswa muurongwa hwekugoverwa kwemari, uye kana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika ratambira urongwa hwemari uhu, mari iyi inofanira kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende pachishandiswa mumwe mutemo unotevera wekutanga, wekugoverwa kwemari.

(2) Kana Mutemo wekugoverwa kwemari mumwaka wezvemari usati wava kushanda pakutanga kwemwaka wezvemari wegore iroro, Mutemo weParamende unogona kupa Mutungamiriri weNyika mvumo yekuti mari ikwanise kutorwa kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yehurumende kuti pave nemari yekushandisa mumabasa eHurumende mumwedzi mina yekutanga yemwaka wezvemari, asi—

(a)

Mutemo haufaniri kubvumidza kutorwa kwemari inopfuura chikamu chimwe chete kubva muzvitatu chemari yaifungidzirwa kuti ichashanda mumwaka wezvemari wakapfuura;

(b) mari chero ipi zvayo ichatorwa pasi peMutemo uyu inofanira kusanganiswa muMutemo wezvekugovewa kwemari wemwaka wezvemari wegore riri kutaurwa nezvaro, asi inofanira kupatsanurwa ichiiswa muzvikamu zvakasiyana zvekushandiswa kwemari.

(3) Kana Paramende ikaparadzwa pasati pava nemari yakakwana inodikanwa pakuita mabasa eHurumende, Mutemo weParamende unogona kubvumidza Mutungamiriri weNyika kupa mvumo yekuti mari itorwe kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende kuitira kuti zvinoda mari zviitwe kusvikira kwapera mwedzi mitatu mushure mekunge Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika ratanga kuita misangano zvichitevera kuparadzwa kwaro, asi mari chero ipi zvayo inenge yatorwa pachishandiswa Mutemo uyu inofanira kuiswa muMutemo wekugoverwa kwemari asi yakapatsanurwa ichiiswa muzvikamu zvakasiyana zvekushandiswa kwemari. 307 Kushandiswa kwemari zvisina mvumo

(1) Kana zvikaonekwa kuti kwakashandiswa mari yakawanda kupfuura yakagoverwa pasi peChikamu chino, kana kuti zvikaonekwa kuti pane mari yakashandiswa pazvinhu zvakange zvisina kugoverwa mari pasi peChikamu chino, Gurukota rinoona nezvemari rinofanira kutura Bhiri reMutemo kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika richikumbira mvumo yekupfuurira mberi nekushandiswa kwemari uku kwakanga kusina kubvumirwa.

(2) Bhiri reMutemo rataurwa nezvaro muchikamu chidiki (1) rinofanira kuturwa kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika nekukurumidza, uye chero zvaita sei, mukati memazuva makumi matanhatu kubva panguva inenge yazivikanwa kuti pakashandiswa mari yakange isina kubvumirwa.

GANHURO 4 Kuchengetedzwa kweMari nePfuma yeHurumende 308 Mabasa evachengeti vemari nepfuma yehurumende

(1) Muchikamu chino— “mari yehurumende” inosanganisira mari chero ipi zvayo yeHurumende kana kuti yakachengetwa neHurumende kana mapoka nevamiririri veHurumende, kusanganisira makanzuru emaguta neematunhu makuru neemaruwa, mapoka anoona nezvemitemo, nemakambani anoshanda ari pasi pehurumende; “pfuma yehurumende” zvinoreva pfuma chero ipi zvayo yeHurumende kana kuti yakachengetwa neHurumende kana mapoka nevamiririri veHurumende, kusanganisira makanzuru emaguta neematunhu makuru neemaruwa, mapoka anoona nezvemitemo, nemakambani anoshanda ari pasi pehurumende.

(2) Ibasa remunhu wese anoona nezvekushandiswa kwemari yeHurumende kuchengetedza mari iyi nekuona kuti yashandiswa pazvinhu zvakabvumidzwa zviri pamutemo chete uye muuwandu hwakabvumirwa zviri pamutemo.

(3) Ibasa remunhu wese anoona nezvekuchengetwa nekushandiswa kwepfuma yehurumende kuchengetedza pfuma iyi nekuona kuti haina kurasika, kuparadzwa, kukanganiswa kana kushandiswa pasiripo kana zvisizvo.

(4) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti kutyorwa kwezviri muzvikamu zvidiki (2) ne (3) kukurumidze kuonekwa uyewo kuti kurangwa kwevanhu vanenge vatyora zviri muzvikamu izvi kukurumidze uye, pakakodzera, kuti zvinenge zvakakanganiswa kana kutorwa zvidzorerwe. GANHURO 5 Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari Munyika 309 Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari nemabasa ake

(1) Panofanira kuva neMuongorori Mukuru wezveMari uyo anoita basa rehurumende asi asiri mushandi wehurumende.

(2) Mabasa eMuongorori Mukuru wezveMari ndeanoti—

(a)

kuongorora magwaro ezvemari, magwaro anoratidza mashandisirwo akaitwa mari nemashandisirwo emari mumapazi ese ehurumende, mumapoka nevamiririri vehurumende, makanzuru emaguta neematunhu makuru uye makanzuru ekumaruwa;

(b) kana achinge akumbirwa neHurumende, kuongorora magwaro ezvekushandiswa kwemari muboka chero ripi zvaro reHurumende, mapoka anoona nezvemitemo, nemakambani anoshanda pasi pehurumende;

(c)

kuraira kuti patorwe matanho ekugadzirisa zvisina kumira zvakanaka mukushandiswa nekuchengetedzwa kwemari yehurumende; uyewo

(d) kuita chero rimwe basa raangapihwa kana kurairwa kuti aite neMutemo weParamende.

(3) Vashandi vehurumende vanofanira kuita zvavanenge varairwa neMuongorori Mukuru wezveMari sekupihwa kwazvakaitwa muchikamu chidiki (2)(c). 310 Kudomwa kweMuongorori Mukuru wezveMari

(1) Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari anoiswa pachinzvimbo neMutungamiriri weNyika apihwa mvumo neParamende.

(2) Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari anofanira kuva mugari wemuZimbabwe anosarudzwa kuchitariswa kuvimbika kwake uye anofanira kunge akakodzera kushanda semuongorori wemagwaro ezvemari kwemakore anosvika kana kupfuura gumi.

(3) Urefu hwenguva inobvumirwa kuti munhu ave pachinzvimbo cheMuongorori Mukuru wezveMari makore asingapfuuri matanhatu uye munhu uyu haafaniri kudomwa kuva Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari mushure mekunge amboita basa iri kwemakore matanhatu kana kupfuura akatevedzana, kana kuti asina kutevedzana asi makore aya achisvika gumi nemaviri kana abatanidzwa.

(4) Asati atanga basa, Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari anofanira kuita mhiko yekuvimbika nekuzvipira kubasa iri pamberi peMutungamiriri weNyika kana mumwe munhu anenge abvumirwa neMutungamiriri weNyika sekutarwa kwazvakaitwa muNhamatidzwa 3. 311 Rusununguko rweMuongorori Mukuru wezvemari Pakushanda basa rake, Muongorori Mukuru wezvemari haafaniri kutaurirwa zvekuita nemumwe munhu kusiya kwekutevedza zviri mumutemo.

312 Mari inotambiriswa Muongorori Mukuru wezvemari

(1) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuzivisa mari nezvimwe zvinopihwa Muongorori Mukuru wezvemari izvo zvinotarwa pamwe chete nekubvumirwa neMutungamiriri weNyika zvichitevera kupihwa mazano neGurukota rinoona nezvemari.

(2) Mari inotambirwa neMuongorori Mukuru wezvemari inofanira kubva muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende, uye haifaniri kudzikiswa paanenge achiri pachinzvimbo ichi. 313 Kubviswa pachinzvimbo kweMuongorori Mukuru wezvemari

(1) Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari anogona kubviswa pachinzvimbo nekuda kwezvikonzero izvi chete—

(a)

kutadza kuita basa rake nekuda kwekurwara nepfungwa kana nemuviri;

(b) kukundikana kuita basa kwakanyanya;

(c) kuratidza unhu hunoshoreka zvikuru;

(2) Kana Gurukota rinoona nezvemari, richiwirirana nekomiti yeparamende inoona nezvemari yehurumende, rikazivisa Mutungamiriri weNyika kuti nyaya yekubviswa pachinzvimbo kweMuongorori Mukuru wezveMari inoda kuongororwa, Mutungamiriri weNyika anofanira kuumba dare rinoongorora nyaya iyi.

(3) Dare rinenge raumbwa sezvakapihwa muchikamu chidiki (2) rinofanira kunge rine vanhu vanosvika kana kupfuura vatatu vanodomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika, uye vanofanira kunge vakamira sezvinotevera—

(a)

mumwe chete wavo anofanira kunge ari munhu akamboshanda semutongi; uye

(b) mumwe chete wavo anofanira kusarudzwa kubva pavanhu vatatu vanenge vakasarudzwa nebazi kana sangano rinenge rakavambwa kuburikidza nemutemo kuti rimiririre vaongorori vezvemari dzeHurumende muZimbabwe.

(4) Bazi kana sangano rataurwa muchikamu chidiki (3)(b) rinofanira kusarudza vanhu vataurwa muchikamu chidiki ichi kana vachinge vanzi vaite izvi neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(5) Dare rinoumbwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu chidiki (2) rinofanira kuongorora nyaya yekubviswa pachinzvimbo kweMuongorori Mukuru wezvemari, uye kana radaro, rinofanira kuzivisa zvinenge zvabuda muongororo iyi kuMutungamiriri weNyika, robva rapa pfungwa dzaro maererano nekuti Muongorori Mukuru wezvemari obviswa pachinzvimbo here kana kuti kwete, uye kana dare iri rikati Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari anofanira kubviswa, Mutungamiriri weNyika, kuburikidza nezvaanotaura izvo zvinodhindwa nechidhindo chenyika, anobvisa Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari pachinzvimbo.

(6) Dare rinodzikwa pasi pechikamu chidiki (2) rine kodzero nemasimba akafanana neenhengo dzemakomisheni anodzikwa pasi peMutemo wezvemakomisheni anoona nezveongororo [Chitsauko 10:07], kana mumwewo mutemo chero upi zvawo unotsiva Mutemo uyu. 314 Vashandi vari pasi peMuongorori mukuru wezvemari Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuzivisa nezvekudzikwa kweboka revanopinza vanhu vanobatsira Muongorori Mukuru wezveMari basa kuti vamubatsire mubasa rake, uye mutemo uyu unofanira kuzivisa maererano ne—

(a)

zvinodiwa pakuti vanhu ava vanzi vakakodzera kuita basa iri;

(b) mashandiro avanoita, zvavanotarisirwa kuita nezvinoitwa pakuvaranga nekuvadzora;

(c)

kushanda zvakasununguka, zvisina kurerekera divi rimwe chete uye kuvimbika kwevanhu ava; uyewo

(d) marongerwo nemashandiro akanaka ehofisi yeMuongorori Mukuru wezvemari. GANHURO 6 zvakasiyana-siyana

315 Kutengwa kwezvinhu nezvimwe zvibvumirano zvinoitwa nehurumende

(1) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kudzika zvinofanira kutevedzwa pakutengwa kwezvinhu nezvekushandisa neHurumende pamwe chete nemapoka ayo uye nevamiririri vayo padanho chero ripi zvaro, kuitira kuti kutengwa kwezvinhu nezvekushandisa kuitwe nenzira iri pachena, yakaenzanirana, iri pachokwadi uye isingatambisi mari.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti pave nehurukuro uye kushanda kwakanaka kwezvibvumirano zvinoitwa neHurumende zvinotevera—

(a)

zvibvumirano zvekushanda pamwe chete nevamwe;

(b) zvibvumirano zvekuvakwa nekushandiswa kwezvivakwa nezvekushandisa; uye

(c)

zvibvumirano zvekuchera zvicherwa nedzimwewo mvumo;

kuita kuti pave nekuitira zvinhu pachena, muchokwadi pasingatambiswe nekuparadzwa mari. 316 Kutungamirirwa kwemapoka anoona nezvemitemo Mutemo weParamende unofanira kuita kuti pave nemashandiro akanaka emapoka anoona nezvemitemo, zvikuru sei, unofanira kuita kuti vatungamiriri vemapoka aya vatungamirire kwenguva yakatarwa uye kuti kana vachidzorerwa pazvinzvimbo, zviitwe nekuda kwekugona basa kwavo. 317 Bhanga guru renyika

(1) Kune bhanga guru iro richazivikanwa seBhanga Guru reNyika, rine chinangwa che—

(a)

kuraira mashandisirwo emari;

(b) kuchengetedza mari yeZimbabwe richikoshesa kuvandudzwa kweupfumi nenzira yakaenzana uye inoenderera mberi kwenguva refu; uyewo

(c)

kuumba nekudzika murayiro wezvemashandisirwo emari.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unogona kutara mamiriro nemarongerwo eBhanga Guru reNyika pamwe chete nekupa kana kuraira mamwe mabasa arinofanira kuita.

CHITSAUKO 18 Mitemo Yakasiyana-siyana nemimwe Yakawedzerwa GANHURO 1 Mitemo Yakasiyana-siyana Maererano nemaKomisheni 318 Kushandiswa kwezviri muGanhuro 1 Chikamu chino chinobata maKomisheni akaumbwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino, pamwe chete nenhengo dzeKomisheni imwe neimwe yerudzi urwu. 319 MaKomisheni anofanira kuva masangano MaKomisheni masangano akaumbwa nemutemo, ane basa risingaperi uye anokwanisa kumhan’ara kana kumhan’arirwa mhosva kumatare edzimhosva. 320 Nhengo dzemaKomisheni nemashandiro adzo

(1) Kunze kwekunge zvakanyorwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino, nhengo imwe neimwe yeKomisheni ichapihwa basa kwenguva inosvika makore mashanu panguva imwe chete uye pachikwanisa kuwedzerwa mamwe makore mashanu chete chete.

(2) Nhengo dzemaKomisheni, kunze kwemaKomisheni aya—

(a)

maKomisheni akazvimiririra;

(b) Komisheni inoona nezveUtongi;

(c)

Komisheni inoona nezvekurwisana nehuwori; uye

(d) Komisheni inoona nezveminda; dzinoshanda kwenguva inodiwa neMutungamiriri weNyika.

(3) Nenzira inoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino, nhengo dzeParamende, nhengo dzemakanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta nedzemakanzuru kana kuti vashandi vemumapazi ari pasi peHurumende hadzibvumirwi kuva nhengo dzemaKomisheni.

(4) Kana Komisheni ina sachigaro nemutevedzeri wasachigaro, vanhu ava vanofanira kuti mumwe ave munhukadzi mumwe ave munhurume.

(5) Dzisati dzatanga basa, nhengo dzemaKomisheni dzinofanira kuita mhiko yekuvimbika uye yebasa pamberi peMutungamiriri weNyika kana mumwe munhu akapihwa mvumo yekuitisa izvi neMutungamiriri weNyika sekunyorwa kwazvakaitwa muNhamatidzwa 3.

(6) Nhengo dzeKomisheni dzinofanira kutambiriswa mari nekuwana zvimwewo zvine chekuita nebasa sekupihwa kwazvakaitwa muMutemo weParamende, uye mari yavanotambira haifaniri kudzikiswa panguva yavanenge vari pazvinzvimbo.

(7) Mari inotambiriswa nhengo dzemaKomisheni pamwe chete nezvimwe zvavanowana inobva kuHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende. 321 Mabasa emaKomisheni nezvaanofanira kuita

(1) Mutemo weParamende unogona kupa mamwe mabasa kuKomisheni uye unogona kuzivisa zvinokwanisa kuitwa nezvisingakwanisi kuitwa neKomisheni, ndokunge rusununguko nekuita basa nemazvo kweKomisheni iyi kusingakanganiswi.

(2) Mutemo weParamende wataurwa nezvawo muchikamu chidiki (1) unogona kubvumidza Komisheni kuti ipe basa rayo kune vamwe vanhu, asi Komisheni haibvumirwi kuti painopa basa rayo kune vamwe vanhu, ivape masimba ayo ekupinza basa kana ekupa mazano kuhofisi chero ipi zvayo yakaumbwa kuburikidza neBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(3) Nenzira inoenderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino, chero chipi zvacho chinosungwa neKomisheni chinofanira kubvumiranwa nenhengo zhinji dzinenge dziri pamusangano wekuita chisungo ichi.

(4) Mutemo weParamende unogona kutaura zvinofanira kutevedzwa neKomisheni, uye kana pasina zvapihwa nemutemo, Komisheni inogona kuzvigadzirira zvainofanira kutevedza, asi zvinotevedzwa izvi chero zvipi zvazvo, zvinofanira kuva zvakaenzanirana uye zvichikurudzira kuitira zvinhu pachena mumashandiro eKomisheni iyi. 322 Kuwaniswa mari kwemaKomisheni Paramende inofanira kuona kuti mari dzakakwana dzagoverwa kumaKomisheni kuti akwanise kuita basa rawo nemazvo. 323 Makomisheni anofanira kuzivisa mashandiro awo kuParamende kamwe chete pagore

(1) Komisheni imwe neimwe inofanira kutura kuParamende kuburikidza neGurukota rainoshanda iri pasi paro, gwaro rinotsanangura zvizere mashandiro nemabasa aro arakaita pagore. Gwaro iri rinofanira kuturwa mwedzi waKurume wegore rinotevera gore riri kutaurwa nezvaro usati wapera.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unogona kuti Komisheni ipewo mamwe magwaro pamusoro pegwaro rinotsanangura zvizere mashandiro ayakaita pagore rakatsanangurwa muchikamu chidiki 1 uye unogona kutara nzira dzekuti magwaro aya anopirwa sei.

GANHURO 2 Zvakasiyana-siyana 324 Kushanda nemwoyo wese pane zvinotarisirwa neBumbiro reMitemo Zvese zvinofanira kuitwa zvakanangana neBumbiro reMitemo zvinofanira kuitwa nemwoyo wese uye nekukasika. 325 Kuwaniswa mari kwemapoka nemasangano akapihwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo

(1) Hurumende inofanira kuona kuti mari dzakakwana dzapihwa—

(a)

kumaKomisheni nemamwe masangano akaumbwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino, kuti agone kuita mabasa awo nemazvo.

(b) kuParamende, kuti iyo nemakomiti ayo ikwanise kuita misangano panguva dzese dzakakodzera; uye

(c)

kumapazi nemapoka ese enyika neHurumende kuti akwanise kuita basa rawo sezvaanofanira pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(2) MaKomisheni nemamwe masangano akaumbwa kuburikidza neBumbiro reMitemo rino anofanira kupihwa mukana wakafanira kuti azivise Kukomiti yeParamende nezvemari dzaachagoverwa mumwaka wezvemari mumwe nemumwe. 326 Mitemo inotarisirwa munyika dzepasi rese

(1) Mitemo yezvinotarisirwa munyika dzepasi rese inotambirwawo semitemo yeZimbabwe, kunze kwekunge isingawirirani nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino kana kuti neMutemo weParamende.

(2) Kana mutemo uchitsanangurwa, dare redzimhosva rimwe nerimwe rinofanira kutambira matsanangurirwo emutemo chero upi zvawo unoenderana nemitemo inotarisirwa munyika dzese nenzira inofambirana nemitemo yemuZimbabwe, richisiya mamwe matsanangurirwo anopikisana nemutemo uyu. 327 Zvibvumirano nedzimwe nyika

(1) Muchikamu chino— “sangano repasi rese” zvinoreva sangano rine nhengo mbiri kana kupfuura renyika dzakasununguka kana kuti rine vamiririri venyika mbiri kana kupfuura dzakasununguka vari mariri; “chibvumirano chenyika dzepasi rese” zvinoreva chibvumirano kana chisungo chiri pakati penyika, kana hurumende kana sangano repasi rese rimwe chete kana kupfuura.

(2) Chibvumirano chepasi rese chawiriranwa kana kuti chaitwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kana pasi pemasimba eMutungamiriri weNyika—

(a)

hachina simba rekubata Zimbabwe kunze kwekunge chabvumirwa neParamende; uye

(b) hachitambirwi semutemo weZimbabwe kunze kwekunge chaiswa mumutemo kuburikidza neMutemo weParamende.

(3) Chibvumirano chisiri chibvumirano chepasi rese asi chiri—

(a) chakabvumiranwa kana kuti chakaitwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti pachishandiswa mvumo yeMutungamiriri weNyika nesangano rimwe chete kana akawanda ekunze kwenyika; uye

(b) chinoda kuti pave nemari dzinozobviswa neZimbabwe; hachibati kana kusunga Zimbabwe kusvikira chabvumwa neParamende.

(4) Mutemo weParamende unogona kuti zvikamu zvidiki (2) ne (3)—

(a)

hazvishandi pachibvumirano chenyika dzepasi rese chero chipi zvacho; kana kuti

(b) zvinoshanda mushure mekunge zvambogadziriswa kuti zviwirirane nechibvumirano chepasi rese chagara chiripo kana kuti chinokwana mune zvimwe zvibvumirano zvakadaro.

(5) Kuburikidza nechibvumirano chayo, Paramende inogona kuti chibvumirano chenyika dzepasi rese chero chipi zvacho hachina basa nekupihwa mvumo sezviri muchikamu chidiki (2), asi chisungo chakadai hachishandi kuzvibvumirano zvine chekuita ne—

(a)

kutorwa kana kugoverwa kwemari inobva kuHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende; kana

(b) kunatsurudzwa kwemutemo weZimbabwe chero kupi zvako.

(6) Kana mutemo uchitsanangurwa, dare redzimhosva rimwe nerimwe rinofanira kutsanangura mitemo nenzira inowirirana nechibvumirano chenyika dzepasi rese chero chipi zvacho chinobata Zimbabwe, richisiya mamwe matsanangurirwo anopikisana nechibvumirano ichi. 328 Kunatsurudzwa kweBumbiro reMitemo

(1) Muchikamu chino— “Bhiri reBumbiro reMitemo” zvinoreva Bhiri reMutemo rakananga nekunatsurudza Bumbiro reMitemo rino; “mutemo wenguva yakatarwa” zvinoreva mutemo weBumbiro reMitemo rino unopa urefu hwenguva inobvumidzwa kuti munhu ange ari pachinzvimbo mumabasa eHurumende.

(2) Mutemo weParamende unonatsurudza Bumbiro reMitemo rino unofanira kuita izvi nenzira yakajeka.

(3) Bhiri reBumbiro reMitemo harikwanisi kuturwa kudare reSeneti kana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika sezviri muchikamu 131 kunze kwekunge Mutauriri weParamende apa chiziviso chemazuva anosvika kana kupfuura makumi mapfumbamwe muGazette achizivisa zvinangwa zveBhiri iri.

(4) Mushure mekunge Mutauriri weParamende achangobva mukuzivisa nezveBhiri reBumbiro reMitemo sezvakapihwa muchikamu chidiki (3), Paramende inofanira kukoka ruzhinji rweZimbabwe kuti rwupe pfungwa dzarwo maererano nezviri muBhiri iri pamisangano yeruzhinji rwevanhu uye kuburikidza nepfungwa dzakaitwa zvekunyorwa, uye inofanira kukoka misangano nekugadzira nzvimbo dzinoita kuti vanhu vape pfungwa dzavo.

(5) Bhiri reBumbiro reMitemo rinofanira kupasiswa parinoverengwa kekupedzisira neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika nemuSeneti kuburikidza nezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzedare rimwe nerimwe zvichibvuma kuti upase.

(6) Kana Bhiri reBumbiro reMitemo richida kunatsurudza chinhu chero chipi zvacho chiri muChitsauko 4 kana muChitsauko 16—

(a)

mukati memwedzi mitatu mushure mekunge rapasiswa neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika nemuSeneti sezvinotarisirwa muchikamu chidiki (5), rinofanira kuitirwa referenda yenyika yese; uye

(b) kana rikatambirwa neruzhinji rwevavhoti mureferenda, Mutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika anofanira kuti bhiri iri riendeswe kuMutungamiriri weNyika nekukasika, uyo anofanira kuribvuma kuti wava mutemo wenyika.

(7) Tisingatarisi zvimwe zvakatsanangurwa muchikamu chino, kunatsurudzwa kwemutemo wenguva yakatarwa, uyo unoita kuti urefu hwenguva inobvumidzwa kuti munhu ange ari pachinzvimbo mumabasa eHurumende huwedzerwe, hakubati munhu akange ari pachinzvimbo ichi kana chimwe chinzvimbo chakafanana nechichi kunatsurudzwa kwemutemo uyu kusati kwaitwa.

(8) Zvikamu zvidiki (6) ne (7) hazvifaniri kunatsurudzwa zvese muBhiri reBumbiro reMitemo rimwe chete uye kunatsurudzwa kwezvikamu izvi zvese hakufaniri kuendeswa kureferenda imwe chete.

(9) Chikamu chino chinokwanisa kunatsurudzwa chete pachitevedzwa zvakatsanangurwa muzvikamu zvidiki (3), (4), (5) ne (6), sezvinonzi chikamu chino chiri muChitsauko 4.

(10) Kana Bhiri reBumbiro reMitemo rikaendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuti aritambire nekurisaina, rinofanira kunge rine zvinotevera—

(a)

gwaro rinobva kuMutauriri weParamende rinoratidza kuti pakuvhoterwa kwaro kekupedzesira rakatsigirwa neuwandu hwezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzeParamende; uye

(b) gwaro rinobva kuMutungamiriri weSeneti rinoratidza kuti pakuvhoterwa kwaro kekupedzesira rakatsigirwa neuwandu hwezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzeSeneti. 329 Kutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino nemitemo ichashandiswa pashanduko yekubva kuBumbiro reMitemo rekare nezvinochengetedzwa Nhamatidzwa 6 inoshanda panotangawo kushanda Bumbiro reMitemo rino, panenge paparadzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rekare uye pava kuuya shanduko itsva inenge yadzikwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino. GANHURO 3 Dudziro 330 Kushandiswa kwezviri muGanhuro 3 Chikamu chino chinoshanda mukududzirwa kweBumbiro reMitemo rino kunze kwekunge zviripo zvine mamwe manzwisisiro. 331 Zvakasiyana-siyana zvinofanira kutevedzwa pakududzirwa kweBumbiro reMitemo Chikamu 46, pamwe chete neshanduko dzingada kuitwa, chinoshandiswa mukutsanangura Bumbiro reMitemo rino kusiya kwezviri muChitsauko 4. 332 Tsananguro MuBumbiro reMitemo rino— “Mutemo weParamende” unoreva—

(a) mutemo chero upi zvawo wakapasiswa neParamende ukagashirwa nekusainwa neMutungamiriri weNyika kuti wava mutemo wenyika.

(b) mutemo wakanga uchishanda muZimbabwe seMutemo weParamende zuva rekutanga kushandisa Bumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, kusanganisira—

(i) mumwe mutemo chero upi zvawo uri mugwaro remutemo rakadzokororwa rakaitwa mugore ra1996 pasi pemasimba emutemo wekugadzirwa nekudzokororwa kwemutemo weStatute Law Compilation and Revision Act [Chitsauko 1:03]; uye

(ii) mumwe mutemo chero upi zvawo ungadaro wakasiirirwa mugwaro remutemo rakadzokororwa rataurwa nezvaro mundima diki (i), asi mutemo uyu ukaramba uchishanda kunyange wakasiirirwa kudaro; uye

(iii) mutemo chero upi zvawo wakadzikwa neParamende yeZimbabwe mushure megwaro remutemo rakadzokororwa

rataurwa nezvaro mundima diki (i) asi zuva racho rekuti utange kushanda risati rasvika; “unhu pakuitwa kwemabasa eUtungamiriri” zvinosanganisira chero zvakafungwa, kana zvakaitwa kana kusaitwa nemushandi weHurumende kana nemunhu akanga achiita basa rine chekuita neHurumende, nekukundikana kana kuramba kwemunhu iyeye kuti asvike pakufunga ikoko kana kuita zvakadaro; “kunatsurudza” zvinosanganisira kugadzirisa, kushandura, kuwedzera, kudzima kana kuita kuti zviwirirane kana kufambirana nezviripo. “sarudzo diki” zvinoreva sarudzo yekutsvaga angapinda panenge paita mukana wangomuka mubasa reParamende kana mukanzuru; “Ishe” zvinoreva Ishe ataurwa nezvake muChitsauko 15; “Basa reHurumende” rine tsananguro yarakapihwa muchikamu 199; “Komisheni” zvinoreva Komisheni yakaumbwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino; “Komiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa” zvinoreva komiti ine zita iroro yakaumbwa pasi pechikamu 151; “Minda yekumaruwa” zvinoreva nzvimbo yakatsaurwa pasi peMutemo weParamende uye yakapihwa vanhu nenzira yemutemo wetsika dzedu zvichitungamirirwa naIshe; “Bhiri reBumbiro reMitemo” zvinoreva Bhiri reMutemo uchiri kugadzirwa zvekuti kana ukange wazova mutemo, unoshandura zvakanyorwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino; “Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo” zvinoreva Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rakaumbwa kuburikidza nechikamu 162(a); “Nyaya ine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo” zvinoreva nyaya ine chekuita nemanzwisisiro, kuchengetedzwa kana kushandiswa kweBumbiro reMitemo rino; “Mutemo wetsika” zvinoreva mutemo unoenderana netsika nemagariro eboka kana nharaunda yemuZimbabwe chero ipi zvayo; “Mutemo weunhu hunotarisirwa” zvinoreva mutemo wakanyorwa pasi uri maererano nezveunhu hunotarisirwa kunhengo dzechikwata chakadzidziswa nezveunhu uhu, kusanganisira vanoshanda basa iri zvisiri zvemisi yese, pavanenge vachiita basa kana pavanokundikana kuita basa sekudzidziswa kwavakaitwa; “chikamu chakadzidzira zvekuchengetedza nyika” zvinoreva—

(a) mauto emumvura, epasi neemuchadenga;

(b) mapurisa;

(c) magadhijeri; kana

(d) rimwe boka chero ripi zvaro rakaumbwa nechinangwa chekuita basa reHurumende kuburikidza neMutemo weParamende uye zvikaziviswa neMutemo uyu kuti ichi chikamu chakadzidzira zvekuchengetedza nyika; “zuva racho” zvinoreva zuva rinotanga kushanda Bumbiro reMitemo rino sekutsanagurwa kwazvakaitwa mundima 3(2) yeNhamatidzwa 6;

“Mutemo uri maererano nesarudzo” zvinoreva Mutemo weParamende unotungamirira kuitwa kwesarudzo pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino; “Mwaka wezvemari” zvinoreva nguva yemwedzi gumi nemiviri inopera musi wa31 Zvita; “basa” zvinosanganisira simba pamusoro pechinhu chinoitwa pabasa iroro; “Gazette” zvinoreva gwaro rine mashoko eHurumende, kusanganisira zvimwe zvingazoburitswa zvichiwedzera mashoko akatanga kubuda; “sarudzo huru” zvinoreva sarudzo huru dzenyika yese pachisarudzwa—

(a) Mutungamiriri weNyika, Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika nenhengo dzeParamende;

(b) nhengo dzemapoka anotungamirira makanzuru ekumaruwa; “Hurumende” zvinoreva Hurumende yeZimbabwe; “vanoshanda vari pasi pehurumende” zvinoreva sangano kana kambani zvinoshanda nekuita mabasa azvo zvichirairwa zvekuita neHurumende kana nemunhu akamiririra Hurumende kuburikidza nekuva nemugove weupfumi wakakura musangano kana kambani iyi kana zvimwewo zvakadaro; “Dare”, zvinoreva Seneti kana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, kunze kwekunge pakapihwa imwewo tsananguro; “Komisheni yakazvimiririra” zvinoreva Komisheni yakataurwa nezvayo muchikamu 232; “mutongi” zvinoreva mutongi weDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo, Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro, Dare redzimhosva repamusoro, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezvevashandi nevashandirwi kana Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveUtungamiriri; “Komisheni inoona neutongi” zvinoreva Komisheni ine zita iri yakaumbwa kuburikidza nechikamu 189; “mutemo” zvinoreva—

(a) zvese zvakanzi zviitwe pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino kana kuti pasi peMutemo weParamende;

(b) zvese zvakaziviswa mumutemo wakanyorwa; kana

(c) mutemo wese usina kunyorwa asi uchishanda muZimbabwe, kusanganisira mutemo wetsika nemagariro; uye “zvinobvumirwa”, “sezvinobvumirwa”, “zviri pamutemo”, “sezviri pamutemo” zvinofanira kududzirwa nezvazvinoreva; “gweta” zvinoreva munhu anobvumirwa kushanda basa rezvemitemo muZimbabwe; “kanzuru yekumaruwa” zvinoreva kanzuru yakataurwa nezvayo muchikamu 3 cheChitsauko 14; “nhengo”, zvinoenderana—

(a) neKomisheni kana rimwe boka rakaumbwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino, zvinosanganisira sachigaro nemutevedzeri wasachigaro;

(b) neboka rezvemitemo, kanzuru yedunhu guru kana yeguta kana kanzuru yekumatunhu nekumaruwa zvinoreva munhu akagadzwa

kana akadomwa kana kusarudzwa kuti ave mukanzuru, muboka revatungamiriri kana rimwewo boka—

(i) rinoona nezvemitemo, kanzuru yedunhu guru kana yeguta kana kanzuru yekumaruwa; kana

(ii) rine basa rekutungamirira basa reboka rezvemutemo, kanzuru yedunhu guru kana yeguta kana kanzuru yekumaruwa; “nhengo yeParamende” zvinoreva Seneta kana nhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika; “kanzuru yeguta” zvinoreva kanzuru yakaumbwa kuburikidza nechikamu 269 ichiumbirwa guta. “dunhu reguta” zvinoreva guta reBulawayo kana guta reHarare, zvichienderana nekuti nderipi riri kutaurwa nezvaro; “Gurukota” rinosanganisira munhu ari kuita basa reGurukota, zvisinei nezita raangadaidzwa naro; “mutemo wenyika” zvinoreva Mutemo weParamende kana mutemo wakanyorwa, wakadzikwa pasi peMutemo weParamende; “mhiko” inosanganisira kubvumira; “mhosva” zvinoreva mhosva kana mhaka inosungisa; “nguva yedambudziko rakaipisisa” zvinoreva nguva yekuti kuziviswe kuti yava nguva yekusagadzikana munyika pasi pechikamu 113; “munhu” zvinoreva munhu akazvimirira kana boka revanhu, vaungana nekuda kwemutemo kana zvisinei nemutemo; “Mutungamiri weNyika” zvinoreva Mutungamiri weNyika yeZimbabwe; “Mutungamiri weSeneti” zvinoreva Mutungamiri weSeneti akasarudzwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 122; “kanzuru yedunhu guru” zvinoreva kanzuru yakaumbirwa dunhu guru risiri guta kuburikidza nechikamu 268; “basa reHurumende” zvinoreva basa reHurumende rinotambirisirwa mari; “mushandi weHurumende” zvinoreva munhu ari kuita basa kana kuti akafanobata chinzvimbo mubasa reHurumende; “mabasa ekuchengetedzwa kwenyika” zvinoreva basa rekuchengetedza nyika rakataurwa muchikamu 207; “Ishe Seneta” zvinoreva ishe akasarudzwa kuti ave nhengo yeSeneti kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 120(1) (b) kana (c); “kusangana” zvinoreva nguva yekuti nhengo dzeSeneti nedzeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika dzinenge dzichiita misangano pasina kumbozorora kusanganisira nguva yekuti dare reSeneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo anenge achishanda mumakomiti; “zuva rekusangana” zvinoreva zuva chero ripi zvaro repakati pesvondo rakatsanangurwa mumitemo inotevedzwa muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kana Seneti kuti izuva rekusangana zvisinei nekuti dare iroro rinosangana here pazuva iroro kana kuti kwete; “Mutauriri” zvinoreva Mutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika akasarudzwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 126;

“Mitemo inotevedzwa”, inoenderana nechero zvipi zvazvo zvinofanira kuitwa ne—

(a) Seneti, zvinoreva kuti mitemo inotevedzwa neSeneti;

(b) Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, zvinoreva kuti mitemo inotevedzwa neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika;

(c) Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvakabatana, zvinoreva mitemo inotevedzwa nemapoka aya akabatana; yakaumbwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 139; “boka rezvemitemo” zvinoreva—

(a) Komisheni yakaumbwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino; kana

(b) boka remitemo rakaumbwa pasi peMutemo weParamende nechinangwa chakatsanangurwa mumutemo iwoyo, rine nhengo dzaro zhinji dzakapihwa basa neMutungamiriri weNyika, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota, Mutevedzeri weGurukota, rimwe boka rezvemitemo kana neKomisheni yakaumbwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino; “zvinoshandiswa pamutemo” zvinoreva chero chipi chine simba remutemo uye chinoumbwa neMutungamiriri weNyika, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota kana mumwewo munhu kana boka rakaumbwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino kana pasi peMutemo weParamende; “mutero” unosanganisira mari dzinobhadharwa pakupinza zvinhu munyika, mari dzinobhadharwa nguva nenguva, mari dzinobhadharwa dzakanangana nechirongwa kana mari dzinotarisirwa kubhadharwa; “ishe” zvinoreva munhu akadomwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 283; “Zimbabwe” zvinoreva nyika yeZimbabwe; “Komisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe” zvinoreva Komisheni ine zita iroro yakaumbwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 238; “Komisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe” zvinoreva Komisheni ine zita iroro yakaumbwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 242; 333 Zvakanangana neZvitsauko, zvikamu, nezvimwe zvakadaro Kushandiswa kweBumbiro reMitemo rino kusinganongedzi ku—

(a)

Chitsauko, chikamu kana Nhamatidzwa, kuchatorwa sekuti kuri kutaura nezveChitsauko kana chikamu kana Nhamatidzwa yeBumbiro reMitemo rino;

(b) chikamu chidiki chinofanira kunzwisiswa sechinoreva chikamu chidiki chechikamu chiri kutaurwa nezvacho;

(c) ndima inofanira kuverengwa seinoreva ndima yeNhamatidzwa, chikamu, chikamu chidiki kana tsananguro yechiri kutaurwa nezvacho;

(d) chindima chidiki chinofanira kunzwisiswa sechinoreva chindima chidiki chendima kana chindima chidiki chiri kutaurwa nezvacho. 334 Mazwi ari muushoma anosanganisira ari muuzhinji uye ari muuzhinji anosanganisirawo ari muushoma MuBumbiro reMitemo rino, mazwi ari muushoma anosanganisira ari muuzhinji, uye ari muuzhinji anosanganisirawo ari muushoma.

335 Matebhuru nemisoro yezvitsauko nezvikamu Matebhuru ezviri mukati nemisoro yeZvitsauko, Zvikamu nezvimwe zvakanyorwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino hazvisi chikamu cheBumbiro reMitemo, asi zvakangoiswa chete kubatsira kuti zvinhu zviri muBumbiro zvikwanise kuwanikwa nyore. 336 Zvakanangana nenguva

(1) MuBumbiro reMitemo rino, pese pakataurwa nezvenguva pachishandiswa mazuva—

(a)

kutanga pazuva iroro kana kuti kubva pazuva rakataurwa, zuva racho iroro hariverengwi panguva inenge ichitaurwa nezvayo;

(b) panoperera zuva kana kuti kusvika musi wakatarwa, zuva kana musi wacho unofanira kuverengwa panguva iri kutaurwa nezvayo.

(2) Zvichienderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino, pese pekuti nguva inofanira kuitwa chinhu sezvakanyorwa muBumbiro reMitemo rino uchange uri musi weMugovera, Svondo kana zuva rezororo renyika yese, nguva yachanga chichifanira kuitwa inowedzerwa, uye chinhu ichocho chinozoitwa zuva rinotevera risiri Mugovera, Svondo kana zororo renyika yese.

(3) Panotaurwa nezvemwedzi muBumbiro reMitemo rino zvinenge zvichireva mwedzi wepakarenda, uye nguva yemwedzi ichatanga kuverengwa musi uri kutaurwa nezvawo kusvika pazuva rakafanana neiroro remwedzi panoperera nguva iri kutaurwa nezvayo.

(4) Kutaura muBumbiro reMitemo rino pasina kujekesa kuti nguva iri kutaurwa igore kunofanira kunzwisiswa sekuti inguva yakareba mwedzi gumi nemiviri. 337 Zvakanangana nevabati vezvinzvimbo Pese panotaurwa nezvemubati wechinzvimbo muBumbiro reMitemo rino kuburikidza nezita rinoreva hofisi yemunhu iyeye, zvinosanganisira munhu chero upi zvake anotenderwa nemutemo kuti amiririre kana kuti aite mabasa ehofisi iyoyo. 338 Zvakanangana neParamende MuBumbiro reMitemo rino, panodiwa kuti gwaro renhoroondo yezvakaitika riendeswe kuParamende, gwaro iri rinofanira kupihwa kana kuturwa kuSeneti nekuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika. 339 Mazano nekubvunza mazano

(1) Pese panodiwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino kuti munhu kana boka ritore matanho kuburikidza nemazano emumwe munhu, munhu iyeye kana boka iroro rinofanira—

(a)

kuzivisa mumwe munhu uyu, kuburikidza netsamba, zvaanotarisira kuita, uye robva rapa munhu uyu ruzivo rwakakwana kuitira kuti munhu uyu anzwisise mamiriro nezvinozoitika nekuda kwechiitiko ichi; uye

(b) kuwanisa mumwe munhu uyu mukana wakakwana wekuti abatsire nemazano; uye munhu kana boka zvinotarisirwa kutevedzera mazano anenge apihwa nemumwe munhu uyu.

(2) Pese panodiwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino kuti munhu kana boka ribvunze mazano kubva kune mumwe munhu, kana kutora matanho mushure mekubvunza mazano kune mumwe munhu, munhu iyeye kana boka iroro rinofanira—

(a)

2013

kuzivisa mumwe munhu uyu, kuburikidza netsamba, zvaanotarisirwa kuita, uye robva rapa munhu uyu ruzivo rwakakwana kuitira kuti munhu uyu anzwisise mamiriro nezvinozoitika nekuda kwechiitiko ichi;

(b) kuwanisa mumwe munhu uyu mukana wakakwana wekuti abatsire nemazano kana zvaanofunga kuti ndizvo zvinofanira kuitwa; uye

(c)

kunyatsofungisisa pamusoro pemazano kana zvinofungwa nemumwe munhu uyu kuti ndizvo zvinofanira kuitwa panyaya iripo;

asi munhu kana boka harimanikidzwi kutevedzera zvinofungwa nemumwe munhu uyu kuti ndizvo zvinofanira kuitwa. 340 Kuiswa kwevanhu pazvinzvimbo

(1) Kunze kwezvakaziviswa muBumbiro reMitemo rino, simba rekuisa munhu pachinzvimbo pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino rinosanganisirawo simba rakafanana ne—

(a) rekudzorera munhu pachinzvimbo chimwe chete;

(b) rekupa munhu chinzvimbo chepamusoro kana kumubvisa kune imwe hofisi achipinda muhofisi iyoyo;

(c) rekuti munhu afanobata chinzvimbo;

(d) rekupa munhu chinzvimbo pachinenge chichine mumwe munhuwo ambomira basa akamirira kuzorega basa zvachose;

(e) rekudzika kana kusiyanisa zvinotarisirwa pakushanda kwemunhu iyeye pabasa iroro, kusanganisira mari inotambiriswa munhu uyu, nguva yaachange ari pabasa nezvimwe zvakanaka zvinopihwa munhu paanosiya basa; uye

(f) pachitevedzwa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, kumisa kana kudzinga munhu uyu basa.

(2) Kana vanhu vaviri kana kudarika vari pachinzvimbo chimwe chete nekuda kwekuti mumwe anenge apihwa chinzvimbo ichi panguva iyo muridzi wechinzvimbo ari pazororo akamirira kuzosiya chinzvimbo, munhu akapedzisira kupihwa chinzvimbo anofanira kutorwa sekuti ndiye ega muridzi wechinzvimbo ichi.

(3) Zvichienderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino, Mutemo weParamende unogona kuti padomwe mutevedzeri kana vatevedzeri vemunhu chero upi zvake ane chinzvimbo chiri pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino uye mutemo uyu unogona kuzivisa mabasa emutevedzeri kana vatevedzeri ava nezvavanofanira kubhadharwa.

(4) Kana pakadomwa mutevedzeri kumunhu ari pachinzvimbo kuburikidza neBumbiro reMitemo rino, mutevedzeri uyu anokwanisa kuita basa chero ripi zvaro rinotarisirwa pachinzvimbo ichi pese panenge muridzi wechinzvimbo asingakwanisi kuita basa nekuda kwechikonzero chero chipi zvacho.

(5) Zvichiendarana nemutemo chero upi zvawo uri muBumbiro reMitemo rino unogona kutapudza nguva kana mikana inokwanisa kuti munhu ange ari pachinzvimbo, munhu anenge asiya chinzvimbo chakadzikwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino anogona, ndokunge akakodzera, kudzorerwa pachinzvimbo ichi. 341 Kusiya mabasa

(1) Munhu chero upi zvake akadomwa kana kusarudzwa kuiswa pachinzvimbo chakadzikwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino anokwanisa kusiya chinzvimbo ichi kuburikidza neziviso yakanyorerwa kumunhu akamudoma kana kumusarudza, asi kana vari vanhu ava—

(a)

Mutungamiriri weNyika, ziviso inofanira kunyorerwa kuMutauriri;

(b) Mutungamiri weSeneti kana mutevedzeri wake, ziviso inofanira kunyorerwa kuMunyori weParamende kana kuziviswa kuSeneti;

(c)

Mutauriri kana mutevedzeri weMutauriri weDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, ziviso inofanira kunyorerwa kuMunyori weParamende kana kuziviswa kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika;

(d) Seneta, ziviso inofanira kunyorerwa kuMutungamiri weSeneti;

(e)

nhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, ziviso inofanira kunyorerwa kuMutauriri;

(f)

nhengo yekanzuru yedunhu guru, ziviso inofanira kunyorerwa kuna sachigaro wekanzuru;

(g) nhengo yekanzuru yeguta, ziviso inofanira kunyorerwa kuna meya weguta;

(h) nhengo yekanzuru yekumaruwa, ziviso inofanira kunyorerwa kumutungamiri wekanzuru iyi.

(2) Kusiya basa kwemunhu achibva pachinzvimbo chakadzikwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino kunotanga pazuva kana nguva iri paziviso yekusiya basa, kana kuti kana pasina zuva kana nguva yakaiswa, panotambirwa ziviso iyi nemunhu anyorerwa kana kuti nemumwewo munhu akabvumirwa neanga anyorerwa kuti aitambire. 342 Kuitwa kwemabasa nezvimwe zvakadaro

(1) Simba, mvumo kana kodzero zvinopihwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino zvinokwanisa kuitwa, uye basa rakapihwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino rinofanira kuitwa pazvinenge zvakakodzera kuti zviitwe.

(2) Mapoka ese akaumbwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino ane masimba ese akakodzera ekuti azadzise zvinangwa zvawo nekuita mabasa awo.

(3) Panenge pakapihwa simba, mvumo kana kodzero neBumbiro reMitemo rino, mamwe masimba kana dzimwe kodzero chero dzipi zvadzo dzakakodzera kana dzinobatsiridza basa iri, dzinotorwa sedzatopihwawo. 343 Kana munhu achinzi haasi pachinzvimbo mumabasa ehurumende Takatarisana nezveBumbiro reMitemo rino, vanhu havanganzi vari pazvinzvimbo zveHurumende nekuda chete kwekuti vanowana mari yemudyandigere, vanotambira chidimbu chemihoro yavo, vanotambira mari yekurega basa kanawo imwe mari ine chekuita nebasa ravakamboshanda muhurumende. 344 Uwandu hwenhengo hunodikanwa kuti musangano uitwe nekusava pamusangano wemapoka akaumbwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo

(1) Boka rakaumbwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana kuti riri pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino rinogona kushanda chero pasina dzimwe nhengo dzaro, ndokunge nhengo dzeboka iri dzinopa mvumo kana dzinoita kuti zvinofanira kuitwa zviitike dzine uwandu hunodikanwa.

(2) Kunze kwekunge Bumbiro reMitemo rino kana mutemo unozivisa maitirwo ebasa muboka iroro ukati pave nemamwe maitiro, uwandu hwechikamu chiri pakati nepakati hwenhengo dzese dzeboka chero ripi zvaro rakaumbwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo kana kuti neBumbiro reMitemo rino ndihwo uwandu hunodikanwa kuti basa riitwe.

(3) MuBumbiro reMitemo rino, pese panotaurwa nezvemavhoti anoti—

(a)

2013

uwandu hwechikamu chiri pakati nepakati hwenhengo dzeboka rine nhengo dzisingakwanisi kupatsanurwa kuiswa muzvipoka zviviri zvakaenzana;

(b) uwandu hwezvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu zvenhengo dzeboka rine nhengo dzisingakwanis kupatsanurwa kuiswa muzvipoka zvitatu zvakaenzana; kana kuti

(c)

uwandu hwezvikamu zvitatu kubva muzvina zvenhengo dzeboka rine nhengo dzisingakwanisi kupatsanurwa kuiswa muzvipoka zvina zvakaenzana;

zvinofanira kutorwa sekuti uwandu hwemavhoti haufaniri kuva hushoma kudarika nhamba yakazara inotevera chikamu chepakati nepakati, zvikamu zviviri kubva muzvitatu kana zvikamu zvitatu kubva muzvina zvichienderana nekuti uwandu hwenhengo dzeboka iri hwakamira sei.

(4) Pese panotaurwa nezvenhengo dzese dzeParamende panenge pachitaurwa nezveuwandu hwevanhu vese vanova nhengo dzeParamende panguva iyoyo. 345 Kupikisana kwemagwaro akasiyana eBumbiro reMitemo Kana pakaita kusapindirana pakati pezvakasiyana-siyana zvakanyorwa nezveBumbiro reMitemo rino, Bumbiro reMitemo rakanyorwa nechiRungu ndiro richashandiswa.

NHAMATIDZWA 1 (Chikamu 4) Mureza weNyika, Rumbo rweNyika, Mucherechedzo weNyika neChidhindo cheNyika GANHURO 1 Mureza weNyika

GANHURO 2 Rumbo rweNyika 1.

Simudzai mureza wedu weZimbabwe Yakazvarwa nemoto wechimurenga; Neropa zhinji ramagamba Tiidzivirire kumhandu dzose Ngaikomborerwe nyika yeZimbabwe.

2.

Tarisai Zimbabwe nyika yakashongedzwa Namakomo, nehova, zvinoyevedza Mvura ngainaye, minda ipe mbesa Vashandi vatuswe, ruzhinji rugutswe Ngaikomborerwe nyika yeZimbabwe.

3.

Mwari ropafadzai nyika yeZimbabwe Nyika yamadzitateguru edu tose; Kubva Zambezi kusvika Limpopo, Navatungamiri vave nenduramo; Ngaikomborerwe nyika yeZimbabwe.
