Rumbo rweNyika (Mumhanzi)

GANHURO 3 Mucherechedzo weNyika

GANHURO 4 Chidhindo cheNyika

NHAMATIDZWA 2 (Chikamu 87) Kutapudzwa kweKodzero paNguva yeDambudziko Rakaipisisa Munyika Dudziro yeNhamatidzwa 1 1. MuNhamatidzwa ino— “musungwa akachengetedzwa” zvinoreva munhu akaiswa muusungwa pasi pemutemo unoshandiswa panguva yedambudziko rakaipisisa munyika unobvumidza kusungwa kwevanhu nechinangwa chekudzivirira kuenderera mberi kwedambudziko; “mutemo wekugadzirisa dambudziko rakaipisisa” zvinoreva mutemo wakanyorwa unobvumidza kutorwa kwematanho ekugadzirisa zvinhu panguva yedambudziko rakaipisisa munyika; “kodzero kana rusununguko rwevanhu rwakakoshesesa” zvinoreva kodzero kana rusununguko zvakatsanangurwa muChikamu 2 cheChitsauko 4; “Dare rekuongorora” zvinoreva dare rakataurwa nezvaro mundima 3(1). Pangasvikwa mukuderedzwa kwekodzero kana rusununguko rwakakoshesesa rwevanhu 2. (1) Mutemo wekugadzirisa dambudziko rakaipisisa unogona kutapudza kodzero dzevanhu nemhando dzerusununguko dzakakoshesesa chero dzipi zvadzo asi kusvika chete pakataurwa muchikamu 87.

(2) Kana kukaziviswa kuti paita dambudziko rakaipisisa munyika kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 113 zvakanangana nenzvimbo imwe chete yenyika yeZimbabwe, mutemo wekugadzirisa dambudziko rakaipisisa haungaderedzi kodzero dzevanhu nerusununguko zvakakoshesesa kuchishandiswa zviri muNhamatidzwa ino mudzimwe nzvimbo dzeZimbabwe dzisina dambudziko. Dare revanoongorora nezvevasungwa 3. (1) Mutemo wekugadzirisa dambudziko rakaipisisa unobvumira kuiswa muusungwa kwevanhu nechinangwa chekudzivisa dambudziko unofanira kuita kuti kudzikwe dare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa.

(2) Dare revanoongorora nezvevasungwa rinofanira kudomwa neMutungamiriri weNyika achipihwa mazano neKomisheni inoona nezveUtongi uye abvunza veKomiti inoona nezveMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa.

(3) Dare revanoongorora nezvevasungwa rinofanira kuumbwa nevanhu ava—

(a) sachigaro, uyo anofanira kunge ari mutongi kana kuti akambova mutongi; uye

(b) pane dzimwe nhengo mbiri, imwe yadzo—

(i) ari kana kuti akambova mutongi kana kuti akakodzera kudomwa semutongi;

(ii) akange ari mejasitiriti muZimbabwe kwemakore anosvika kana kupfuura manomwe; kana kuti

(iii) ari munhu ava nemakore manomwe kana kupfuura akakodzera kushanda segweta muZimbabwe.

Kodzero dzakakoshesesa dzevasungwa

## Section 4

(1) Vasungwa vese—

(a)

vanofanira kuziviswa nekukasika kunogoneka, uye chero zvaita sei mukati memazuva manomwe, zvikonzero zvekusungwa kwavo;

(b) vanofanira kubvumidzwa nekukasika—

(i) vachizvibhadharira, kusarudza nekubvunza mazano kugweta ravanozvisarudzira pachavo uye pakavanzika; kana kuti

(ii) kana vachida, kubvunza mazano kugweta ravanenge vatsvagirwa neHurumende uye richibhadharwa neHurumende;

uye vanofanira kuziviswa nekukasika kunogoneka maererano nekodzero dzavo dziri mundima ino; uye

(c) vanofanira kubatwa zvakanaka uye zvine ruremekedzo rwechiremerera chemunhu.

(2) Panodiwa kuti musungwa apiwe ruzivo kuburikidza nezviri mundima ino—

(a)

ruzivo urwu runofanira kupihwa mumutauro unonzwisiswa nemusungwa; uye

(b) kana musungwa asingagoni kuverenga kana kunyora, gwaro chero ripi zvaro rine ruzivo urwu rinofanira kutsanangurwa nenzira inoita kuti anzwisise. Kuongororwa kwemhosva dzevasungwa

## Section 5

(1) Mhosva yemusungwa mumwe nemumwe inofanira kuendeswa kudare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa mukati memazuva gumi mushure mekusungwa kwake kekutanga uye dare iri rinofanira kuziviswa zita remusungwa, nzvimbo yaakachengeterwa nezvikonzero zvekusungwa kwake.

(2) Mhosva yemusungwa mumwe nemumwe inofanira kudzorerwa kudare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa mushure memazuva makumi matatu ega ega kubva pazuva rakapedzisira kuongororwa mhosva yake. Mhosva inogona kudzorerwa kudare iri mushure memazuva mashoma kupfuura makumi matatu kana zvirizvo zvanzi zviitwe nedare iri.

(3) Dare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa rinofanira kutarisisa mhosva dzese dzarinenge rapihwa nekukasika.

(4) Pakutongwa kwemhosva kwese kunoitwa nedare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa, vasungwa vane nyaya dziri kuongororwa vanofanira kubvumirwa kuva pakutongwa uku kuti vataure nyaya dzavo pachavo kana kuti, kana vachida—

(a)

kuburikidza nemagweta avanenge vapihwa neHurumende uye achibhadharwa neHurumende; kana kuti

(b) vachizvibhadharira, kuburikidza negweta ravanenge vazvisarudzira pachavo.

(5) Sezviri kutaurwa mundima diki (1) maererano nenguva yemamazuva gumi iyo inosanganisira nguva pfupi dziri pasi pemazuva gumi dzeusungwa asi dzikaverengwa pamwe chete dzichisvika pamazuva gumi, asi kana musungwa akasunungurwa mukati memazuva gumi mushure mekunge asungwa kekutanga uye akazoiswa muusungwazve mukati memazuva gumi abva mukusunungurwa.

Pfungwa dzedare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa

## Section 6

Mushure mekuongorora mhosva yemusungwa, dare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa rinogona kunyora pfungwa dzaro kubazi rehurumende rinenge rakati munhu asungwe richizivisa kuti musungwa uyu oramba ari muusungwa here kana kuti kwete. Bazi rehurumende iri rinofanira kuita zvinenge zvanzi zviitwe nedare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa. Vasungwa vanenge vasunungurwa havafaniri kusungirwa mhosva imwe chete zvakare

## Section 7

(1) Musungwa anenge asunungurwa kuburikidza neripoti inenge yaitwa nedare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa ichitaridza kuti hapana chikonzero chakasimba chekuti munhu ave muusungwa, haafaniri kuzosungwa zvakare nemhosva inoratidza kufanana kwakanyanya neyaakambosungirwa pekutanga.

(2) Takanangana nendima ino, munhu anonzi asungirwa mhosva yakafanana neyekutanga kunze kwekunge dare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa rapa ripoti yekuti, semaonero aro, pane zvikonzero zvitsva uye zvine musoro zvinoita kuti munhu asungwe zvakare. Kudzivirira musungwa kuti asakwidza mhosva kumatare

## Section 8

Nhamatidzwa ino haifaniri kunzwisiswa sekuti iri kudzivisa kodzero yemusungwa yekuenda kudare redzimhosva achipikisa kuva pamutemo kwekusungwa kwake, zvisinei nekuti nyaya yake iri pamberi pedare revanoongorora mhosva dzevasungwa here kana kuti kwete.

NHAMATIDZWA 3 (Zvikamu 94, 104, 114, 122, 123, 126, 127, 128, 185, 259, 272, 310 ne320) Mhiko neKubvumira Zvinofanira kucherechedzwa: 1. Munhu anofanira kuita mhiko chero ipi zvayo pane dzinotevera anogona, ndokunge achida, kungobvumira panzvimbo yekushandisa mashoko akapihwa pano. 2. Kana munhu ari kuita mhiko chero ipi zvayo pane dzinotevera, kana kuti ari kubvumira mashoko anotevera, akasiyanisa mashoko emhiko kana ekubvumira nenzira yekuti hazvina chazvinokanganisa zvakanyanya, kusiyanisa ikoko hakukanganisi kukosha kwemhiko kana kwekubvumira uku. Mhiko kana Kubvumira Kwekuvimbika Ini,.............................................. ndinopika [kana kubvuma nemwoyo wese] kuti ndichatendeka nekuzvipira kuZimbabwe nekutevedza mitemo yeZimbabwe. Saka ndibatsirei Mwari. [Apa panosiiwa pakubvumira] Mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku kunoitwa pamberi pemunhu akanzi ndiye anoiitisa pamutemo wakakodzera.

Mhiko kana Kubvumira Kwebasa Ini,............................................... ndinopika [kana kubvuma nemwoyo wese] kuti ndichashandira Zimbabwe zvakanaka nemuchokwadi muhofisi ye............................... Saka ndibatsirei Mwari. [Apa panosiiwa pakubvumira] Mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku kunoitwa pamberi pemunhu akanzi ndiye anoiitisa pamutemo wakakodzera.

Mhiko kana Kubvumira kweMutungamiriri weNyika nevatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika

Ini,.................................................... ndinopika [kana kubvuma nemwoyo wese] kuti seMutungamiriri weNyika [kana kuti mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika] yeZimbabwe, ndichatendeka kuZimbabwe uye ndichateerera, kukoshesa nekuchengetedza Bumbiro reMitemo nemimwe mitemo yeZimbabwe, uye— •

ndichakurudzira zvese zvinoita kuti Zimbabwe ibudirire, nekupikisa zvese zvingakanganise Zimbabwe;

•

ndichachengetedza nekukurudzira kodzero dzevanhu veZimbabwe;

•

ndichaita basa rangu nesimba rangu rese nekuziva kwangu nekugona kwangu kwese ndiri pakuvimbika kwehana yangu; uye

•

ndichazvipira kuti zvinhu zvinakire Zimbabwe nevanhu vayo.

Saka ndibatsirei Mwari. [Apa panosiiwa pakubvumira] Mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku kunoitwa pamberi peMukuru wevatongi kana kuti kana asipo, pamberi pemutongi anomutevedzera anenge aripo. Mhiko kana Kubvumira kweGurukota reHurumende Ini,.................................................... ndinopika [kana kubvuma nemwoyo wese] kuti ndichatendeka kuZimbabwe nemuhofisi yeGurukota, ndichakoshesa Bumbiro reMitemo nemimwe mitemo yese yeZimbabwe; uye kuti ndichabatsira Mutungamiriri weNyika yeZimbabwe nemazano ndakasununguka uye nemafungiro angu akanaka pese pandinonzi ndiite izvi, kuitira kuti basa reHurumende yeZimbabwe rifambe zvakanaka; kuti handizotauriri, zviri pachena kana nechibhende, zvakavanzika zvese zvinenge zvakurukurwa muDare reMakurukota eHurumende kana zvimwe zvakavanzika zvinenge zvaziviswa kwandiri pandinenge ndichiita basa rangu seGurukota; uye kuti pakuita zvinhu zvese, ndichaita basa rangu nekuvimbika uye nepese pandinogona. Saka ndibatsirei Mwari. [Apa panosiiwa pakubvumira] Mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku kunoitwa pamberi peMutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti, kana asipo, pamberi pemukuru wevatongi kana kuti kana asipo, pamberi pemutongi anomutevedzera anenge aripo. Mhiko kana Kubvumira kweMutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende Ini,.................................................... ndinopika [kana kubvuma nemwoyo wese] kuti ndichatendeka kuZimbabwe nemuhofisi yeMutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende, ndichakoshesa Bumbiro reMitemo nemimwe mitemo yese yeZimbabwe; uye kuti ndichabatsira Mutungamiriri weZimbabwe nemazano ndakasununguka uye

nemafungiro angu akanaka pese pandinonzi ndiite izvi, kuitira kuti basa reHurumende yeZimbabwe rifambe zvakanaka, kuti handizotauri zviri pachena kana nechibhende, zvakavanzika zvese zvinenge zvakurukurwa muDare reMakurukota eHurumende kana zvimwe zvakavanzika zvinenge zvaziviswa kwandiri pandinenge ndichiita basa rangu seMutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende; uye kuti pakuita zvinhu zvese, ndichaita basa rangu nekuvimbika uye nepese pandinogona. Saka ndibatsirei Mwari. [Apa panosiiwa pakubvumira] Mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku kunoitwa pamberi peMutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti, kana asipo, pamberi pemukuru wevatongi kana kuti kana asipo, pamberi pemutongi anomutevedzera anenge aripo. Mhiko kana Kubvumira kweNhengo yeParamende Ini,.................................................... ndinopika [kana kubvuma nemwoyo wese] kuti ndichatendeka kuZimbabwe, kuti ndichakoshesa Bumbiro reMitemo nemimwe mitemo yese yeZimbabwe, uye kuti ndichaita basa rangu saSeneta kana senhengo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika (zvichienderana nezvinenge zvakakodzera) nekuvimbika uye nepese pandinogona. Saka ndibatsirei Mwari. [Apa panosiiwa pakubvumira] Mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku kunoitwa pamberi peMunyori weParamende. Mhiko kana Kubvumira kweMutongi Ini,..................................................... ndinopika [kana kubvuma nemwoyo wese] kuti ndichatendeka kuZimbabwe uye kuti muhofisi ye..............................Ndichakoshesa nekuchengetedza Bumbiro reMitemo nemimwe mitemo yese yeZimbabwe, uye ndichatonga mhosva dzese zvakarurama kuvanhu vese pasina kutya, kufarira kana kuvenga uye sezvinoenderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo nemutemo. Saka ndibatsirei Mwari. [Apa panosiiwa pakubvumira] Kana munhu anenge achiita mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku ari mutongi, mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku kunofanira kuitwa pamberi peMukuru wevatongi kana kuti pamberi pemutongi anomutevedzera anenge aripo. Pamwe pese, mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku kunoitwa pamberi pemunhu akanzi ndiye anoitisa mhiko sekupihwa kwazvakaitwa pasi pemutemo wakakodzera. Mhiko kana Kubvumira kweNhengo yeKanzuru yeDunhu guru kana yeKanzuru yeGuta Ini,.................................................... ndinopika [kana kubvuma nemwoyo wese] kuti ndichatendeka kuZimbabwe uye, muhofisi yenhengo yekanzuru yedunhu guru [kana yeguta] re...................................................., [taurai zita redunhu] ndichakoshesa Bumbiro reMitemo nemimwe mitemo yese yeZimbabwe; kuti ndichapa mazano kuna Sachigaro [kana Meya] we.................................................... [taurai zita redunhu] ndakasununguka uye nemafungiro angu akanaka pese pandinonzi ndiite izvi, kuitira kuti basa redunhu rifambe zvakanaka; kuti handizotauri zviri pachena kana nechibhende, zvakavanzika chero zvipi zvazvo zvinenge zvakurukurwa mukanzuru yedunhu guru [kana yeguta] kana zvimwe zvakavanzika chero zvipi zvazvo zvinenge zvaziviswa kwandiri pandinenge ndichiita basa rangu senhengo yekanzuru iyoyo; uye kuti pakuita zvinhu zvese, ndichaita basa rangu nekuvimbika uye nepese pandinogona..

Saka ndibatsirei Mwari. [Apa panosiiwa pakubvumira] Mhiko iyi kana kubvumira uku kunoitwa pamberi pemunhu akanzi ndiye anoitisa mhiko sekupihwa kwazvakaitwa pasi pemutemo wakakodzera.

NHAMATIDZWA 4 (zvikamu 92, 121, 124 ne158) Zvinodikanwa kuti Munhu Ave Akakodzera Kuvhota Zvinodikanwa kuti munhu anyorese kuvhota 1. (1) Zvichienderana nezviri mundima diki (2) nemundima 2, munhu anokodzera kunyoresa kuvhota mugwaro revavhoti redunhu kana—

(a)

ane makore ekuberekwa gumi nemasere kana kupfuura; uye

(b) ari mugari wemuZimbabwe.

(2) Mutemo wezvesarudzo unogona kutara zvimwe zvinodiwa zvine chekuita nepanogara munhu kuti pave nechokwadi chekuti vanonyoresa kuvhota vanyoreswa mugwaro revavhoti rakanyanyokodzera, asi zvimwe zviwedzerwa zvinodiwa izvi zvinofanira kunge zvichiwirirana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino, zvikuru sei nezviri muchikamu 67. Zvinotadzisa munhu kunyoresa kuvhota 2. Munhu anorambidzwa kunyoresa semuvhoti—

(a)

kana akachengetedzwa nechikonzero chekuti murwere wepfungwa pasi peMutemo weParamende une chekuita neurwere hwepfungwa;

(b) kana akanzi nedare redzimhosva haagoni kuzviitira zvinhu zvake pachake, ndokunge kutonga kwedare uku kuchiri kukosheswa; kana kuti

(c)

kana akambobatwa nemhosva pasi pemutemo wezvesarudzo uye akanzi neDare redzimhosva repamusoro haabvumirwi kunyoresa semuvhoti kana kuti akanzi haabvumirwi kuvhota, kwenguva yaakanzi haabvumirwe, asi nguva yacho haifaniri kupfuura makore mashanu. NHAMATIDZWA 5 (Zvikamu 130 ne131)

Zvinotevedzwa pakuitwa kweMabhiri eMitemo neZvimwe Zvinhu muParamende KURONGWA KWENDIMA GANHURO 1 Kuziviswa kweMabhiri eMitemo, zveKukurukura neZvikumbiro Ndima 1. Tsananguro yeNhamatidzwa 5. 2. Dare rinovamba Mabhiri eMitemo. 3. Nhengo dzinokwanisa kuparura Mabhiri eMitemo nezvekukurukura. 4. Paramende haiiti nyaya dzine chekuita nezveMabhiri eMitemo yezveMari kana

kuvamba nyaya dzinofanira kukurukurwa kana zvikumbiro pamusoro pezvemari kunze kwekunge yanzi iite izvi neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende. GANHURO 2 Zvinofanira Kutevedzwa Maererano neMabhiri eMitemo 5. 6. 7.

Kufambiswa kweMabhiri emitemo kubva kune rimwe dare achienda kune rimwe. Kusawirirana pakati pematare. Mabhiri eMitemo yezvemari. GANHURO 3 Maripoti eKomiti inoona nezveMitemo yeParamende

8.

## Section 9 — Maripoti eKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende maererano nezveMabhiri

eMitemo. Maripoti eKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende maererano nenyaya dzezvemitemo. GANHURO 1 Kuziviswa kweMabhiri eMitemo, Zvekukurukura neZvikumbiro Tsananguro yeNhamatidzwa 5 1. MuNhamatidzwa ino— “Bhiri reMutemo wezvemari” zvinoreva Bhiri reMutemo rinobvumidza—

(a) kudzikwa, kuwedzerwa kana kuderedzwa kwemutero kuti zvinakire Nyika;

(b) kugoverwa kwemari, kana kudzika, kuwedzera kana kuderedza mari chero ipi zvayo inofanira kubhadharwa neHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende kana imwewo homwe yakachengetwa kana kuti iri mumaoko eHurumende;

(c) kuwedzera kana kudzorera chikwereti chemari yeNyika kana chave kudikanwa;

(d) kuregerera pakukundikana kwekuunganidza mari dzemutero weNyika; kana kuti

(e) kubvumira kushandiswa kwemari neHurumende zvanga zvisina kurongwa. Dare rinovamba Mabhiri eMitemo

2\. (1) Bhiri reMutemo chero ripi zvaro rinogona kuvambwa mudare reDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(2) Bhiri reMutemo chero ripi zvaro, kunze kweBhiri reMutemo wezvemari, rinogona kuvambwa mudare reSeneti. Nhengo dzinokwanisa kuparura Mabhiri eMitemo nezvekukurukura 3. Zvichienderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino neMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa muParamende—

(a)

Seneta, chero upi zvake, anogona kuvamba Bhiri reMutemo muSeneti kana kutura nyaya dzekukurukura kana kuunza chikumbiro kuSeneti;

(b) chero nhengo ipi zvayo yeDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika inogona kuvamba Bhiri reMutemo muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kana kutura nyaya dzinoda kukurukurwa kana kuunza chikumbiro kuDare;

(c)

Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende chero upi zvake anogona kuvamba Bhiri reMutemo chero ripi zvaro kana kutura nyaya dzinoda kukurukurwa kana kuunza chikumbiro muSeneti kana muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(d) nhengo chero ipi zvayo inenge yavamba Bhiri reMutemo mudare reParamende inogona kuti kana Bhiri reMutemo rainenge yavamba rapasiswa muDare rainenge iri nhengo, inogona kundovamba Bhiri reMutemo iri kune rimwe Dare. Paramende haioni nezvenyaya dzine chekuita nezveMabhiri eMitemo yezveMari kana kuvamba nyaya dzinofanira kukurukurwa kana zvikumbiro pamusoro pezvemari kunze kwekunge yanzi iite izvi neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende. 4. (1) Kunze kwekunge Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende ati zviitwe, hapana Dare pamatare maviri eParamende rinogona—

(a)

kupfuurira mberi neBhiri reMutemo chero ripi zvaro, kusanganisira kunatsurudza Bhiri reMutemo rinofungidzirwa neMutungamiriri weSeneti kana Mutauriri, zvichienderana nekuti idare ripi, kuti Bhiri reMutemo iri riri maererano nezvemari;

(b) kupfuurira mberi nekukurukura nyaya dzinenge dzaunzwa kuzokurukurwa nezvadzo muParamende, kusanganisira nyaya dzinoda kunatsurudzwa, dzinofungidzirwa neMutungamiriri weSeneti kana Mutauriri, zvichienderana nekuti idare ripi, kuti inyaya dzinofanira kunge dzine chekuita nezvinotevera—

(i) kudzikwa, kuwedzerwa kana kuderedzwa kwemutero kuti zvinakire Nyika;

(ii) kugoverwa kwemari, kana kudzika, kuwedzera kana kuderedza mari chero ipi zvayo inofanira kubhadharwa neHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende kana imwewo homwe yakachengetwa kana kuti iri mumaoko eHurumende;

(iii) kuwedzera kana kudzorera chikwereti chemari yeNyika kana chave kudikanwa;

(iv) kusiya kukundikana kuunganidza mari dzemutero weNyika kuchienderera mberi; kana kuti

(v) kurega kushandiswa kwemari neHurumende zvisina mvumo kuchienderera mberi. kana

(c)

kutambira chikumbiro chero chipi zvacho chinganzi, sekuona kweMutungamiri weSeneti kana Mutauriri, zvichienderana nekuti idare ripi, chinokumbira kuti kuitwe chinhu chero chipi zvacho chakatsangurwa mundima diki (b).

(2) Ndima diki (1) haishandi paBhiri reMutemo ravambwa, kana zvinoda kukurukurwa muParamende kana natsurudzo kana chikumbiro chinenge chasvitswa neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota reHurumende kana Mutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende.

GANHURO 2 Zvinofanira Kutevedzwa Maererano neMabhiri eMitemo Kufambiswa kweMabhiri emitemo kubva kune rimwe dare achienda kune rimwe 5. (1) Bhiri reMutemo rakatangira kune rimwe rematare eParamende uye rapasiswa kuDare iroro rinofanira kupfuudzwa kuenda kune rimwe Dare nekukasika, uye zuva rinenge raendeswa Bhiri iri kune rimwe Dare rinofanira kunyorwa mubhuku reDare rarabva.

(2) Bhiri reMutemo rinenge raunzwa kune rimwe Dare reParamende rinofanira kuvambwa muDare iri nekukasika, uye Dare iroro rinogona kuramba Bhiri iri kana kuripasisa risina kunatsurudzwa kana kuti ranatsurudzwa.

(3) Bhiri reMutemo rinenge raendeswa kune rimwe Dare reParamende sekutsanagurwa kwazvakaitwa mundima ino, rikapasiswa asi rine zvanatsurudzwa pariri, rinofanira kudzorerwa kuDare kwarakatangira nenatsurudzo dzinenge dzakatsigirwa neMunyori weParamende, uye Dare rinenge radzorerwa Bhiri reMutemo iri rinogona kuriramba kana kuripasisa kana kunatsurudza chero chipi zvacho chinenge chiri panatsurudzo dzinenge dzaitwa.

(4) Mushure mekunge Bhiri reMutemo radzorerwa kuDare kwarakatangira nenzira inoenderana nezviri mundima diki (3), natsurudzo chero ipi zvayo inenge yaitwa pariri nerimwe Dare ikarambwa kana kunatsurudzwa nevekuDare kwarakatangira, rimwe Dare iri rinogona kubvisa natsurudzo kana kubvumira kuti Bhiri iri rinatsurudzwe, kuburikidza nekutumira mashoko anotumirwa kudare kwakavambira Bhiri. Kusawirirana pakati pematare 6. (1) Zvichienderana nezviri mundima ino, kana—

(a)

Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvikange zvisina kuwirirana panatsurudzo inenge yaitwa paBhiri reMutemo rinenge rakavambwa muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika mukati memazuva makumi mapfumbamwe kubva pakuparurwa kweBhiri iri muSeneti;

(b) Seneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvikange zvisina kuwirirana panatsurudzo inenge yaitwa paBhiri reMutemo rinenge rakavambwa muSeneti mukati memazuva makumi mapfumbamwe kubva pakadzorerwa Bhiri kuSeneti; kana kuti

(c)

Bhiri reMutemo rakatangira kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika rikarambwa kana kuti rikasapasiswa neSeneti mukati memazuva makumi mapfumbamwe kubva pakavambwa Bhiri reMutemo iri muSeneti;

Bhiri reMutemo iri rinogona kuendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuti aritambire sezvarainge riri parakapasiswa neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, kunze kwetunhu tudiki tunenge tuchida kuzogadziriswa nekufamba kwenguva kana kuti natsurudzo chero ipi zvayo inenge yawiriranwa neSeneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(2) Kana, pamaonero eMutauriri, Bhiri reMutemo rinenge—

(a)

ratangira muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika; uye

(b) rakavambwa muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika mukati memazuva makumi mapfumbamwe zvichitevera kuvambwa kwarinenge rakamboitwa muSeneti umo marinenge rakabva; rine nyaya dzakafanana nedzakange dziri muBhiri reMutemo rekumashure, kunze kwetunhu tudiki tunenge tuchida kuzogadziriswa nekufamba kwenguva, zvinoreva kuti ndima diki (1) inoshandiswa paBhiri reMutemo uyu zvichiita sekuti nguva yemazuva makumi mapfumbabwe yakataurwa mundima diki (a) ne(c) dzendima diki iyoyo dzaitaura nguva yekusangana kwematare kwemazuva masere.

(3) Bhiri reMutemo rataurwa nezvaro mundima diki (1) kana (2) harigoni kuendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuti aritambire semutemo kunze kwekuti, mushure menguva yemazuva makumi mapfumbamwe kana mazuva masere akataurwa nezvawo mundima diki yakakodzera, Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika rabvumirana kuti Bhiri reMutemo iri rinofanira kuendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuti aritambire semutemo.

(4) Bhiri reMutemo rinenge raendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuti aritambire semutemo kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima ino, rinofanira kuendeswa pamwe chete nechitupa chinobva kuMutauriri chinotaridza kuti Bhiri iri ndiro rine chekuita nendima ino uye kuti zvinodikanwa mundima iyi zvazadzikiswa.

(5) Bhiri reMutemo rinoendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima ino rinotorwa sekuti rakapasiswa neParamende riri zvariri parinoendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika.

(6) Takanangana nendima ino—

(a)

Bhiri reMutemo rinotangira muDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika rinotorwa sekuti rakavambwa muSeneti pazuva rekusangana kweSeneti kekutanga zvichitevera kuendeswa kweBhiri iri kuSeneti, sekunyorwa kwazvakaitwa mugwaro reDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima 5(1);

(b) Bhiri reMutemo rinotangira muSeneti rinotorwa sekuti rakadzorerwa kuSeneti pazuva rekusangana kweSeneti kekutanga zvichitevera zuva rakadzorerwa Bhiri iri kuSeneti kekutanga kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima 5(3).

(7) Ndima iyi haishandiswi kuMabhiri eMitemo ezveBumbiro reMitemo kana kuti ezvemari. Mabhiri eMitemo yezvemari 7. (1) Seneti haina simba rekunatsurudza Bhiri reMutemo wezveMari asi inogona kukurudzira kuti Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika riite natsurudzo kuBhiri iri.

(2) Munyori weParamende anofanira kutsinhira nadzurudzo dzese dzinoitwa dzinenge dzakurudzirwa neSeneti kuti dziitwe paBhiri reMutemo wezveMari uye anofanira kuendesa nadzurudzo yakatsinhirwa kuDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika.

(3) Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika rinofanira kuongorora nadzurudzo dzese dzinenge dzaendeswa kwariri pasi pezviri mundima ino uye rinogona kuisa nadzurudzo idzi muBhiri reMutemo wezveMari rinenge richitaurwa nezvaro.

(4) Kana Seneti ikasapasisa Bhiri reMutemo wezveMari mukati memazuva masere ekusangana anoverengwa kubva pazuva rakaparurwa Bhiri reMutemo muSeneti, Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika rinogona kubvumirana kuti Bhiri reMutemo iri rinofanira kuendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika, uye Bhiri reMutemo iri rinogona kuzoendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika riri sezvarakange rakaita parakapasiswa neDare reVanodzika Mitemo muNyika.

(5) Bhiri reMutemo wezveMari rinenge raendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuchitevedzwa chibvumirano chiri mundima diki (4)—

(a)

rinotorwa sekuti rapasiswa neParamende riri sezvarakange riri parakapasiswa neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika; uye

(b) parinoendeswa kuMutungamiriri weNyika kuti aise siginecha yake, rinofanira kuendeswa pamwe chete nechitupa chinobva kuMutauriri chichitaridza kuti Bhiri reMutemo iri rakapasiswa kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima ino. CHIKAMU 3 Maripoti eKomiti Inoona nezveMitemo yeParamende Maripoti eKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende maererano nezveMabhiri eMitemo 8. (1) Zvichienderana nezviri mundima ino, hapana Dare rinogona kupedza hurukuro yeBhiri reMutemo kunze kwekunge ripoti yeKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende yaturwa kuDare.

(2) Ndima diki (1) haishandi pa—

(a)

Bhiri reMutemo wezveBumbiro reMitemo; kana kuti

(b) Bhiri reMutemo chero upi zvawo unenge waturirwa ripoti neKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende, kunze kwekunge Bhiri reMutemo iri rakazonatsurudzwa kubva pakaturwa ripoti iyi.

(3) Kana Komiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende ichinge isina kutura ripoti yeBhiri reMutemo mukati menguva yakatarwa pasi peMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa kana mukati menguva chero ipi zvayo inenge yawedzerwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muMitemo inofanira kutevedzwa, zvinofanira kutorwa sekuti Komiti iyi iri kuti hapana chiri muBhiri reMutemo iri chekuti kana rikadzikwa semutemo, chingakanganisa Bumbiro reMitemo rino, uye Dare rinenge richiongorora Bhiri reMutemo iri rinogona kupfuurira mberi sezvinonzi Komiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende yakatotura ripoti yayo.

(4) Zvichienderana nezviri mundima diki (5) ne(6), kana Komiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende yapa ripoti yekuti pane zviri muBhiri reMutemo zvekuti kana rikadzikwa semutemo zvinenge zvichipikisana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino, Dare rakakodzera rinofanira kuongorora ripoti iyi uye kana Dare rabvumirana kuti

zviri muBhiri reMutemo zvinopikisana neBumbiro reMitemo rino, Dare iri harifaniri kupasisa Bhiri reMutemo iri.

(5) Kana Dare rechikamu cheParamende richiongorora ripoti yeKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende yataurwa nezvayo mundima diki (4) maererano neBhiri reMutemo rakavambwa neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, Gurukota reHurumende kana mutevedezri weGurukota reHurumende, munhu akavamba Bhiri reMutemo iri anofanira kupihwa mukana wakakwana wekupa pfungwa dzake maererano neripoti iyi.

(6) Kana Dare rechikamu cheParamende rikabvumirana, kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima diki (4), kuti pane chiri muBhiri reMutemo chekuti kana rikazodzikwa semutemo chinopikisana neBumbiro reMitemo rino, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana Gurukota reHurumende vanogona kunyorera kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo mukati memazuva gumi nemana zvichitevera kupasiswa kwechibvumirano chekuti kana Bhiri reMutemo iri rikadzikwa semutemo, rinenge richizowirirana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino, uye kana Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rikatsinhira zvinenge zvataurwa izvi, Dare rakakodzera rinogona kupfuurira mberi nekuongorora uye kupasisa Bhiri iri. Maripoti eKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende maererano nenyaya dzezvemitemo 9. (1) Seneti kana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvisati zvaongorora ripoti yeKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende yekuti pane zviri mumutemo zvinopikisana neBumbiro reMitemo rino kana kuti neMutemo wekuumbwa kweBumbiro reMitemo rino here, Komiti iyi inogona kusakoshesa ripoti iyi kana ichinge yagutsikana kuti zvange zviri muBhiri reMutemo zvinopikisina neBumbiro reMitemo rino zvabviswa kana kuti zvanatsurudzwa nenzira inopedza kupikisana.

(2) Kana, mushure mekuongorora ripoti yeKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende maererano nezviri mumutemo zvinopikisana neBumbiro reMitemo rino, Seneti kana Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvikabvumirana kuti pane zvinopikisana neBumbiro reMitemo rino, Munyori weParamende anofanira kuzivisa nezvechibvumirano ichi kune vakadzika mutemo uyu, uye vanhu ava vanofanira kuti, mukati memazuva makumi maviri nerimwe mushure mekuziviswa—

(a)

vanyorere kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kuti zvinzi mutemo uyu unopindirana neBumbiro reMitemo rino; kana kuti

(b) vachiparadza mutemo uyu.

(3) Kana vanoona nezvekudzikwa kwemutemo vanyorera kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kuti pashandiswe zviri mundima diki (2)(a), mutemo uyu unombosendekwa padivi kuchimirirwa pfungwa dzeDare iri.

(4) Kana, mushure mekuongorora ripoti yeKomiti inoona nezvemitemo yeParamende yekuti zviri mumutemo zvinopikisana neMutemo weParamende, Seneti kana kuti Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika zvabvumirana kuti mutemo uyu unopikisana neMutemo weParamende—

(a)

mutemo uyu unobva waraswa; uye

(b) Munyori weParamende anofanira kuburitsa chiziviso muGazette nekukasika, achizivisa ruzhinji rwevanhu nezvechibvumirano chakaitwa nezvachinoreva.

Kupinda kweHurumende itsva. Kupfuurira mberi kwemitemo yagara iripo. Manzwisisirwo emitemo yagara iripo. Mitemo yeZvinofanira kutevedzwa muParamende. Vashandi vagara vari pabasa. Mutemo unogadzirirwa sarudzo neurefu hwenguva yekuti Mutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga ange ari pabasa pamwe chete nekudomwa kweVatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika. Kupfuurira mberi kwemamwe mabasa eutungamiriri. Muchengetedzi weruzhinji rwevanhu. Kubviswa kwemari iri muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende yekare.

## Section 20

Matare edzimhosva nezvinofanira kutevedzwa mukutongwa kwemhosva. Mitemo ine chekuita neMukuru weChikamu chinoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva. Kodzero dzine chekuita nemudyandigere. GANHURO 1 Zvemavambo Dudziro muNhamatidzwa yeChitanhatu

1\. MuNhamatidzwa ino, kunze kwekunge zvisingapindirani nezviri kutaurwa nezvazvo— “zuva riri kutaurwa nezvaro” zvinoreva zuva rinotanga kushanda Bumbiro reMitemo rino sekududzirwa kwazvakaitwa mundima 3(2); “mutemo wagara uripo” zvinoreva mutemo wakanyorwa wakange uchishanda muZimbabwe panguva iri pashure pezuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino, zvisinei nekuti Mutemo weParamende kana kuti mumwewo mutemo; “mutemo uripo” zvinoreva mutemo wagara uripo kana mumwewo mutemo, zvisinei nemamiriro awo, wakange uri muZimbabwe panguva iri pashure pezuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino; “sarudzo dzekutanga” zvinoreva—

(a) sarudzo dzekutanga dzekusarudza Mutungamiriri weNyika pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino;

(b) sarudzo huru dzekutanga dzekusarudza nhengo dzeParamende kuchitevedzwa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino; uye

(c) sarudzo dzekutanga dzekusarudza nhengo dzemapoka anotungamirira makanzuru ematunhu makuru neemaguta nemakanzuru ekumaruwa nenharaunda;

dzinoitwa mushure mezuva rakaziviswa;

“Bumbiro reMitemo rekare” zvinoreva Bumbiro reMitemo reZimbabwe rakatanga kushanda musi wa18 Kubvumbi, 1980 rikazonatsurudzwa nekufamba kwenguva; “zuva rekuburitswa” zvinoreva zuva rekudzikwa kweBumbiro reMitemo rino kana mutemo warakadzikwa nawo muGazette kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 51 (5) cheBumbiro reMitemo rekare. Zvinoreva Nhamatidzwa yeChitanhatu 2. Nhamatidzwa ino ine simba, nyangwe pane zvinopikisana chero zvipi zvazvo, pamusoro pemimwe mitemo yese yeBumbiro reMitemo rino. GANHURO 2 Kutanga Kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino neKuraswa kweBumbiro reMitemo reKare Kutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino 3. (1) Nhamatidzwa ino, pamwe chete ne—

(a)

2013

Chitsauko 3, chiri maererano neUgari;

(b) Chitsauko 4, cheKuziviswa kweKodzero;

(c)

Chitsauko 5, chiri maererano nezvesarudzo nekutanga basa kweMutungamiriri weNyika;

(d) Chitsauko 6, chiri maererano nesarudzo yenhengo dzeDare reParamende pamwe chete nekudaidzwa kweParamande mushure mesarudzo huru dzenyika uye kutambirwa kweMitemo yeParamende neMutungamiriri weNyika;

(e)

Chitsauko 7, chiri maererano nesarudzo; kunze kweChikamu 158, 160 ne161

(f) Chitsauko 8, chiri maererano nemashandiro nemasimba eDare reBumbiro reMitemo

(g) Chitsauko 9, chiri maererano nezvinotevedzwa pakutungamirira ruzhinji rwevanhu neutungamiriri;

(h) chikamu 208, chiri maererano nemashandiro evari mumabasa ekuchengetedzwa kwenyika;

(i)

Chitsauko 12, maererano nepane chekuita nezveKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe; uye

(j)

Chitsauko 14, chiri maererano nehurumende dzematunhu makuru nemakanzuru;

zvinotanga kushanda pazuva rekuburitswa kweBumbiro reMitemo rino muGazette.

(2) Kunze kwezvimwe zvinenge zvakaziviswa muNhamatidzwa ino, zvimwe zvese zviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino zvinotanga kushanda pazuva rinotanga basa Mutungamiriri weNyika anenge asarudzwa musarudzo yekutanga.

(3) Pakati pezuva rekuburitswa muGazette nezuva rinotanga kushanda Bumbiro reMitemo rino, mitemo iri muBumbiro reMitemo rino yakatsanangurwa mundima diki (a) kusvika (i) dzendima diki (1) ine simba kupfuura mitemo yakafanana neyemuBumbiro reMitemo rekare. Kuraswa kweBumbiro reMitemo rekare 3. Kuchitevedzwa zviri muNhamatidzwa ino, Bumbiro reMitemo rekare rinorega kushanda kubva zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino.

GANHURO 3 Sarudzo dzeKutanga Kuchengetedzwa kwemiganhu yematunhu makuru nemiganhu yesarudzo yagara iripo 5. Miganhu yematunhu makuru, yematunhu esarudzo dzenhengo dzeParamende neyemawadhi inoramba iri sezvayakange iri zuva rekuziviswa kweBumbiro reMitemo rino muGazette risati rasvika kana takatarisana nesarudzo dzekutanga. Kunyoreswa kwevavhoti 6. (1) Munhu chero upi zvake akange akanyoreswa semuvhoti mugwaro revavhoti zuva rekuziviswa kweBumbiro reMitemo rino muGazette risati rasvika ane kodzero yekuramba ari mugwaro revavhoti kuitira kuti agone kuvhota musarudzo dzekutanga.

(2) Takatarisana nesarudzo dzekutanga, Mutungamiriri wevanoita basa rekunyoresa vavhoti, achitungamirirwa neKomisheni inoona nezvesarudzo muZimbabwe, ndiye achanyoresa vavhoti pamwe chete nekugadzira magwaro evavhoti.

(3) Vane basa rekunyoresa vavhoti pasi peMutemo weSarudzo vanofanira kuita chirongwa chekunyoresa vavhoti zvakasimba pamwe chete nekuongorora gwaro revavhoti kwemazuva anosvika kana kupfuura makumi matanhatu kubva zuva rekuziviswa kweBumbiro reMitemo rino muGazette. Kupikiswa kwesarudzo dzeMutungamiriri weNyika dzekutanga 7. Kupikiswa kwezvinenge zvabuda pasarudzo dzeMutungamiriri weNyika dzekutanga chero kupi zvako kunofanira kunzwikwa uye kutongwa pachishandiswa chikamu 93 cheBumbiro reMitemo rino. Mutemo wezveSarudzo 8. Sarudzo dzekutanga dzinofanira kuitwa kuchitevedzwa Mutemo wezveSarudzo unoenderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino.

GANHURO 4 Zvinochengetedzwa neMitemo Ichashandiswa Pakubva Mune zveKare neKupinda Muzvitsva Kupinda kweHurumende itsva 9. Hurumende inoumbwa kuchitevedzwa zviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino ndiyo ichatsiva Hurumende yeZimbabwe yakange iripo pane zvese. Kupfuurira mberi kwemitemo yagara iripo 10. Kuchitevedzwa zviri muNhamatidzwa ino, mitemo yese yagara iripo inoramba ichishanda asi inofanira kunzwisiswa nenzira inoenderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino.

Manzwisisirwo emitemo yagara iripo 11. (1) Kunze kwekunge zvichipikisana nezvinenge zvichiitika, kutaura chero kupi zvako kwemutemo wagara uripo kune chekuita ne—

(a)

Mutungamiriri weNyika kunofanira kunzwisiswa sekunoreva Mutungamiri weNyika anoshanda zvinoenderana nezviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino;

(b) Paramende inofanira kunzwisiswa sekuti pari kutaurwa—

(i) Seneti, kana zviri kutaurwa nezvazvo zviri maererano nebasa rinoitwa neSeneti yega pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino;

(ii) Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika, kana zviri kutaurwa nezvazvo zviri maererano nerimwe basa chero ripi zvaro kunze kwerataurwa nezvaro mundima diki (i) kana kudzikwa kwemutemo;

(c)

Dare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika rinofanira kunzwisiswa seBoka reNhengo dzeParamende Rinodzika Mitemo yeNyika;

(d) vashandi veHurumende zvinofanira kunzwisiswa sevashandi veHurumende;

(e)

Komisheni inoona nezvevashandi veHurumende inofanira kunzwisiswa seKomisheni inoona nezvevashandi vehurumende;

(f)

Vari mumabasa ezvemajeri vanofanira kunzwisiswa sevanoona nezvemajeri nekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa;

(g) Komisheni inoona nezvemajeri inofanira kunzwisiswa seKomisheni inoona nezvekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa;

(h) Mukuru mukuru wezvemajeri anofanira kunzwisiswa seMukuru mukuru anoona nezvekudzorerwa mugwara kwevasungwa;

(i)

Mukuru mukuru anoona nezvekushandiswa zvakanaka kwemari yeHurumende nekuongororwa kwayo anofanira kunzwisiswa semukuru mukuru anoongorora nezvekushandiswa kwemari yeHurumende;

(j)

Panyaya dzine chekuita nekuparwa kwemhosva, Gweta Guru reHurumende rinofanira kunzwisiswa seMukuru mukuru anoona nezvekutongwa kwemhosva.

(2) Panenge Bumbiro reMitemo rino rapa masimba ekudzika mutemo panyaya chero ipi zvayo kumunhu kana boka revanhu, uye nyaya iyi yakataurwa nezvayo mumutemo wagara uripo wakadzikwa nemumwe munhu kana boka revanhu, mutemo wagara uripo uyu unoshanda sekunge wakagadzirwa nemunhu kana boka revanhu rine simba rekuudzika pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino. Mitemo inofanira kutevedzwa 12. Mitemo inotevedzwa neParamende yakanga ichishanda zuva rakatanga kushanda Bumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika icharamba ichishanda semitemo inotevedzwa neSeneti neDare reVanodzika Mitemo yeNyika kusvika yatsiviwa kana kunatsurudzwa zvichienderana neBumbiro reMitemo rino. Vashandi vagara vari pabasa 13. Munhu chero upi zvake akange ari pachinzvimbo kana kuti akange akafanobata chinzvimbo muHurumende pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rekare, zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, anoramba ari kana kuti akafanobata chinzvimbo ichocho kana chimwe chakafanana neichocho pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino, pasina chinoshanduka pamashandiro ake kusvikira nguva yekuti arege basa sezvakange zviri muchibvumirano chekare kana kuti kusvika asiya basa kana kuti akasiya basa zvichitevera kukwana kwemakore ekuberekwa ekuti munhu anofanira kusiya basa kana kuti achinge abviswa pabasa kuchitevedzwa zviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino kana zvaitarisirwa pamashandiro ake ekare. Mutemo unogadzirirwa sarudzo neurefu hwenguva yekuti Mutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga ange ari pabasa pamwe chete nekudomwa kwevatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika 14. (1) Zvisinei nezviri muchikamu 92, pasarudzo dzekutanga nesarudzo dzeMutungamiriri weNyika chero dzipi zvadzo dzinoitwa mukati memakore gumi mushure mesarudzo yekutanga, vanhu vachange vachikwikwidza musarudzo dzekuva Mutungamiriri weNyika havasarudzi vanhu vachava vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika sekutsanangurwa kwazvakaitwa muchikamu ichi.

(2) Pasina kupedza nguva, munhu anenge asarudzwa seMutungamiriri weNyika musarudzo chero ipi zvayo yakataurwa nezvayo mundima diki (1) anofanira kusarudza vatevedzeri vake vasingadariki vaviri avo vanoshanda kwenguva yaanoda iye.

(3) Kana—

(a) mumwe Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika akadomwa kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima diki (2), munhu iyeye ndiye Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika wekutanga kana kuchitariswa zviri muBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(b) vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vaviri vakadomwa kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima diki (2), Mutungamiriri weNyika anogona kusarudza mumwe wavo kuti afanobata chinzvimbo cheMutungamiriri weNyika kana iye asiri munyika kana kuti kana asiri kukwanisa kuita basa rake nekuda kweurwere kana chimwewo chikonzero.

(4) Zvisinei nezviri muchikamu 101 asi kuchikosheswa zviri mundima diki

(5) ne(6), kana munhu anenge asarudzwa kuva Mutungamiriri weNyika musarudzo chero ipi zvayo yakataurwa nezvayo mundima diki (1) akashaya, akasiya basa kana kuti akabviswa pachinzvimbo—

(a) Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika kana kuti kana pane Vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vaviri, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika akapedzisira kusarudzwa kuti afanobata chinzvimbo sezvakatsanangurwa mundima diki (3)(b) ndiye achafanobata chigaro kusvikira Mutungamiriri weNyika mutsva atanga basa sekupihwa kwazvakaitwa mundima diki

(5); uye

(b) chinzvimbo cheMutungamiriri weNyika chinenge chisisina munhu chinofanira kupindwa nemunhu anosarudzwa nebato rezvematongerwo enyika rakanga richimiririrwa neMutungamiriri weNyika akange asarudzwa.

(5) Bato rezvematongerwo enyika rinobvumidzwa kusarudza munhu kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima diki (4)(b) rinofanira kuzivisa Mutauriri zita remunhu warinenge rasarudza mukati memazuva makumi mapfumbamwe mushure mekunge chinzvimbo cheMutungamiriri weNyika chasara chisina munhu, uye kana izvi zvaitika, munhu asarudzwa uyu anotanga basa seMutungamiriri weNyika mushure mekuita mhiko yeMutungamiriri weNyika kuchitevedzwa zviri muchikamu 94, uye anenge asarudzwa uyu anofanira kuita mhiko iyi mukati memaawa makumi mana nemasere zvichitevera kuziviswa kweMutauriri nezvezita rake.

(6) Kana Mutungamiriri weNyika asarudzwa musarudzo dzinotaurwa nezvadzo mundima diki (1) akashaya, akasiya basa kana kuti akabviswa pachinzvimbo asi iye anga asina bato rezvematongerwo enyika raanomirira, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika, kana kuti kana pane vatevedzeri veMutungamiriri weNyika vaviri, Mutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika akapedzisira kusarudzwa kuti afanobata chigaro kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima diki (3)(b) ndiye achava Mutungamiriri weNyika. Kupfuurira mberi kwemamwe mabasa eUtungamiriri 15. Zvisinei nemutemo chero upi zvawo wemuBumbiro reMitemo rekare, zvinzvimbo zvinotevera zvakange zviripo pazuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo kuchitevedzwa Nhamatidzwa 8 yeBumbiro reMitemo rekare, zvinoti che—

(a)

Mutungamiriri weNyika neMutevedzeri weMutungamiriri weNyika;

(b) Mutungamiri weHurumende neMutevedzeri weMutungamiri weHurumende; uye

(c)

2013

Gurukota reHurumende neMutevedzeri weGurukota reHurumende;

zvicharamba zviripo kusvikira zuva rekuti Mutungamiri weNyika wekutanga atange basa pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino, uye vanhu vaishanda vari pazvinzvimbo izvi vacharamba varipo sezvakakodzera. Muchengetedzi weruzhinji rwevanhu 16. (1) Mutemo weMuchengetedzi weruzhinji rwevanhu [Chitsauko 10:18] unoparadzwa.

(2) Nyaya chero ipi zvayo yakange ichiongoorwa neMuchengeti weruzhinji rwevanhu zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika inofanira kuendeswa kuKomisheni inoona nezvekodzero dzevanhu muZimbabwe kuti ipedziswe. Kubviswa kwemari iri muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende yekare 17. Mari yainge iri muHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende yakaumbwa neBumbiro reMiremo rekare, zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, ichava mari yeHomwe yeMuunganidzwa weMari yeHurumende yakaumbwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino. Matare edzimhosva nezvinofanira kutevedzwa mukutongwa kwemhosva 18. (1) Mundima ino— “nyaya ine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo isina kupera kutongwa” zvinoreva—

(a)

kupikisa, kunyorera kana kutaura kune chekuita nekutyorwa kwekodzero kunofungidzirwa kuti kwakaitwa muBumbiro reMitemo rekare; kana kuti

(b) mhosva chero ipi zvayo ine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo, sezvakatsanangurwa muchikamu 332 cheBumbiro reMitemo rino; uye yange ichiri kutongwa muDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro rakadzikwa neBumbiro reMitemo rekare pazuva riri pashure pekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(2) Zvisinei nezviri muchikamu 166, kwemakore manomwe mushure mekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino, Dare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo richange rine vanhu ava—

(a)

Mutongi Mukuru neMutevedzeri weMutongi Mukuru; uye

(b) vamwe vatongi veDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro vanomwe avo vanofanira kushanda pamwe chete vachitonga nyaya ine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo chero ipi zvayo.

(3) Kana pakaita chinzvimbo chisina munhu muDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo mumakore manomwe ekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino, chinzvimbo ichi chinofanira kutorwa nemumwe mutongi kana mumwe mutongi anowedzerwa kudare iri kana kuti nemutongi anofanobata chinzvimbo anobva kuDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro, zvichienderana nemamiriro ezvinhu: Kana pakamuka chinzvimbo muhofisi yeMutevedzeri weMukuru mukuru wevatongi panguva iyoyo, chinzvimbo ichi chinofanira kutsiviwa maringe nezvinotaurwa pachikamu 180(2) ne(3). [Chikamu chakagadziriswa neChikamu 8 cheMutemo 10 wa2017]

(4) Kusvikira padzikwa mutemo wakasiyana kuburikidza neMutemo weParamende—

(a)

mitemo inogona kudzikwa muDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro (Chitsauko 7:13 cheMutemo weDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro) kuti itungamirire mashandirwo ebasa muDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo.

(b) mitemo yeDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro inoshanda, ine shanduko dzakakodzera chero dzipi zvadzo, muDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo maererano nenyaya chero ipi zvayo isina kutaurwa nezvayo mumitemo yakatsanangurwa mundima diki (a); asi mitemo yakadai chero ipi zvayo ine chekuita nemashandiro eDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo inofanira kuenderana nezviri muchikamu 85 neChitsauko 8.

(5) Matare anoti Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro reZimbabwe, Dare redzimhosva repamusoro reZimbabwe, Dare redzimhosva rezvevashandi nevashandirwi neDare redzimhosva rezveUtungamiriri, sekuumbwa kwaakange akaitwa zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, achaumbwa agozivikanwa seDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro, Dare redzimhosva repamusoro, Dare redzimhosva rezvevashandi nevashandirwi uye Dare redzimhosva rezveUtungamiriri pasi peBumbiro reMitemo rino, uye mitongo yakapihwa nematare aya zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika icharamba ichikosheswa.

(6) Munhu wese akange, zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, ari mutongi weDare redzimhosva rezvevashandi nevashandirwi kana kuti weDare redzimhosva rezveUtungamiriri, achava mutongi weDare redzimhosva rezvevashandi nevashandirwi kana kuti weDare redzimhosva rezveUtungamiriri, zvichienderana nekuti zvakamira sei, uye ane mashandiro nemihoro yakafanana neyevatongi veDare redzimhosva repamusoro panguva iyoyo, urefu hwenguva yaachava pachinzvimbo seMutungamiriri weDare redzimhosva rezvevashandi nevashandirwi kana kuti weDare redzimhosva rezveUtungamiriri achinzi ari kushanda semutongi weDare redzimhosva repamusoro.

(7) Matare emamejasitiriti, matare emadzishe nemamwe matare akaumbwa neMutemo weParamende zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika acharamba achishanda pazuva iri uye kana zuva iri rapfuura sezvinonzi akaumbwa neMutemo wakataurwa nezvawo muchikamu 174, uye mitongo chero ipi zvayo yakapihwa nematare aya zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika icharamba ichikosheswa.

(8) Nyaya ine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo inenge isina kupera kutongwa chero ipi zvayo—

(a)

umo gakava pakati pevanenge vachikwikwidzana rinenge risati ratanga kunzwikwa zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika inofanira kuendeswa kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima diki (2);

(b) umo gakava pakati pevanenge vachikwikwidzana rinenge ratanga kunzwikwa pakasvika zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino inofanira kupedziswa neDare redzimhosva repamusorosoro kunze kwekunge mativi ese anenge achikwikwidzana akawirirana kuti iendeswe kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo rakaumbwa kuchitevedzwa zviri mundima diki (2), uye kana zvadaro Dare redzimhosva repamusorosoro rinofanira kuendesa nyaya iyi kuDare redzimhosva rinoona nezveBumbiro reMitemo.

(9) Nyaya dzese, kunze kwenyaya dzine chekuita neBumbiro reMitemo dzinenge dzisati dzapera kutongwa mudare redzimhosva chero ripi zvaro, zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, dzinogona kupfuurira mberi nekutongwa mudare redzimhosva iroro kana rimwe dare redzimhosva rakaenzana neiri rakaumbwa neBumbiro reMitemo rino, zvichienderana nekuti zvinhu zvakamira sei, zvichiita sekuti Bumbiro reMitemo rino rakange richishanda pakavamba kutongwa kwenyaya idzi, asi—

(a)

zvinofanira kutevedzwa mukutongwa kwenyaya idzi ndezvakange zvichifanira kutevedzwa pakutongwa kwadzo zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika; uye

(b) zvinofanira kutevedzwa zvataurwa mundima diki (a) zvinoshanda kunyaya idzi kunyangwe zvichipikisana nemutemo chero upi zvawo uri muChitsauko 4 cheBumbiro reMitemo rino.

(10) Takatarisana nendima diki (9)—

(a)

mhosva huru inonzi yatanga kutongwa kana munhu ari kupomerwa mhosva iyi achinge ataura pamusoro pemhosva iyi;

(b) mhosva iri maererano nekugarisana kwevanhu inonzi yatanga kutongwa kana masamanisi akange atumirwa kana kuti mhosva yapirwa kudare, zvichienderana nekuti ndezvipi zviripo. Mitemo ine chekuita neChikamu chinoona nezvekureferetwa nekutongwa kwemhosva 19. (1) Mutongo chero upi zvawo unenge wapihwa kana zvinenge zvaitwa neGweta Guru reHurumende kana kuti nemunhu anenge akamirira Gweta iri munyaya dzemhosva huru, zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, dzichanzi dzatorwa neMukuru weVanoona nezvekuferefetwa nekutongwa kwemhosva musi uyu kana kuti zuva iri rapfuura.

(2) Munhu akange ari pachinzvimbo cheGweta Guru reHurumende zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika, achapfuurira mberi ari pachinzvimbo cheMukuru wevanoona nezvekuferefetwa nekutongwa kwemhosva musi uyu uye kana zuva iri rapfuura. Kodzero dzine chekuita nemudyandigere 20. Kodzero yakagara iripo maererano nemari yemudyandigere zuva rekutanga kushanda kweBumbiro reMitemo rino risati rasvika uye yakange yakachengetedzwa neBumbiro reMitemo rekare icharamba iripo uye ine ruchengetedzo rwakafanana muBumbiro reMitemo rino.
